# Mac.apk flags reference

Every environment variable and command-line flag the shipped Mac.apk reads, with its default,
its accepted values and what it does. The handful a user is likely to want are summarised in
the README's "Advanced: flags and settings" section and repeated in the first table below.

The audience column classifies each row as `user`, `dev`, `diag` (diagnostic output), `bisect`
(a gate that restores an older behaviour for an A/B) or `experiment`. Flags marked `bisect` or
`experiment` are not supported configurations; they exist so a regression can be attributed,
and a future release may remove them without notice. Switches that only matter when building
Mac.apk from source, the maintainers' test tooling, and the diagnostics that exist only for the
maintainers' investigation of one specific app are left out of this copy.

---

# Every environment variable and command-line flag the shipped Mac.apk reads, grouped by subsystem, with default, accepted values, effect and audience for each.

---

## How to set a flag

- **Running the runtime directly:** environment variables go before the command: `MACAPK_LOG_LEVEL=7 /Applications/Mac.apk.app/Contents/Resources/macapk-runtime --apk path/to/app.apk`. CLI flags (`--orientation`, `--android-root`, ...) go on the command line as shown in the CLI table below.
- **An installed app:** `MACAPK_X=1 /Applications/<App>.app/Contents/MacOS/<AppStub>` from a Terminal -- the per-app stub `execv`s the staged shared runtime and inherits whatever environment it was started with, exactly like any other macOS executable. `open` does NOT forward a shell's environment to the launched app (macOS launch services strips it); use `open --env MACAPK_X=1 -a /Applications/<App>.app` (per-key) or launch the binary inside `Contents/MacOS/` directly if you need this from a script.
- **The launcher GUI does three separate things**, in increasing order of specificity (later wins):
  1. Its own Settings panel translates screen-size / orientation / verbose-logging pickers into the matching `--width/--height`, `--orientation`, and `--verbose` CLI flags, and a few Dev-menu toggles (revealed by 5-tapping the launcher's title) into specific env vars -- see "The Mac.apk window's settings" below for the exact mapping.
  2. **Settings > Default environment** is a freeform key=value editor applied to *every* launch -- this is the general escape hatch for typing any `MACAPK_*` var (or anything else) without touching a Terminal at all.
  3. An individual app's **Get Info sheet** holds a *per-app* freeform key=value editor that is applied on top of (and overrides) the global Default environment for that one app only.

---

## The 12 flags a user actually needs

*(Everything else in this document exists for diagnosis; these are the ones worth knowing about for normal use.)*

| Flag | What it does |
| --- | --- |
| `--android-root <dir>` (CLI) / `MACAPK_ANDROID_ROOT` | Where the runtime creates the Android filesystem mirror (`data/data/<pkg>/...`) for the app being launched -- saves live here. Default (bare dev invocation only; an installed app's stub always passes its own bundle path) is `~/Library/Caches/Mac.apk/scratch/android`; also settable from the launcher's Settings panel folder picker. |
| `--orientation auto\|portrait\|landscape` (CLI) | Window orientation. `auto` reads the launchable activity's `android:screenOrientation` from the manifest and falls back to portrait. Exposed as a Picker in the launcher. |
| `--width N --height N` (CLI) | Explicit window pixel size, overriding the orientation default. Exposed as a size-preset Picker in the launcher (an "Auto" preset omits these and uses the biggest canvas the display supports). |
| `--fullscreen` / `MACAPK_FULLSCREEN=1` | Launches the app in a fullscreen window instead of a titled one. |
| `--hide-dock-icon` / `MACAPK_HIDE_DOCK_ICON=1` | Hides the runtime's own Dock icon/tile for this launch. |
| `MACAPK_FOLLOW_SYSTEM_APPEARANCE=0` | Forces light mode regardless of the Mac's current appearance. Unset (default) follows macOS's light/dark setting. |
| `--force-abi armeabi-v7a` (CLI) / `MACAPK_FORCE_ABI` | Forces the ARM32 lifter/recompiler path even when the APK ships an `arm64-v8a` library it would otherwise prefer. This is the launcher's "Force ARM32" Dev-menu toggle. |
| `MACAPK_LOG_LEVEL=<0-7>` | Runtime log verbosity floor (mirrors AOSP's own per-tag priority filter; unset defaults to INFO, AOSP's own default floor). Level 7 is needed to unlock the lowest-level `NativeLog.print` diagnostics. |
| `--dev-trace` (CLI) / `MACAPK_DEV_TRACE=1` | Turns on the broad diagnostic-tracing master switch (JNI, bionic-shim trace points, ...) in one step. Expect roughly 1 fps on a big game while it's on. This is the launcher's "Verbose trace (very slow)" Dev-menu toggle. |
| `MACAPK_ALLOW_UNSAFE_X18=1` | Diagnosis-only escape hatch that lets an arm64 guest load even when the host's x18-preservation capability probe fails. Never a fix for a real failure: macOS 26.4 is a hard floor for 64-bit native code. |
| `MACAPK_INSTALL_NOUI=1` | Skips the install confirmation dialog -- useful for a scripted/unattended install (`open --env MACAPK_INSTALL_NOUI=1 -a /Applications/Mac.apk.app <apk>`). |
| `MACAPK_EXTRACT_ASSETS=1` | Restores the pre-v1.0.1585 behaviour of extracting an app's assets onto disk instead of reading them from the installed APK in place. A bisect gate; only worth trying if an app that used to work stops finding its own assets. |

---

## CLI flags: `macapk-runtime` (`/Applications/Mac.apk.app/Contents/Resources/macapk-runtime`)

| Flag | Required/optional | Effect |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `--apk <path>` | Required unless `--peek-manifest` or `--gms` is given | The APK to launch. Made absolute (not symlink-resolved) before use, because `ApplicationInfo.sourceDir`/`getPackageCodePath()` etc. must answer with an absolute path the way a device does. Also exported as `MACAPK_APK_PATH` so `PresentedSdk` can read `targetSdkVersion` from the binary manifest before the framework's `Build.<clinit>` runs -- apktool strips `<uses-sdk>` from every decoded manifest, so the binary manifest is the only reliable source at that point. |
| `--width N` / `--height N` | Optional | Explicit window pixel size; overrides whatever the resolved orientation would have picked. |
| `--orientation auto\|portrait\|landscape` | Optional, default `auto` | `auto` reads `android:screenOrientation` off the launchable activity and falls back to portrait; `portrait`/`landscape` force it unconditionally. `--portrait`/`--landscape` are bare aliases for the same thing. |
| `--android-root <dir>` | Optional | User-visible directory for the Android filesystem mirror. Sets `MACAPK_ANDROID_ROOT` so every independent root-resolver downstream (the installer's cache fallback, the boot code's env chain, the GLES3.1 verdict cache) agrees. Falls back to the `MACAPK_ANDROID_ROOT` env var if the flag is absent, then to a compiled-in default expanded once `$HOME` is known. |
| `--verbose` | Optional | Sets the CLI-level verbose flag. |
| `--extract-only` | Optional | Non-launching mode: runs `apk_unpacker` + the android-root asset extract to backfill manifest/icon/`meta.json` for the launcher's Installed-Apps panel, then exits 0 -- no JVM, no Cocoa window, no native libs loaded. |
| `--peek-manifest <apk>` | Optional | Prints one JSON line (`package`, `versionCode`, `versionName`, `label`) read from the **binary** manifest, then exits. No extraction, no JVM, no window -- used so the install confirm dialog can show identity before paying for any of that. |
| `--gms <subcommand> [args...]` | Optional | Drives the GMS/Play-Services layer (accounts, sign-in, tokens, licensing) with no APK loaded. Everything after `--gms` is passed through verbatim as the subcommand's own args. This is the only path that can put the Google sign-in page on screen for the launcher's Accounts panel, and the only way an installed app (no launcher process to delegate to) gets one. Subcommands are handled by `com.macapk.gms.GmsCli` on the Java side (not further enumerated here; out of scope for a flag reference). |
| `--fullscreen` | Optional | Sets `MACAPK_FULLSCREEN=1` for this process -- see the env var's own row in "Launch & window" below. |
| `--hide-dock-icon` | Optional | Sets `MACAPK_HIDE_DOCK_ICON=1`. |
| `--dev-trace` / `--verbose-trace` | Optional | Sets `MACAPK_DEV_TRACE=1`, `MACAPK_TRACE_BL=1`, `MACAPK_TRACE_JNI_HANDLES=1` in one step -- the CLI/headless equivalent of the launcher's "Verbose Trace" Dev-menu toggle. Warns that it costs roughly 1 fps on HCR-class games. |
| `--force-abi <abi>` | Optional | Sets `MACAPK_FORCE_ABI=<abi>` -- the CLI equivalent of the launcher's "Force ARM32" toggle, so a headless run doesn't need a separate shell-env layer. |
| `-h` / `--help` | -- | Prints the usage banner and exits 0. |

Any unrecognized flag, or no `--apk`/`--peek-manifest`/`--gms` at all, prints usage and exits 2.

---

## The Mac.apk window's settings

The GUI translates a small set of pickers/toggles into the CLI flags and env vars above, then layers freeform user text on top:

| Setting | Maps to |
| --- | --- |
| Canvas-size preset Picker | `--width`/`--height` (omitted for the "Auto" preset, which lets the runtime pick the biggest size the display supports for the resolved orientation) |
| Orientation Picker (Auto/Portrait/Landscape) | `--orientation <value>` |
| "Show live log panel (verbose)" toggle | `--verbose` |
| "Write log to folder" toggle | Launcher-local file logging; not an env var passed to the runtime. |
| Android-root / app-storage folder picker ("where app files are stored") | `MACAPK_APP_CACHE_DIR` (the runtime treats this as authoritative when set) |
| App icon / label (derived automatically per installed app, not user-typed) | `MACAPK_APP_ICON`, `MACAPK_APP_LABEL`, `MACAPK_DEFAULT_ICON` (fallback icon bundled next to the runtime) |
| Dev menu -> "Force ARM32" toggle (menu revealed by 5-tapping the launcher's title) | `MACAPK_FORCE_ABI=armeabi-v7a` |
| Dev menu -> "Verbose trace (very slow)" toggle | `MACAPK_DEV_TRACE=1`, `MACAPK_TRACE_BL=1`, `MACAPK_TRACE_JNI_HANDLES=1` |
| "Verbose logging (all classloads)" toggle | When **off** (the default, for a fast/quiet UI run): sets `MACAPK_LOG_LEVEL=4` + `MACAPK_QUIET_CLASSLOAD=1`. When **on**: sets neither, so the run stays at AOSP's own INFO-level default floor with the classloader pair unsuppressed. A direct CLI run (no launcher involved) sets neither and is verbose by default. |
| "Show app versions in Applications" toggle | Cosmetic UI only (adds the version string to the Applications-panel row label); not an env var. |
| **Settings > Default environment** | Freeform key=value list, applied to every launch. |
| Per-app **Get Info** sheet environment | Freeform key=value list for that one app, applied on top of (overriding) the global Default environment. |

Order of application: derived/automatic vars first, then Dev-menu toggles, then the quiet-logging default, then `model.defaultEnv` (global user text), then the selected app's own `app.env` (per-app user text) -- so a per-app override always wins.

Separately, the tiny per-app stub every installed app actually runs reads `MACAPK_STUB_LOG` (`=0` disables its own stub-level logging), `MACAPK_STUB_NO_UI` (`=1` suppresses its error alert panel, so an unattended harness doesn't block on a dialog forever) and its own internal `MACAPK_STUB_REFRESHED` re-exec marker (v1.0.1592: a stub that differs from the installed Mac.apk's template replaces itself and re-executes), and the installer honours `MACAPK_RUNTIME_ROOT` and `MACAPK_CANONICAL_APP` overrides for where the staged shared runtime and the one canonical installed app live.

---

## Subsystem reference tables

Every `MACAPK_*` environment variable the shipped runtime, launcher or per-app stub reads, grouped by the subsystem that reads it.

Diagnostic/trace flags are kept with the subsystem whose file reads them (e.g. `MACAPK_TRACE_SQL` is under Install & storage, `MACAPK_TRACE_TOUCH` is under Launch & window) rather than pooled into one giant trace table. "Logging & tracing" below holds the flags whose reader is a cross-cutting hub file with no more specific subsystem home, plus the two master switches (`MACAPK_LOG_LEVEL`, `MACAPK_DEV_TRACE`).

---
## Launch & window

*(Diagnostic/trace flags whose primary reader happens to live in a launch/window/activity-lifecycle file are kept here with their subsystem rather than pooled into "Logging & tracing" below -- e.g. `MACAPK_TRACE_TOUCH` sits with touch/activity dispatch, not in the generic trace table.)*

| Flag | Default (unset) | Accepted values | Effect | Audience |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| MACAPK_ACTIVITYTHREAD_INSTANTIATE | Reflective `newInstance()` component construction | `mh` (MethodHandle-based construction) | Selects the primitive `AppComponentFactory` uses to instantiate Activities/Applications. `=mh` is an A/B-only alternate path (2 fewer java.base frames in a launch-chain trace). | experiment |
| MACAPK_ACTIVITYTHREAD_LAUNCH | Activity/Application construction goes through today's `Class.forName` path | `1` / `true` | Routes Application construction through an AOSP-shaped `ActivityThread` chain instead of the direct path. Off = today's behavior. | experiment |
| MACAPK_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TASK | Off | `1` | Extra handling for an intent carrying `NEW_TASK\|CLEAR_TASK` flags together. Default off; comment notes the common combination this addresses. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ACTIVITY_RECREATE | ON: a real `Activity.recreate()` happens on a resource-affecting config change | `0` | `=0` restores the pre-v1.0.996-era notify-only behavior (no actual recreate) instead of really recreating the Activity. | bisect |
| MACAPK_QUIT_PROCESS_GROUP | ON (v1.0.1658): on quit the app's OWN forked descendants (un-exec'd fork children, APK native executables via macapk-elfexec) are SIGKILLed with it, as AOSP ProcessRecord.killLocked -> killProcessGroup does; system-role children (install helper, relaunch shell, `open`) are left alone. Also routes NSApplication terminate: (Dock Quit, logout, a 'quit' Apple event) through the same teardown instead of AppKit's exit(0) | `0` | `=0` restores the pre-v1.0.1658 quit exactly (`_exit()` only, fork children re-parented to launchd; terminate: ends in exit(0)). Gate: `debug/app_procgroup_gate.sh`. | bisect |
| MACAPK_FORK_WATCHER | ON (2026-09-30): before an app's FIRST fork (raw clone() fork in the syscall router, or the by-name `fork()` shim `__macapk_arm64_fork`) a small watcher process (a grandchild, not the app's child; since the 2026-10-01 review a FRESH `macapk-elfexec --macapk-forkwatch <pid>` image posix_spawned from the shim's own bin/ dir, in its own session -- a fork() of the app pinned every page the app later rewrote; the double-fork remains only as the fallback when that helper is missing) holds a kqueue NOTE_EXIT on the app pid; every fork (and fork of a fork) registers with it, and on ANY app death (SIGKILL, SIGTERM, crash, exit) it SIGKILLs each registered fork's subtree, as AOSP killProcessGroup does. Pid reuse is guarded by the recorded start time | `0` | `=0` starts no watcher and registers nothing: every fork path is exactly the pre-change call (`fork()` a bare ::fork()), forks outlive a killed app. Gate: `debug/forkwatch_gate.sh`. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ROUTER_EXIT | ON (2026-09-30): a guest's raw `exit_group` (94) ends the process (`_exit(code & 0xff)`) and raw `exit` (93) ends the calling thread (`pthread_exit`) or, when it is the last thread, the process -- Linux kernel/exit.c semantics. Pre-change the router answered -ENOSYS and the syscall RETURNED: Instagram's ForkJail child fell through and spun at ~100% CPU | `0` | `=0` restores the -1/ENOSYS answer for non-ptrace-emulation guests. Gate: `debug/forkwatch_gate.sh` (exit cases). | bisect |
| MACAPK_CLOSE_REMOVES_TASK | ON (v1.0.1658): closing the app removes its task the way AOSP Task.removeActivities does -- the activities beneath the top are destroyed from the state they are in and never restored to the front first | `0` | `=0` restores the old restore-then-finish teardown (onRestart/onStart/onResume on each buried activity, plus a window re-apply that waited up to the 2 s quit cap on the AppKit main thread: Solitaire closed in 2021 ms vs 19 ms). Gate: `debug/closetask_gate.sh`. | bisect |
| MACAPK_QUIT_PUMPS_RENDER | ON (2026-09-30): while the close / Cmd-Q / Dock Quit waits on the host (AppKit main) thread for the Android main thread's teardown, that thread keeps running render ticks (NativeCocoa.hostRenderTick -> -[MTKView draw] -> drawInMTKView), because it IS our GLThread and apps pause on it (AOSP GLSurfaceView.GLThread keeps drawing while the UI thread pauses; libGDX AndroidGraphics.pause/destroy wait for onDrawFrame). On timeout it logs the Android main thread's stack. Once a tick has been pumped on an exiting quit, a backstop thread leaves the process at cap + 2 s if the tick has not come back (review 2026-10-01; a tick runs guest code no timeout preempts). | `0` | `=0` restores the plain 2 s await: every libGDX app (ShatteredPD, Apple Flinger, Retrowars, Unciv, Feudal Tactics, Klooni) sat out the whole cap with its pause never delivered (close 2.03-2.07 s vs 36-42 ms). Gate: `debug/quitpump_gate.sh`. | bisect |
| MACAPK_QUIT_HIDE_AFTER_PAUSE | ON (2026-09-30): during a close's teardown, once the top activity has returned from onPause the app's windows are ordered off screen (AOSP hides a finishing resumed activity already at the finish request, ActivityRecord.finishIfPossible setVisibility(false), and stops/destroys it only after the pause; hiding before the pause returns cut Unity's pause off in lanes19, so we hide right after it) and no more render ticks are pumped; onStop/onDestroy continue under the same 2 s cap | `0` | `=0` keeps the window on screen until the whole teardown returns: OpenTTD, whose own MainActivity.onDestroy sleeps 2000 ms before System.exit, showed a frozen window for 2 s. Gate: `debug/quitpump_gate.sh` (hide control). | bisect |
| MACAPK_ACTIVITY_REFERRER | ON | `0` | `Activity.getReferrer()` runs AOSP's body over the launching package (own, or the cross-app sender). | bisect |
| MACAPK_ACTIVITY_REUSE | ON | `0` | Self starts reuse an existing instance like ActivityStarter: SINGLE_TOP/singleTop on the top -> onNewIntent; CLEAR_TOP / singleTask / singleInstance -> finish everything above the instance (deferred resume) and onNewIntent, or recreate a standard instance started without SINGLE_TOP (Task.clearTopActivities). onNewIntent always follows onRestart+onStart (any targetSdk); a RESUMED top pauses, receives it and resumes with no window-focus movement; NEW_TASK | runtime/java/com/macapk/loader/ActivityHost.java reuseExistingInstance / onActivityFinished | bisect |
| MACAPK_ACTIVITY_THEME_ANCHOR | ON (current AOSP-faithful anchor behavior) | `0` | `=0` reverts to the pre-change theme-anchor resolution for an Activity. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ALLOW_HOST_URL_AT_LAUNCH | Off: a host URL open requested during launch is refused (with the reason logged) | `1` | `=1` restores the old "always open immediately" behavior for a URL an app tries to open the host browser with right at launch. | bisect |
| MACAPK_AOSP_ACTIVITY_THEME | ON (current forwarding behavior, since v914) | `0` | `=0` restores the pre-v914 theme-forwarding behavior for an Activity's resolved theme. | bisect |
| MACAPK_APP_CACHE_DIR | ANGLE's blob cache and the launcher's own cache root fall back to their own defaults | A directory path | The runtime's own cache root (used e.g. by the ANGLE shader-blob cache); the launcher sets this per-app so caches land under its own app-storage tree. | user |
| MACAPK_APP_ICON | The bundled Mac.apk default icon is used for the Dock tile | A filesystem path to a PNG | The launched app's own icon, applied to its Dock tile and window; set automatically by the launcher/stub from the app's install record, not typically hand-typed. | dev |
| MACAPK_APP_LABEL | Falls back to the Activity's own title | A display-name string | Window title / Dock tile label the launcher passes for the app being launched; `Activity` reads it before falling back to the activity title. | dev |
| MACAPK_AUDIO_SELFTEST | Off (no selftest at boot) | `1` | Drives the audio backend self-test at boot (tone/PCM roundtrip); combine with `MACAPK_AUDIO_DUMP=<path>` to capture the output. | diag |
| MACAPK_AWT_PREWARM | Off (gate inverted on purpose -- prewarming is opt-IN) | `1` | Opts in to prewarming AWT during boot; the comment notes the gate was deliberately inverted (default off) after measuring the prewarm's cost against a baseline. | experiment |
| MACAPK_CLINIT_PAD | 0 (no padding) | An integer *n* | Diagnostic-only: inserts *n* recursive stack frames before running an app's static initializers, to test how deep the launch call stack can safely get. ARM32-path only. | diag |
| MACAPK_CONFIG_REPORT_GATE | ON (current, since v1.0.1047) | `0` | `=0` restores the pre-v1.0.1047 behavior for whether a resource-only configuration change (vs. one the app's `configChanges` manifest entry actually declares handling) gets reported to the Activity. | bisect |
| MACAPK_DECOR_FITS_HONOUR | Off: window-insets "fits system windows" decor requests are ignored (comment says honouring them by default would currently be WORSE than not gating at all) | `1` | Restores honouring `WindowInsets`/`fitsSystemWindows` decor requests instead of ignoring them; explicitly a one-run kill switch, not a recommended default. | experiment |
| MACAPK_DECOR_WINDOW_LAYOUT | On (2026-09-29) -- whenever the traversal lays out the activity's installed content root, the window's DETACHED decor (the stub PhoneWindow decor beside it; skipped when the decor is the root or an ancestor of it) is measured EXACTLY and laid out at the window's size, as ViewRootImpl.performTraversals lays out a device's decor, so `getWindow().peekDecorView().getWidth()/getHeight()` answer the window size. Sized to the window FRAME (content width x contentBottom: nav bar excluded unless the app is immersive, letterbox applied), not the surface | `0` | Restores the never-laid-out 0x0 decor exactly (the method returns before touching anything). OpenTTD's SDL port then reads 0x0 and resets its video surface to 0x0 on every layout (29-35 `screen size changed ... to 0x0` per run) and clicks do nothing. docs/touch-input-owner-issues_2026-09-29.md; gate debug/touchinput_gate.sh (W1-W3, controls C2 + C3 mutant) | bisect |
| MACAPK_DRAG_AND_DROP | On (2026-09-29) -- View.startDragAndDrop / startDrag start an IN-WINDOW drag session (android.view.MacApkDragSession): refused when no pointer is down or a drag is active (AOSP transferTouchFocus / 'drag already in progress'), the source's touch stream is cancelled once, ACTION_DRAG_STARTED to the window, ENTERED/EXITED/LOCATION to the frontmost interested view (target-local coords), DROP with the ClipData, ENDED with the drop result; the shadow is drawn above the frame. events are delivered on the UI thread (hop to the main looper from the Cocoa thread); a drag from a floating window is refused; updateDragShadow/cancelDragAndDrop are real. setOnDragListener is real and OnDragListener has AOSP's onDrag(View, DragEvent) (both ungated) | `0` | Restores `startDragAndDrop` returning false (the drag never starts; nothing else in the session runs; updateDragShadow/cancelDragAndDrop no-op as before). Anuto TD then cannot place a tower (credits stay 500). docs/touch-input-owner-issues_2026-09-29.md; gate debug/touchinput_gate.sh (D2-D8 + D10-D12, control C1; production wiring D8/D10/D11/D12 also by the C3-C6 mutants) | bisect |
| MACAPK_DEFAULT_ICON | No fallback icon is available if the app has none of its own | A filesystem path to a PNG | The bundled Mac.apk fallback icon path, handed to the runtime so an app with no icon of its own is never blank/generic. Set automatically by the launcher/stub, not typically hand-typed. | dev |
| MACAPK_DISPLAY_DECOR | ON: `Display` reports a decor-adjusted (real == app) size ladder | `0` | `=0` collapses `Display`'s size-reporting ladder back to a single (real==app) answer instead of the AOSP-faithful decor-aware one. | bisect |
| MACAPK_DOCPICK_TEST_PATHS | unset (the real panel) | colon-separated host paths | Harness seam: answer a document pick with these paths WITHOUT showing a panel (logged when used). | dev |
| MACAPK_DOCSPROVIDER_DISPATCH | ON | `0` | android.provider.DocumentsProvider routes URIs and call() the way AOSP does (roots/document/children/tree query, getType, open*, METHOD_CREATE/RENAME/DELETE/COPY/MOVE/REMOVE/IS_CHILD/FIND_PATH/EJECT/METADATA, enforceTree); serves app-shipped providers too. `=0`: ContentProvider defaults (the pre-change path). v1.0.1653. | bisect |
| MACAPK_DOC_INITIAL_URI | ON | `0` | A picker intent's EXTRA_INITIAL_URI naming one of our documents/trees opens the panel at that folder (DocumentsUI launchToInitialUri). v1.0.1653. | bisect |
| MACAPK_DOC_PICKER | ON | `0` | GET_CONTENT / OPEN_DOCUMENT / CREATE_DOCUMENT resolve to DocumentsUI's PickActivity and open the macOS open/save panel; the result is DocumentsUI's exact shape and grants, served by HostDocumentsProvider under `com.android.externalstorage.documents`. `=0`: no resolver row, no picker (the start is unresolved). v1.0.1653, docs/document-picker-host-panel_2026-09-27.md. | bisect |
| MACAPK_DOC_TREE | ON | `0` | ACTION_OPEN_DOCUMENT_TREE opens a directory panel; the result is `buildTreeDocumentUri` with READ | PERSISTABLE | PREFIX (DocumentsUI onPickFinished :518-522); children are `macapk:<token>/<rel>` confined to the granted root; ExternalStorageProvider.shouldBlockFromTree analogues refused ("Can't use this folder"). Needs MACAPK_DOCSPROVIDER_DISPATCH. v1.0.1653. | runtime/java/com/macapk/loader/DocumentPickerHandler.java; HostDocumentsProvider.java; runtime/java/android/provider/DocumentsProvider.java | bisect |
| MACAPK_APP_ACCOUNTS | ON | `0` | android.accounts.AccountManager serves the account types the app's OWN authenticator declares (manifest service with action + meta-data android.accounts.AccountAuthenticator -> <account-authenticator accountType>): addAccountExplicitly/getAccountsByType/get+setUserData/peek+setAuthToken/invalidateAuthToken/get+set+clearPassword/removeAccountExplicitly with AccountManagerService semantics, persisted in <android_root>/data/system_ce/0/accounts_ce.properties; removeAccount runs the app's authenticator in-process (getAccountRemovalAllowed; an answer carrying KEY_INTENT does not remove); getAuthToken answers a stored token without the authenticator and otherwise runs the app's authenticator and saves its token (unless customTokens); renameAccount + getPreviousName; the store file is 0600 (lanes21 review); an unmanaged type is a SecurityException with AOSP's message; listeners get the current accounts. com.google stays with com.macapk.gms. Without it Reddit (com.reddit.account) could never persist a signed-in session (lanes20 reddit-password-integrity). =0: every pre-change answer (false / empty / no-op / null / removeAccount false). | bisect |
| MACAPK_CALLING_ACTIVITY | ON | `0` | Activity.getCallingActivity()/getCallingPackage() answer AOSP's resultTo: the requestor of a startActivityForResult (claimed at attach, cleared when finish() sends the result), null for a plain start and after finish; a requestor in another process still answers null. Returning null unconditionally made the Facebook SDK's LoginFragment bounce ("null calling package") in every app, so Facebook sign-in never started (lanes20). =0: always null (pre-change). | bisect |
| MACAPK_CHOOSER_INITIAL | ON | `0` | An ACTION_CHOOSER whose EXTRA_INTENT the host cannot serve resolves to the first servable of its first 2 EXTRA_INITIAL_INTENTS (ChooserActivity lists them as targets; no EXTRA_INTENT, or a non-Intent among the 2, finishes the chooser as on AOSP); e.g. createChooser(ACTION_PICK images) + initial GET_CONTENT image/* opens the picker. `=0`: a chooser resolves for EXTRA_INTENT alone (the pre-change path). v1.0.1658, docs/permissions-camera-exec-lane_2026-09-29.md. | bisect |
| MACAPK_PHOTO_PICKER | ON | `0` | MediaStore.ACTION_PICK_IMAGES (the photo picker; androidx PickVisualMedia on API 33+) opens the same macOS panel filtered as MediaProvider's MimeFilterUtils does (image/* + video/* unless EXTRA_MIME_TYPES / a media type narrows it; a non-media EXTRA_MIME_TYPES cancels), multi-select when EXTRA_PICK_IMAGES_MAX > 1, READ-only grant. `=0`: PICK_IMAGES unresolved (the pre-change path). v1.0.1658. | bisect |
| MACAPK_PURPOSE_REFRESH | ON | `0` | The control panel (once per launch, off the main thread) and `macapk-installhelper reconcile` re-stamp every installed bundle whose Info.plist lacks a TCC purpose string the generator writes (InstallPipeline.refreshStalePurposeStrings, via finalizeIdentity; a running app is skipped by its pidfile flock, and the pidfile reads "0" while the refresh holds it), and reconcile reports a `stalePurposeStrings` finding. `=0`: neither happens (nothing re-stamped an installed bundle before). Read by the Swift GUI/helper process, so a Dock-launched GUI needs `launchctl setenv`. v1.0.1658, docs/permissions-camera-exec-lane_2026-09-29.md. | bisect |
| MACAPK_TYPEDARRAY_TEXTARRAY_AOSP | ON | `0` | TypedArray.getTextArray answers like AOSP: null exactly when !hasValue (unset, @null, @empty), the array for a reference, NotFoundException for a literal (an unreadable reference, and hasValue's no-source universal-true arm, keep the old empty answer: our gap, not a non-array). `=0`: an empty array for every non-array case (the pre-change path, which made AnkiDroid's All-files item vacuously granted). v1.0.1658. | bisect |
| MACAPK_EMPTY_WINDOW_DECOR | ON: an activity that never calls setContentView gets its window's OWN decor, empty, added at the end of its resume (AOSP ActivityThread.handleResumeActivity android14 :5016-5041): the theme's windowBackground (a title bar on a legacy screen_title theme) with any view the app added to android.R.id.content, which stays the content FRAME. Not added when the activity is finishing, has windowNoDisplay / setVisible(false), started an activity for result from its own onResume (Activity.mStartedActivity, cleared before onResume as performResumeActivity :4936-4939 does; added at its next resume), or is not the top of the shared window's stack. No framework-built GLSurfaceView, and GLSurfaceView.sLastRendererBearing is never written. 2026-09-27 engine-hook lane. | `0` | Restores the pre-change path byte for byte: ActivityHost.ensureFallbackContentView after onCreate builds an empty GLSurfaceView (black, pulls in ANGLE, rebinds the shared MTKView delegate) unless the v1.0.504 renderer-bearing guard skips it. Retirement note: docs/fallback-glsurfaceview-content-retired_2026-09-27.md; gate `emptywindow`. | bisect |
| MACAPK_HANDSHAKE_DRAIN_ALL | Off: the v1.0.575 render-thread main-queue drain is armed only for a GameActivity/NativeActivity (and never with MACAPK_AOSP_SURFACE_ORDER=1). | `1` | Arms the drain for EVERY launch's onStart/onResume window, the pre-v1.0.1645 behaviour (it ran AntennaPod's LAUNCH_ACTIVITY on the AppKit thread). Bisect only. | bisect |
| MACAPK_FCM_TEST_PUSH | Off (no synthetic push) | `k=v,k2=v2` (or `1` for a default payload) | Injects one synthetic push through the real FCM delivery path at boot, for testing an app's push handling without a real server round-trip. | diag |
| MACAPK_FCM_TEST_PUSH_DELAY_MS | 20000 (20s) | An integer (milliseconds) | Overrides the delay before `MACAPK_FCM_TEST_PUSH` fires, so the app's boot/registration has time to finish first. | diag |
| MACAPK_FIT_WINDOW_TO_ASPECT | ON: the window is nudged to the app's aspect ratio rather than sitting in a letterbox band | `0` | `=0` restores the pre-change behavior of leaving a letterbox band instead of fitting the window to the content's aspect ratio. | bisect |
| MACAPK_FOLLOW_SYSTEM_APPEARANCE | ON: light/dark mode follows the current macOS appearance | `0` | `=0` forces light mode regardless of the Mac's appearance setting; the safe default when unset is to follow macOS. | user |
| MACAPK_FULLSCREEN | Off: normal titled window | `1` | Launches into a fullscreen window. Set by `--fullscreen` on the CLI. | user |
| MACAPK_HIDE_DOCK_ICON | Off: the runtime shows its own Dock icon | `1` | Hides the runtime's own Dock icon/tile for this launch. Set by `--hide-dock-icon` on the CLI. | user |
| MACAPK_HOST_BROWSER_SCHEMES | ON | `0` | The host browser row (com.macapk.host) answers a web (http/https) query with ONE filter carrying BOTH http and https, as a real browser's VIEW filter does (Chromium AndroidManifest :281-289); AppAuth's isFullBrowser requires both. =0: the queried scheme only (pre-change: "The app couldn't find a browser"). | bisect |
| MACAPK_HOST_URL_GRACE_MS | A built-in default grace period | An integer (milliseconds) | Overrides the grace period `MACAPK_ALLOW_HOST_URL_AT_LAUNCH`'s launch-time URL-open check uses. | dev |
| MACAPK_RECORD_INPUT | unset = OFF (unless the ENABLED folder switch below applies) | a file path, a directory (ending `/` or existing: writes `<pkg>_<YYYYmmdd-HHMMSS>.inject` there with retention), or `0` (forces OFF, even with ENABLED) | INPUT RECORDER (`InputRecorder`, 2026-10-01): records every host input event AT DELIVERY (TouchDispatcher.doDispatch / deliverKey / deliverTrackpadNow, on the thread that hands it to the app) as a MACAPK_INJECT_FILE script (`inputrec` header + `+<ms> ptr | mptr | ...`) that replays exactly; files 0600; password-field text is never written (`type-redacted <n>`). Read once per process (static final): OFF costs one constant test per event and allocates nothing. docs/input-recorder_2026-10-01.md | runtime/java/com/macapk/loader/InputRecorder.java | diagnostic |
| ~/Library/Application Support/Mac.apk/recordings/ENABLED (file, not env) | absent = OFF | present = record every launch of every app (Dock-launched INSTALLED apps have no environment) into that folder, 20 newest per app, 64 MB total, 16 MB per recording | The input recorder's switch for the owner: `python3 debug/inputrec.py on` / `off`. Honoured ONLY when the runtime's android root is an installed bundle's (`*.app/Contents/android`) and IGNORED when MACAPK_INJECT_FILE / MACAPK_DEBUG_AUTOCLICK / MACAPK_DEBUG_AUTOKEY is set, so no harness run (scratch root, or those envs) writes into or prunes the owner's folder; MACAPK_RECORD_INPUT=0 also overrides it. | diagnostic |
| MACAPK_INSTALL_NOUI | Off: the install confirm dialog is shown | `1` | Skips the install confirmation dialog -- how an unattended script (`open --env MACAPK_INSTALL_NOUI=1 -a ...`) installs an APK without a human clicking through a dialog. | user |
| MACAPK_KEYBIND_PROFILE | The launcher auto-picks (and remembers) a keybind profile for the package on first run | A profile name string | Forces a specific keyboard-input profile instead of the auto-picked/remembered one for this package. | dev |
| MACAPK_KILL_ON_UNCAUGHT | Gated behavior (see source; default is the safer of the two, flip-then-land shape) | a truthy value | Controls whether the process is actually killed after an uncaught exception is reported, vs. reported-only. | bisect |
| MACAPK_LAUNCH_ACTION | `ACTION_MAIN` | An Intent action string | Overrides the action of the synthetic launch Intent Boot builds for the app's launchable component. | dev |
| MACAPK_LIFECYCLE_TRACE | Off | any non-null value | Traces Activity/Application lifecycle callback order (onCreate/onStart/onResume/...). | diag |
| MACAPK_MANIFEST_EXPORTED_DEFAULT | ON | `0` | An activity / alias / receiver / service with no `android:exported` is exported iff it has an `<intent-filter>` (ParsedActivityUtils :486-498, ParsedServiceUtils :82-84). | bisect |
| MACAPK_MANIFEST_PROVIDER_EXPORTED_LEGACY | ON | `0` | A `<provider>` with no `android:exported` is exported iff targetSdk < 17 (ParsedProviderUtils :86-93). v1.0.1653. | bisect |
| MACAPK_MEDIA_SELFTEST | Off | `1` | Runs the media-decode self-test at boot. | diag |
| MACAPK_MENU_TRACE | Off | any non-null value | Traces options-menu inflate/dismiss and the XML-resource-inflate path it shares with `Resources`' menu handling, plus locating hosted androidx Fragments. | diag |
| MACAPK_PERSISTED_URI_GRANTS | ON | `0` | ContentResolver.take/releasePersistableUriPermission and getPersistedUriPermissions follow UriGrantsManagerService (a PERSISTABLE grant required, mode subset, cap 512, persisted time), stored in the app data dir. `=0`: the old no-ops. v1.0.1653. | bisect |
| MACAPK_PI_UNRESOLVED_CANCELED | ON | `0` | An ACTIVITY PendingIntent that resolves to nothing: `send()` throws CanceledException (after OnFinished), `startIntentSender[ForResult]` throws ActivityNotFoundException "No Activity found to handle null" (PendingIntent :1048-1056, PendingIntentRecord.sendInner :553-629, Activity.startIntentSenderForResultInner :5969-5978). `=0`: swallowed. v1.0.1653. | bisect |
| MACAPK_PM_REGISTRY_ACTIVITIES | ON | `0` | Another installed Mac.apk app's activities resolve from ITS installed manifest (queryIntentActivities scoped to it, getActivityInfo, getLaunchIntentForPackage). | bisect |
| MACAPK_PROVIDER_PROCESS_GATE | Gated behavior for protected-fleet apps (see source) | `1` | Flips a ContentProvider process-affinity check used by some protected-fleet apps. | bisect |
| MACAPK_RESTART_INTENTS | No restart-intents file; a relaunch after the previous process exits starts fresh | A filesystem path | Names the file the previous process's exit handler writes pending relaunch Intents into, so `Boot` can hand them to the newly-started process -- the mechanism behind an app-initiated "restart the app" flow. | dev |
| MACAPK_RESULT_QUEUED_LAUNCH | ON | `0` | startActivityForResult whose launch is QUEUED (posted LAUNCH_ACTIVITY from inside the running main loop) keeps its pairing (keyed by Intent identity, so two starts in one turn keep their own; re-keyed to the COPY an implicit start launches, and bound to the Intent an IntentSender send dispatches) until the launch is dispatched; ActivityHost settles RESULT_CANCELED only if no activity claimed it (missing target, or a top delivery as AOSP does; never to a requestor that already finished). =0: settle at once, which answered every in-app for-result start RESULT_CANCELED before its target attached (pre-change). | bisect |
| MACAPK_RETIRE_UNITY_PROBE | ON (since 2026-09-27): no Unity render-gate probe -- no `unity-state` log line, no reflective read of `mUnityPlayer.mState`, no `resumeUnity()` call, no render-gate watch thread. Window focus is delivered exactly as before (the "delivering window focus to X (surface ready)" line stays), as AOSP does it: the app's own `onWindowFocusChanged` forwards to its player | `0` | `=0` restores the old probe exactly: one `unity-state after-surface-focus` line per focus delivery for EVERY app (it was never gated), `MACAPK_UNITY_FORCE_RESUME=1` live again, and under `MACAPK_TRACE_LIFECYCLE=1` the per-callback probe + the 60 s watch. Offline gate `debug/geh_unity-render-gate-probe_gate.sh` (`--log` for the live falsifier: 0 unity-state lines by default, `--expect-probe` for `=0`); docs/unity-render-gate-probe-retired_2026-09-27.md. Delete with the probe after one clean sweep. | bisect |
| MACAPK_REWIRE_KEEP_CANVAS (bisect gate, default ON since v1.0.997) | ON: a rewired root view keeps its Canvas-tier driver attached | `0` | `=0` restores the pre-v1.0.997 behavior where a rewired root view lost its Canvas-tier rendering driver. | bisect |
| MACAPK_SELF_CAPTURE_WINDOW_MS | A built-in default window | An integer (milliseconds) | Reads at the source; effect not further documented at the site beyond being a self-capture time window (see the Rendering section's `MACAPK_SELF_CAPTURE*` family for the mechanism this tunes). | diag |
| MACAPK_SKIP_QOS_BOOST | Off: the QoS boost call runs | `1` | Skips a QoS-class boost call during boot, as a targeted workaround (see `docs/runtime_2026-07-23.md`) for whatever cost that call was found to have. | bisect |
| MACAPK_STACK_PAD | Off (0) | An integer *n* | Diagnostic: pads the launch dispatch stack by *n* frames, ARM32-path-only companion to `MACAPK_CLINIT_PAD` for isolating "the launch stack got deeper" symptoms. | diag |
| MACAPK_START_ALIAS | ON | `0` | An `<activity-alias>` launches its targetActivity. | bisect |
| MACAPK_START_ANF | ON | `0` | An implicit start nothing resolves, or an explicit start of a component in no installed package, throws `ActivityNotFoundException` in both Instrumentation.checkStartActivityResult shapes (android14 :2232-2240); startActivityForResult then leaves no pairing. `=0`: silently dropped. | bisect |
| MACAPK_START_EXPORTED_ONLY | ON | `0` | A caller targeting SDK 34+ matches only EXPORTED components on an implicit start (ResolveIntentHelper :115-148). | bisect |
| MACAPK_START_RESOLVE | ON | `0` | Implicit `startActivity` resolves through the ONE resolver (PackageManager.queryIntentActivities, MATCH_DEFAULT_ONLY): the app's OWN implicit targets launch (they were dropped); an unpinned https VIEW never self-routes (Android 12+ unverified web links go to the browser). `=0`: the pre-v1.0.1652 host-only path. | bisect |
| MACAPK_STUB_LOG | On: the per-app stub logs its own diagnostic lines | `0` | `=0` turns the per-app stub's own logging off outright. | dev |
| MACAPK_STUB_REFRESHED | unset | `1` (set by the stub itself) | INTERNAL re-exec marker, never for a user to set: when a per-app stub finds its own file differs from the installed Mac.apk's stub template it swaps the template in and re-executes itself with this set, so the fresh copy knows it was just refreshed (it logs "re-executed") and a copy that still differs does not loop; the stub unsets it before handing over to the runtime (v1.0.1592, `StubRefresh` in InstallCore.swift). | dev |
| MACAPK_TOUCH_DIAG | Off | any non-null value | Traces where a touch lands in the Activity/View dispatch chain (Java side); the native counterpart is the ARM32-side half of this same env var (see the Rendering/Native tables' `MACAPK_TOUCH_DIAG` cross-reference). | diag |
| MACAPK_TRACE_APPINFO | Off | any non-null value | Traces `ContextWrapper`'s `ApplicationInfo` resolution path. | diag |
| MACAPK_TRACE_DEFSTYLE | Off (zero cost when unset) | any non-null value | Traces the 4-arg `obtainStyledAttributes`/theme `defStyleAttr` resolution chain across `Context`, `Resources`, `ContextThemeWrapper`, and `View` -- the flag the theme-anchor investigations cite repeatedly. | diag |
| MACAPK_TRACE_GETSVC | Off (except `jobscheduler`, always traced) | `1` | Traces `ContextWrapper.getSystemService()` lookups; `jobscheduler` is always traced regardless of this flag. | diag |
| MACAPK_TRACE_JNI | Off | any non-null value | Patches the process-wide JNIEnv function-pointer table to log every JNI call made through it. | diag |
| MACAPK_TRACE_LAUNCH_STACK | Off | `1` | Dumps the call stack of the app's own launch path at the point Application construction begins. | diag |
| MACAPK_TRACE_LIFECYCLE | Off | `1` | Logs one `lifecycle-> <callback> on <class>` line per lifecycle callback ActivityHost delivers (`runTolerant`), app-agnostic (a narrower sibling of `MACAPK_LIFECYCLE_TRACE`; used by `debug/back_probe.sh`). The Unity render-gate probe it also used to run after each callback, and its 60 s watch thread, run only under `MACAPK_RETIRE_UNITY_PROBE=0` since 2026-09-27. | diag |
| MACAPK_TRACE_TITLE | Off | any non-null value | Traces the ActionBar/Toolbar title-propagation chain from `Activity.setTitle()`. | diag |
| MACAPK_TRACE_TOUCH | Off | `1` | Logs the first few dispatched `MotionEvent`s (Java side: `MotionEvent`, `View`). | diag |
| MACAPK_UITHREAD_TRACE | Off (read once, cached) | any non-null value | Traces `Handler.post()`/`Looper` queueing: which thread queued what, and how often -- read once at class-init instead of per-call, per the comment, because a prior version paid a `System.getenv` cost on every `post()`. | diag |
| MACAPK_UNITY_FORCE_RESUME (⚠ diagnostic only, NOT A FIX per its own comment; RETIRED 2026-09-27) | Off | `1` | Reflectively invokes the Unity player's `resumeUnity()` after a surface-focus delivery. Read ONLY under `MACAPK_RETIRE_UNITY_PROBE=0` (ignored by default since 2026-09-27); its own removal condition was met the day it landed (da9f7d5f found the real cause, a fatal Firebase messaging init). | diag (retired) |
| MACAPK_URI_GRANT_ENFORCE | ON (needs MACAPK_PERSISTED_URI_GRANTS) | `0` | URI grants GATE access, as UriGrantsManagerService makes them on a device: ContentResolver / ContentProviderClient run ContentProvider.Transport's enforceRead/WritePermissionInner before calling a provider that stands in for ANOTHER package (HostDocumentsProvider = com.android.externalstorage, requires MANAGE_DOCUMENTS), so an un-granted document URI throws SecurityException (getType goes anonymous); DocumentsProvider.callUnchecked runs its checks and records narrow grants (canonicalize / rename / copy / move); Context.checkUriPermission and its family answer from the grants (AMS.checkUriPermission). `=0`: every check answers GRANTED and HostDocumentsProvider's per-pick capability alone decides, as before. v1.0.1657. | bisect |
| MACAPK_URL_SCHEMES | ON | `0` | Host URL intake: at boot the bundle Info.plist CFBundleURLTypes is kept equal to the manifest VIEW+DEFAULT+BROWSABLE schemes (minus http/https/mailto/tel/geo, pseudo-schemes, the OS-reserved schemes smb/otpauth/pcast/rtsp/x-apple-*/itms*/..., and any scheme LaunchServices already lists a NON-Mac.apk handler for -- a native app that owns it keeps it; when the LaunchServices query is unavailable nothing is changed) and the bundle is re-registered with lsregister; a kAEGetURL Apple Event handler (installed before finishLaunching) queues the URL, and Java delivers it as a browser-shaped VIEW+BROWSABLE+NEW_TASK intent to the app own exported activity (root when it cold-launched the app). =0: no registration, no handler, no delivery (the pre-change path). | bisect |
| MACAPK_URL_REDIRECT_OPENER | ON (needs MACAPK_URL_SCHEMES) | `0` | Redirect routing for a SHARED sign-in scheme -- PROVEN for fbconnect ONLY (Facebook SDK Custom Tab; several Mac.apk apps bundle it; Firebase's genericidp/recaptcha hand-off is an `intent:` URI this does not touch): a user-initiated host-browser open (http/https; HostUrlIntake.shouldClaim, decided AFTER the refusal checks, never on a programmatic or harness-refused open) makes this app's bundle the LaunchServices default handler of every scheme it claims that another Mac.apk bundle also handles and no native app does (NSWorkspace setDefaultApplicationAtURL:toOpenURLsWithScheme:), so the redirect returns to the tab's opener as Chrome's Custom Tab client rule does on a device. =0: LaunchServices' own binding, which sent Duolingo's fbconnect://cct.com.duolingo redirect to the installed Subway Surf (measured, lanes20). | bisect |
| MACAPK_URL_INTENT_HANDOFF | ON (needs MACAPK_URL_SCHEMES) | `0` | The browser's part of an `intent:` URI. On a device the BROWSER parses `intent:` (Intent.parseUri URI_INTENT_SCHEME), sanitises it (BROWSABLE added, component and selector cleared, S.browser_fallback_url consumed) and starts the named package; Firebase Auth's non-GMS redirect page navigates to `intent://firebase.auth/#Intent;scheme=genericidp | runtime/java/com/macapk/loader/HostUrlIntake.java INTENT_HANDOFF / withIntentHandoff / hostIntentFor; debug/urlscheme_gate.sh H rows | bisect |
| MACAPK_USER_ROT_RESTORE (bisect gate, since v1.0.996) | ON | `0` | `=0` restores the pre-v1.0.996 user-rotation-restore behavior. | bisect |
| MACAPK_VIDEO_IMAGE_SELFTEST | Off | `1` | Runs the video/image self-test at boot (mirrors `MACAPK_MEDIA_SELFTEST`'s gating shape). | diag |
| MACAPK_XAPP_RECEIVE | ON | `0` | The receiving app drains its cross-app inbox at boot (oldest exported arrival becomes the ROOT), on a doorbell ring, on unhide and on NSApplicationDidBecomeActive; DELIVER_TO_TOP / TASK_TO_FRONT / PUSH dispositions. | bisect |
| MACAPK_XAPP_START | ON | `0` | A start that resolves to ANOTHER installed Mac.apk app is handed to it: bundle guard (registry + /Applications + Info.plist identity), exported/permission checks, a Parcel envelope in the target's inbox, the AF_UNIX doorbell, then `open -a`. | bisect |
## Install & storage

| Flag | Default (unset) | Accepted values | Effect | Audience |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| MACAPK_ANDROID_ROOT | Falls back to `$HOME/Documents/android` (native resolver); a Java-side `~/Library/Caches/Mac.apk/scratch/android` dev fallback is inert in real runs since the runtime always passes an already-resolved path | A filesystem path (tilde expanded) | Overrides the root of the user-visible Android filesystem mirror (data/app, sdcard, etc.) that the boot code, Environment, SettingsStore and the native path-remap shim all resolve against. Consulted after a CLI `--android-root` value and before the `$HOME/Documents/android` default. | user |
| MACAPK_CANONICAL_APP | `/Applications/Mac.apk.app` | A filesystem path to a .app bundle | Overrides which installed app bundle the build/promote/doctor/restage scripts treat as the one canonical installed Mac.apk, for staging, promotion, and staleness checks. | dev |
| MACAPK_COMPONENT_GATING | ON: component/application enabled-state gating (AOSP's PackageUserStateUtils.isEnabled) is enforced | "0" disables; anything else is ON | Default-on gate for enforcing manifest/runtime component-enabled state on provider and component construction. Setting it to 0 degrades the check to the pre-v1.1.0 always-manifestEnabled behavior for A/B. | bisect |
| MACAPK_DEBUG_REWRITER | Off; boot logs stay quiet | "1" | Logs every class ClassFileRewriter detects and rewrites (e.g. constructor/supertype fixups), used to verify byte-identical rewritten output when other rewriter gates are toggled. | diag |
| MACAPK_DEX_FRAME_DEBUG | Off | Any non-null value (presence check) | Names the bytecode offset that caused CodeWriter to drop a StackMapTable during DEX conversion, since a dropped table is otherwise invisible until a much later "expecting a stackmap frame" verifier error. | diag |
| MACAPK_DEX_FRAME_SELFCHECK | Off, saving the doubled frame-encoding work | Any non-null value | Re-decodes every StackMapTable the writer produces and fails loudly if it does not read back as the frames it was given, turning the frame encoder into a self-checked component. | diag |
| MACAPK_DEX_INIT_REPOINT | ON: the plan that re-synthesizes R8-deleted constructors is built and applied | "0" disables it | Controls the fix that undoes R8's constructor re-pointing (calling a superclass constructor directly), which ART tolerates but the JVM's split verifier rejects per JVMS 4.9.2. "0" is an A/B escape hatch that turns the whole fix off. | bisect |
| MACAPK_DEX_NARROW_DEBUG | Off | Any non-null value | Diagnostic-only flag naming methods whose type inference fell back to the ART exception-edge rule, where residual handler-frame mismatches concentrate. | diag |
| MACAPK_DEX_OUTLINE_DEBUG | Off | Any non-null value | Logs the specific reason MethodOutliner refused to hoist a region out of an oversized method; refusals are otherwise invisible since the class just fails to translate as it did before this transform existed. | diag |
| MACAPK_DEX_OUTLINE_FORCE | -1/off; the real 65535-byte JVM code_length cap is the only trigger | An integer (a pretend byte-length threshold) | Lowers the "method too large" threshold so ordinary small test methods get outlined too, letting the maintainers' semantic test diff outliner output against javac (javac itself cannot compile a method that actually hits 65535 bytes). | experiment |
| MACAPK_DEX_CLINIT_SPILL | On | 0 restores the argument-slot refusal (byte-identical pre-change output) | LAST RESORT for a <clinit> (not an interface's): when MethodSplitter's parameter-only retry ladder fails and one of its refusals was JVMS 4.3.3's 255-argument-slot ceiling, a second ladder lets a part over the ceiling receive its values through private static synthetic fields (macapk$spill$N) instead of parameters; every method the parameter ladder can place is unchanged. Without it such a <clinit> is STUBBED and the class never initialises (Instagram X/11D, 1,234 enum constants). Gate: debug/clinit_spill_gate.sh. | bisect |
| MACAPK_DEX_INITIALIZER_HELPER_FINALS | On | 0 keeps ACC_FINAL (byte-identical pre-change output) | In a class emitted at class-file version 53+ (a NestHost/NestMembers attribute raises it to 55), clears ACC_FINAL on each own field a synthetic helper split or outlined out of an initializer writes: statics written from a <clinit> helper, instance fields from an <init> helper. HotSpot otherwise throws IllegalAccessError ("Update to [non-]static final field ... attempted from a different method") at the helper's first put: the class never initialises, or no instance is ever constructed. Gate: debug/clinit_spill_gate.sh. | bisect |
| MACAPK_DEX_SPLIT_DEBUG | Off | Any non-null value | Logs why MethodSplitter refused to split an oversized method, and how many parts a split method produced. | diag |
| MACAPK_DEX_SPLIT_FORCE | -1/off; the real 65535-byte code limit is the only trigger | An integer (bytes; a pretend code-size limit) | Lowers MethodSplitter's size threshold so ordinary methods get split, letting the semantic gate diff split output against javac on small cases. | experiment |
| MACAPK_DEX_SPLIT_NO_LIVENESS | Liveness-based trimming is on, so a split part only receives registers actually live at its entry | Any non-null value disables it | Escape hatch that makes MethodSplitter pass every DEFINED register to a split part instead of only the LIVE ones, so a suspected liveness-analysis bug can be A/B'd in one run. | bisect |
| MACAPK_DEX_SUPER_IFACE | The super-interface substitution plan is built and used | "0" disables it | Escape hatch to turn off the plan that substitutes default-interface-method call targets during DEX conversion. | bisect |
| MACAPK_DEX_THREADS | `min(available processor count, a fixed max)` worker threads | An integer (thread count); values below 1 ignored | Overrides the DEX-to-class conversion worker-thread count. Output is byte-identical regardless of thread count, so this mainly exists to A/B the parallel and sequential paths. | experiment |
| MACAPK_DEX_WIDE_HANDLERS | ON when the method has wide handler edges: exception-handler edges are computed from every instruction rather than only ones that can throw | "0" disables it | Reconciles a Dalvik/JVM disagreement about which instructions route to an exception handler; "0" restricts edges to instructions canThrow() recognizes. | bisect |
| MACAPK_DEXFILE_DEFINE_IN_LOADER | ON (since 2026-09-30) | 0 disables (only a leading `0` turns it off) | `DexFile.loadClass(name, loader)` / `loadClassBinaryName` DEFINES the class in `loader` when it is one of our `BaseDexClassLoader`s (AppClassLoader, PathClassLoader, DexClassLoader), as ART's `DexFile_defineClassNative` does (`DefineClass(..., class_loader, ...)`), so `Class.forName(name)` / `loader.loadClass(name)` on that loader finds it afterwards. `=0` = the previous path (a class defined by a private child `URLClassLoader`, invisible to the passed loader); a loader that is not a `BaseDexClassLoader` always takes the old path. Efteling's Adyen 3DS2 SDK defines a class out of a dex it writes to code_cache then does a plain `Class.forName` from the app loader (`ThreeDS2Service.<clinit>` -> `ExceptionInInitializerError` killed React Native's TurboModule setup). Gate `dexfiledefine`. | behaviour |
| MACAPK_EXTRACT_ASSETS | Off; assets are read directly from the APK in place, and any previously-extracted asset tree is reclaimed (deleted) on launch | "1" | Restores the legacy behavior of tar-extracting assets/* from the APK into the app's data directory instead of reading them from the APK in place. It is the bisect gate for the "assets read from the APK" change. | bisect |
| MACAPK_EXTRACT_ONLY | The unpacker assumes this process will boot the app afterward, so it waits for any in-flight background DEX conversion to finish | "1" (set internally) | Internal signal, set by the runtime when the launcher's --extract-only install-mode CLI flag is used, telling the unpacker to return to the installer immediately rather than waiting on a background DEX conversion. | dev |
| MACAPK_FD_TRACE | Off | Any non-null value | Logs AssetFileDescriptor construction (with a stack trace) and getParcelFileDescriptor() calls, to debug native consumers (e.g. Unity) that mmap an asset slice. | diag |
| MACAPK_FORCE_ANIM_SCALE_0 | Off; Settings.Global animation-scale reads return their normal value | "1" | Forces the animator/transition/window animation duration scales to 0 so Compose's animateTo() snaps instantly, as a decisive test for animations that appear frozen. Explicitly marked not a shipping flag. | diag |
| MACAPK_FORCE_REEXTRACT | Off; a fresh asset tar-extraction or native-library unzip is skipped whenever the on-disk freshness stamp already matches the current APK identity | "1" | Forces re-extraction of assets (when MACAPK_EXTRACT_ASSETS=1) and of the install directory's native libraries, ignoring the freshness stamp that would otherwise skip the work. | diag |
| MACAPK_FW_RES_APK | ⚠ CORRECTED 2026-09-20 (Phase 5 step 9, v1.0.1590): the two fallbacks after the env-var/next-to-jar lookup (apktool's `~/Library/apktool/framework/1.apk` and a cwd-relative `thirdparty/framework-res/*.apk`) were deleted along with apktool retirement. An unset/invalid value with no `tools/framework-res.apk` staged next to the jars is now a loud error (`NativeLog.e`: "framework resource apk NOT FOUND"), not a silent fallback. | A filesystem path to a framework-res.apk | Pins an explicit framework-res.apk as the source for both the framework asset mount (AssetManager) and the framework resource table (ResourceIndex), ahead of every other candidate. Set only by the maintainers' answer-probe harness to match its API-30 reference emulator. | diag |
| MACAPK_GLIDE_HTTPFETCHER_SHIM | Off; Glide's DownloadRequestLoader class is left untouched | "1" | Opt-in bytecode rewrite that redirects F-Droid's Glide-based DownloadRequestLoader to a framework HttpFetcher_Macdr shim. Still opt-in because of a cross-classloader visibility issue with Glide's own interfaces. | experiment |
| MACAPK_INSTALLHELPER | Resolves macapk-installhelper next to the running runtime binary's bundle, else the canonical installed app | A filesystem path to an executable | Picks which macapk-installhelper binary the install path invokes; it does not change what the helper demands, since the helper alone owns the install consent/authorization gate. | dev |
| MACAPK_PM_ALWAYS_METADATA | Off; the GET_META_DATA Bundle is only populated when the caller passes the GET_META_DATA flag | "1" | Forces getApplicationInfo/getPackageInfo to always populate the metadata Bundle regardless of the caller's flags, restoring the pre-v1.0.923 unconditional behavior for A/B. | bisect |
| MACAPK_PM_REGISTRY | ON; another Mac.apk-installed app's PackageInfo/ApplicationInfo is reported as installed (subject to package-visibility filtering) | "0" disables it | Default-on gate for consulting the cross-app installed_apps.json registry so getPackageInfo/getInstalledPackages can see other Mac.apk-installed apps; "0" is the explicit A/B opt-out. | bisect |
| MACAPK_PM_REGISTRY_FILE | Reads `~/Library/Application Support/Mac.apk/installed_apps.json` | A filesystem path to a JSON registry file | Points the installed-apps registry reader at an alternate file, so a regression test can drive a poisoned or synthetic registry without touching the user's real one. Only changes what PackageManager reports, not install authorization. | diag |
| MACAPK_PM_SELF_ONLY | ON; PackageManager truthfully reports only the running app (plus visible registry entries) as installed, and denies permission checks for other packages | "0" restores the legacy behavior | Default-on gate for AOSP-correct "not installed"/"not granted" answers about other packages; "0" restores the legacy blanket lie (every package looks installed, every permission reads GRANTED) for A/B. | bisect |
| MACAPK_USER_FOLDERS | ON: for an INSTALLED app (android root = a registry row's bundle), Boot links the volume's Pictures / Movies / Music / Download / Documents to ~/Pictures, ~/Movies, ~/Music, ~/Downloads, ~/Documents, first MOVING what the app already had there into the real folder (no clobber: the app's copy of a name the user has becomes "name (from <pkg>).ext"; journal `<root>/data/system/storage/usermap-journal.tsv`); DCIM stays a volume folder; scratch roots (dev binary, harnesses) are never mapped | "0" creates, refreshes and moves nothing (links made by an earlier launch stay) | Owner reversal 2026-09-28: the volume's collection folders ARE the Mac user's real folders. | Owner / bisect |
| MACAPK_MEDIASTORE_HOSTSCAN | ON: MediaStore's walk follows only the volume's OWN mapping links, verified by name AND target (a Java-creatable symlink elsewhere is never followed -- review 2026-09-28, AOSP scanner: no FOLLOW_LINKS), never enters a macOS package (.photoslibrary, .musiclibrary, .app, or any dir with Contents/Info.plist), walks the TCC-protected mapped Documents/Download for any app that holds All files OR legacy read OR a READ_MEDIA_* grant (not All-files only -- review 2026-09-28: AOSP's AccessChecker filters by media type, not by folder, so a plain READ_MEDIA_IMAGES app must still find its own images there), and re-walks a slow (>=250 ms) mapped tree at most every 5x its walk time | "0" restores the old walk (follows every link, enters packages, walks everything every query) | Keeps a gallery from indexing Photos' internals or raising a Documents prompt; the whole-disk-walk escape (an app-planted decoy symlink) and the "READ_MEDIA_IMAGES can't see its own Downloads photos" gap were found in review, not in the original landing. | Bisect |
| MACAPK_MEDIASTORE_ACCESS | ON: MediaStore rows follow AOSP AccessChecker -- Images/Video/Audio need READ_MEDIA_IMAGES/VIDEO/AUDIO (or READ_EXTERNAL_STORAGE where it still counts), audio without it is ringtone-style only, Files = the readable media types, Downloads needs legacy read, All files sees everything; openFile's READ half obeys the same rule, and (review 2026-09-28) its WRITE half separately requires All files or legacy WRITE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE for any write-shaped mode ("w"/"rw"/truncate/append) -- a bare READ_MEDIA_* grant used to let openFile("w") truncate the user's own real photo | "0" restores the old index-wide answer (every row to every app, every mode) | The Mac user's real photos reach an app only after the user allowed it, and only for the access it was allowed. | Bisect |
| MACAPK_PHOTOKIT | ON: MediaStore also lists the macOS Photos LIBRARY (PhotoKit via runtime/src/photos_bridge.mm, dlopen'd on first use) as read-only Images/Video rows under DCIM/Camera/, for an app holding READ_MEDIA_IMAGES/VIDEO (or a grant implying them); bytes are exported into DCIM/Camera only when a query projects _data/_size or openFile opens the row (refused at AOSP's low-storage threshold); EXTRA_SIZE thumbnails are rendered by PhotoKit; macOS's own Photos prompt is raised once, non-blocking, only from a .app with NSPhotoLibraryUsageDescription; limited access = the selected assets; denied/revoked removes every Photos row and exported copy | "0" | The =0 arm never calls the Photos source and never creates the macapk_photos table: files rows, tables and source calls are identical to the pre-change provider (debug/photos_gate.sh identity row). | behaviour |
| MACAPK_PHOTOKIT_SOURCE_DIR | Unset: the Photos backend is PhotoKit | A directory path (also -Dmacapk.photokit.source.dir) | DIAGNOSTIC: every regular file in the directory stands in for a Photos asset (optional .status = notdetermined/restricted/denied/authorized/limited, .canprompt = may prompt, .generation = a pinned library generation so the listing cache can hit), so the offline gate and a live gallery run exercise the whole MediaStore side without anybody's library and without a TCC prompt. Never set in a shipped app. | diag |
| MACAPK_LOADTHUMB_TYPED | ON: ContentResolver.loadThumbnail opens a content:// Uri the AOSP way, openTypedAssetFile(uri, "image/*", {EXTRA_SIZE: Point}) (frameworks/base ContentResolver.java:4166-4172), so a provider that renders thumbnails (MediaStore Photos rows) gets the size hint; providers that do not override openTypedAssetFile answer exactly as before | "0" | Restores the untyped openAssetFileDescriptor(uri, "r") open. | behaviour |
| MACAPK_REWRITER_PRESCAN | ON; a cheap byte-needle prescan skips the full structured class parse for classes that cannot match any rewrite pattern | "0" disables it | Rollback escape hatch that forces ClassFileRewriter to always do the full structured parse, used to verify the prescan produces byte-identical rewritten classes to the always-full-parse baseline. | bisect |
| MACAPK_REWRITER_PRESCAN_STATS | Off; no skip/full-parse tally is logged | "1" | Logs a running skip-vs-full-parse tally every 500 classes seen, to measure the prescan's skip ratio during rollout verification. | diag |
| MACAPK_RUNTIME_ROOT | The launcher resolves and self-heals the shared runtime staged at `~/Library/Application Support/Mac.apk` | A filesystem path to a runtime install root (containing Resources/macapk-runtime) | Points the launcher at an explicit alternate runtime tree instead of the normally staged/self-healing one, short-circuiting both resolveRuntime() and ensureStaged() so an A/B target is never overwritten or healed. | dev |
| MACAPK_SQLITE_JOURNAL_MODE | "delete" (WAL is coerced off process-wide) | One of delete/truncate/persist/memory/wal/off (whitelisted; invalid values fall back to delete with a warning) | Global (not per-APK) override of the SQLite journal mode used on every open and re-applied after any SQL that touches journal_mode; exists so the WAL-vs-heap-corruption question can be A/B'd without a rebuild. | bisect |
| MACAPK_SQLITE_TX_PER_THREAD | ON; each thread gets its own transaction stack, matching AOSP's SQLiteSession model | "0" restores the legacy shared stack | Bisect gate for the per-thread transaction-stack fix (which fixed an Aurora Store bug); "0" restores the old single shared TxState stack across all threads. | bisect |
| MACAPK_TRACE_CURSOR | Off | "1" | Logs each SQLiteCursorImpl step call (statement handle and sqlite3_step return code) during cursor movement, to localize cursor-iteration bugs. | diag |
| MACAPK_TRACE_DEVICEINFO | Off | "1" | Logs the real per-call answers from PackageManager's device/feature-profile queries (hasSystemFeature, getSystemAvailableFeatures, getSystemSharedLibraryNames, getSharedLibraries), added while auditing what Aurora Store's device-properties provider reads. | diag |
| MACAPK_TRACE_SQL | Off | "1" | Logs every native SQL exec/prepare call with its SQL text and return code, plus SQLiteDatabase's cursor-factory selection, to localize bugs where an INSERT appears to succeed but a table stays empty. | diag |
| MACAPK_TRACE_TX | Off | "1" | Logs transaction begin/end depth per thread and database, to trace transaction interleaving across threads. | diag |
## Resources & theme

| Flag | Default (unset) | Accepted values | Effect | Audience |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| MACAPK_ANIMATOR_INFLATE | ON: real animator/state-list-animator XML inflation runs | 0/1 (only "0" disables) | Real inflation of animator and state-list-animator XML runs instead of the old stub; =0 restores the pre-fix stub, a labelled bisect gate since real inflation can throw NotFoundException where the stub never could. | bisect |
| MACAPK_APK_PATH | per-app SDK clamp can't read the manifest; falls back to the flat DEFAULT_SDK (29) with a warning logged | a filesystem path to the running APK | Exported automatically by the native launcher so PresentedSdk can open the APK's manifest and read targetSdkVersion; without it, MACAPK_SDK_FROM_TARGET stays inert. | dev |
| MACAPK_ARSC_INDEX | (DELETED at v1.0.1590 step 9b, same day this doc was written) | -- | Until v1.0.1590 the switch between apktool's decoded text tree (`=0`, the pre-v1.0.1589 default) and the resources.arsc ingest (`=1`, default from v1.0.1589). Phase 5 step 9 retired apktool and step 9b deleted the text loaders and this gate: `ResourceIndex` ingests every installed member's resources.arsc unconditionally (an ingest failure leaves the index EMPTY with a `NativeLog.e`), so the variable is no longer read anywhere. | bisect (retired) |
| MACAPK_RES_FILES_IN_PLACE | (DELETED at v1.0.1590 step 9b, same day it was introduced) | -- | Introduced with Phase 5 step 7 as the bisect back to the decoded-tree resolver for file resources; step 9b deleted that resolver and the gate. Every file-based resource is served from the installed set's table rows (`ResourceIndex.openRaw` / `openRawByName`); the variable is no longer read anywhere. | bisect (retired) |
| MACAPK_ATTRSTYLE | ON: style="?attr"/"@style" refs resolve, plus framework EditText style rows and the underline drawable | 0/1 (only "0" disables) | Bundles three related fixes (style-ref resolution, Widget.Material.EditText rows, the edit_text_material underline) behind one switch so a fleet A/B always comes from a single binary; =0 restores the pre-lane behaviour of all three. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ATTR_NAME_RESOURCE | ON: getAttributeNameResource answers the real per-namespace attribute id | 0/1 (only "0" disables) | Lets androidx's ColorStateListInflaterCompat (and similar AttributeSet consumers) key selector rows on the real attribute id instead of always 0; =0 opts back out to the old always-0 answer. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ATTR_NS_EXACT | ON: an app-package attr slot reads only the res-auto namespace | 0/1 (only "0" disables) | Namespace-aware lookup for app-declared styleable attribute slots, matching how AOSP fills slots by attribute resource id; =0 reverts to the old fuzzy any-namespace match, a labelled bisect gate. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ATTR_RESID_THEME | ON: a copied theme cascade (via Theme.setTo) is consulted for id-keyed ?attr resolution | 0/1 (only "0" disables) | Lets a theme cascade copied by setTo answer an id-keyed attribute-reference lookup instead of dead-ending; =0 restores the pre-fix static-anchor answer, a bisect gate for a 23-app recapture rather than a supported mode. | bisect |
| MACAPK_DIAG_INFLATE_IDS | off: no logging | 1 | Logs the resource ids of every view in a subtree right after it is inflated. | diag |
| MACAPK_DRAWABLE_CACHE_TRACE | off: no logging | any non-null value | Logs every hit/miss of the per-Resources DrawableCache, for verifying or debugging the cache; never on the hot path otherwise. | diag |
| MACAPK_FW_SIMPLE_VALUES | ON (wide): getValue() reports any resolved simple type for a framework id, not just colours | 0/1 (only "0" narrows) | Widens which resolved value types (ints, bools, dimensions, fractions, floats, strings) Resources.getValue() reports for a framework resource id; =0 narrows it back to colours only, for bisecting a regression without a full revert. | bisect |
| MACAPK_FW_VIEW | ON since v1.0.1104: the platform (framework-res.apk) resolution view builds regardless of the app-table ingest gate | 0/1 (only "0" disables) | Decouples building the read-only platform-table resolution view from whether the app's own resources.arsc is being ingested, so framework style/attr lookups can hit the real platform table either way; =0 restores the pre-v1103 coupling. | bisect |
| MACAPK_NAV_BAR_SIDE | ON: navBarPosition() can report the nav bar on the left/right edge in landscape | "0" or "false" disables; anything else is on | Bisect gate for the side-navigation-bar geometry work; =0 forces navBarPosition() back to NAV_BAR_BOTTOM, restoring the exact pre-change geometry in one run. | bisect |
| MACAPK_SCREENLAYOUT_DERIVE | ON: Configuration's screenLayout size/long bits are recomputed live from current dp metrics | 0/1 (only "0" disables) | Recomputes screenLayout from the Configuration's live dp metrics the way AOSP derives it (seeded optimistically, then reduced), instead of leaving the static value; =0 is the kill switch back to the static value. | bisect |
| MACAPK_SDK_CEILING | the compiled ceiling, 36 | an SDK integer, clamped to [29, MAX_KNOWN_SDK=37] | Overrides the compiled ceiling on the per-app targetSdk clamp without a rebuild, so each candidate ceiling can be A/B'd in one run instead of a rebuild-plus-sweep cycle. | experiment |
| MACAPK_SDK_FROM_TARGET | ON since v1.0.1012: presented SDK = clamp(targetSdkVersion, FLOOR, CEILING) per app | 0/1 (only "0" disables) | Presents each app the highest Android version its own targetSdkVersion supports instead of a flat level; =0 disables the per-app clamp and restores the flat DEFAULT_SDK (29) for every app. | bisect |
| MACAPK_SDK_INT | unset: the level comes from MACAPK_SDK_FROM_TARGET's clamp (or the flat default) | an SDK integer in AOSP's [1,10000], capped at MAX_KNOWN_SDK=37 | Absolute override of the presented Android version that wins over every other gate, used to force one specific API level per run for testing. | experiment |
| MACAPK_SKIP_ALL_IMAGE_DRAW | off: ImageView draws normally | 1 | Skips onDraw entirely for every ImageView, a diagnostic for isolating whether an ImageView's own drawing is causing a rendering problem. | diag |
| MACAPK_SKIP_IMAGEVIEW_DRAW | off: no class is skipped | a simple class name (e.g. "FeatureImage") | Skips onDraw only for ImageView subclasses whose simple class name matches the given string, a narrower version of MACAPK_SKIP_ALL_IMAGE_DRAW for isolating one widget type. | diag |
| MACAPK_STATUSBAR_BLEND | the status/nav bar uses the AOSP DecorView.calculateBarColor path; the retired blend never fires | 0/1 | =1 restores the retired behaviour of blending the bar with the sampled colour of the content row beneath it (the sense flipped from opt-out to opt-in once measurement showed the blend path never actually fired on the fleet); =0 explicitly forces the plain colorBackground strip. | bisect |
| MACAPK_STYLE_PARENT_EMPTY | an explicit parent="" on a style suppresses dotted-name parent inference (the aapt2-correct behaviour), on the text-decoded ingest path | 0/1 (only "0" disables) | A text-decode repair knob; =0 disables the parent="" suppression so a dotted style name always infers a parent, restoring the older (aapt2-incorrect) behaviour for bisecting a regression. | bisect |
| MACAPK_TEXT_SP | ON: TextView's default text size is the density-scaled equivalent of 14sp | 0/1 (only "0" disables) | Scales TextView's default text size to match AOSP's 14sp-equivalent default instead of a static raw-pixel value; =0 reverts to the old static 14f raw-px default so the fix can be A/B'd. | bisect |
| MACAPK_THEME_STRING_FALLBACK | ON since v1.0.920: getString()/getText() can resolve a theme-only string attribute through the Theme's cascade | 0/1 (only "0" disables) | Lets a validated theme-cascade lookup answer getString()/getText() when no XML/style/defStyle value exists (e.g. a manifest theme's viewInflaterClass string); =0 opts out, a labelled bisect gate. | bisect |
| MACAPK_THEME_TIER_WINDOWBG | off: windowBackground/android:windowBackground are excluded from the theme-tier serve | 1 (only value that re-enables) | Re-enables the theme tier's "honest" serve of windowBackground for parity work; left off by default because it broke unitconverter's dark Help dialog (AppCompat wants a darkened version we don't implement). | experiment |
| MACAPK_THEME_TIER_XML | ON: an XML-inflated TypedArray also consults the theme as tier 5 | 0/1 (only "0" disables) | Extends AOSP's five-tier attribute resolution (theme as tier 5) to the XML-inflation path, which previously stopped at tier 4; =0 reverts to the tier-4 stop, a labelled bisect gate for the fleet measurement. | bisect |
| MACAPK_TRACE_ATTR | off: no per-attribute tier tracing | an attribute name (e.g. "textAppearance") | Prints every resolution tier's answer for one named attribute on every TypedArray parse, added to find "which tier failed" while diagnosing a Material ThemeEnforcement throw. | diag |
| MACAPK_TRACE_ATTRCOLOR | off | 1 | Logs which resolution tier answered a ?attr colour reference and with what value, to tell the XML-drawable path apart from the View/TypedArray path when they disagree. | diag |
| MACAPK_TRACE_BARCOLOR | off | any non-null value | Logs the computed status/nav bar flags, declared colour, contrast-scrim flag, and the final resolved bar colour. | diag |
| MACAPK_TRACE_CL_PARAMS | off | 1 | Logs each ConstraintLayout LayoutParams field as it is set from a parsed attribute, naming the target id and whether the set succeeded. | diag |
| MACAPK_TRACE_CSL_INFLATE | off | any non-null value | Logs whether Mac.apk's own ColorStateList inflater or the app's bundled androidx inflater actually ran for a res/color selector, the load-bearing question for the state-list un-flattening work. | diag |
| MACAPK_TRACE_DEFSTYLE_APPLY | off | 1 | Prints which defStyleAttr/style resolved for a 4-arg TypedArray parse, used to localise widgets that get no default style via the fleet's INK IMBALANCE column. | diag |
| MACAPK_TRACE_FEATUREIMG | off | 1 | Logs bitmap-apply and draw details for ImageView subclasses whose simple name contains "FeatureImage", tracing Glide's rescue image-loading path. | diag |
| MACAPK_TRACE_FWSTYLE | off | 1 | Logs which style names the platform (framework-res.apk) resolution table answers that the app's own table did not, naming the exact seam where MACAPK_FW_VIEW changes behaviour. | diag |
| MACAPK_TRACE_GETVALUE | off | any non-null value | Logs the id and resolved type/name whenever Resources.getValue() can't resolve a path, e.g. to find exactly which drawable resource an app failed to load. | diag |
| MACAPK_TRACE_GUIDE | off | any non-null value | Diagnostic tracing and tree-dump accessors for a ConstraintLayout guideline and related fill/tint/stroke drawable state, used to see why a filled shape isn't painting. | diag |
| MACAPK_TRACE_IMAGEVIEW_DRAW | off | "1" (one-shot per instance) or "verbose" (every draw) | Traces ImageView.onDraw for the non-bitmap-drawable path; verbose mode catches double-traversal of the same instance within one frame that one-shot mode would hide. | diag |
| MACAPK_TRACE_IMAGEVIEW_MEASURE | off | 1 | Logs an ImageView's onMeasure spec modes/sizes, intrinsic size, and resulting output size. | diag |
| MACAPK_TRACE_IMAGEVIEW_SET | off | 1 | Logs an ImageView's bounds, drawable class, and extracted bitmap size at the moment setImageDrawable installs a new drawable. | diag |
| MACAPK_TRACE_LAYOUTREQ | off | 1 | Dumps each inflated view's isLayoutRequested() state and child count at the end of inflate(), to tell whether a ConstraintLayout's children lost that flag before or after inflation (which decides if the constraint solver ever registers them). | diag |
| MACAPK_TRACE_MTLBTN | off | any non-null value | Logs a Material button's element/style-declared backgroundTint, the value chosen, its resolved colour, and the theme-overlay container colour while inflating that widget. | diag |
| MACAPK_TRACE_RESOLVEATTR | off | an attribute name, or "*" | Traces Theme.resolveAttribute, a separate path from TypedArray that androidx's SplashScreen handoff uses; a silent false there strands an app on its splash theme for the whole session. | diag |
