| MACAPK_TRACE_RL | off | 1 | Logs each RelativeLayout child's resolved rules, margins, and measured size during the first measure pass. | diag |
| MACAPK_TRACE_TA | off | any non-null value | Logs how many of a large (>40-attribute) styleable's attributes actually resolved on a TypedArray parse. | diag |
| MACAPK_TRACE_THEMEPROBE | off | any non-null value | Logs every theme-only getString() answer (attribute name and resolved value) once macapkThemeString has validated it. | diag |
| MACAPK_TRACE_TINT | off (zero cost unset) | any non-null value | Logs the effective resolved tint colour applied to an ImageView's drawable, so a wrong icon colour names its resolved ARGB instead of being guessed from a screenshot. | diag |
| MACAPK_TRACE_WINDOW_FLAGS | off | any non-null value (some call sites also require it to equal "1") | Traces window-flag/style-attribute resolution across several call sites (Theme.applyStyle, resolveAttrRaw, AlertDialog, Button's default-style probe), naming which applied style answered a window* attribute. | diag |
| MACAPK_TRACE_XMLPULL | off | any non-null value | Dumps the XmlPullParser event stream (event type, name, depth) as a consumer's parse loop sees it. | diag |
| MACAPK_TW_TRACE | off | any non-null value | Logs every addTextChangedListener call on a TextView, naming the watcher's class, and each watcher-firing setText; an EDITOR's text in that line is `<redacted:N>` unless MACAPK_TRACE_KEYS=1, a password field's always (KeyLogPolicy.viewText). | diag |
| MACAPK_VIEW_THEME | ON since 2026-08-01: ContextThemeWrapper (and Activity) owns and applies its own theme cascade | "0" or "false" disables; anything else is on | The single gate for View-level theming across the whole corpus (android:theme on a layout element, Theme.setTo actually copying a cascade); with it on, getTheme() returns the wrapper's own theme instead of forwarding to the base context. =0 reverts to the old pass-through. | bisect |
| MACAPK_WINDOW_BOX | ON: the ANGLE render layer, SurfaceView renderer surface size, and renderer-direct touch are all confined to the app-window box | "0" or "false" disables; anything else is on | Bisect gate for the v1.0.996 window-box work; =0 restores the pre-change behaviour where GL surfaces get the full display drawable and the nav strip composites over the app's own bottom rows. | bisect |
| MACAPK_WINDOW_FLAGS_FROM_THEME | ON since v1.0.894: the app's real theme decides windowNoTitle/windowActionBar/etc. | 0/1 (only "0" disables) | Reads the theme (not just the manifest style name) for window* attributes, which is what lets AppCompat pick the decor action-bar screen layout it should; =0 opts out, a labelled bisect gate. | bisect |
| MACAPK_XML_DRAWABLE | ON: getDrawable() builds a real drawable for <layer-list>/<shape>/<selector> roots | 0/1 (only "0" disables) | Makes XML drawables other than <vector> actually paint, a strictly additive change; =0 disables it, reverting to the old empty-VectorDrawable-stub behaviour. | bisect |

**Note on MACAPK_ANDROID_ROOT** (appears in this table via the Install & storage agent, corrected here): its declaration comment at the source ("cli value > MACAPK_ANDROID_ROOT env > $HOME/Documents/android > ./android") is itself stale. The ACTUAL implementation, `ResolveAndroidRootDir()` in the source, resolves to `$HOME/Library/Caches/Mac.apk/scratch/android` when both the CLI flag and the env var are unset (moved off `~/Documents/android` on 2026-08-12; see the comment at the source for why -- an installed app never reaches this default at all, since its stub always passes an explicit `--android-root <bundle>/Contents/android`, so this default only matters for the bare dev `macapk-runtime --apk X` invocation). the source's comment agrees with the code; `config.h`'s does not. Trust the implementation over either comment.
## Rendering & GLES/Metal

| Flag | Default (unset) | Accepted values | Effect | Audience |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| MACAPK_AGDK_TRACE | Off -- no log of GameActivity's OnGlobalLayoutListener registration/dispatch | `1` | Confirms which ViewTreeObserver instance receives GameActivity's global-layout listener and that it keeps firing, so a boot stall waiting on APP_CMD_CONTENT_RECT_CHANGED can be localized. | diag |
| MACAPK_ANGLE_BACKEND | Metal backend (or auto-detected Vulkan when the guest needs GLES 3.1) | `metal` or `vulkan` | Selects ANGLE's rendering backend; `vulkan` forces creation through eglGetPlatformDisplayEXT with EGL_PLATFORM_ANGLE_TYPE_VULKAN_ANGLE so RenderDragon-class engines get a real GLES 3.1 context via MoltenVK. | user |
| MACAPK_ANGLE_BLOB_CACHE | On -- ANGLE shader/program blob cache enabled when a cache dir resolves | `0` | Disables the ANGLE shader-compile blob cache entirely, so every shader recompiles from source each run instead of being served from disk. | user |
| MACAPK_ANGLE_BLOB_DIR | `<MACAPK_APP_CACHE_DIR>/.angle_blob` | a directory path | Overrides where the per-ANGLE-build shader blob cache is stored on disk. | user |
| MACAPK_ANGLE_BLOB_MAX_MB | 256 | an integer (MB) | Sets the disk-space budget for the ANGLE blob cache directory. | user |
| MACAPK_ANGLE_BLOB_TRACE | Off -- no per-blob cache log lines | `1` | Logs each individual shader-blob cache put/get. | diag |
| MACAPK_BG_NULL_CLEARS_COLOR | On -- `setBackground(null)` also clears the tracked background color | `0` | Reverts to the pre-v1.0.923 behavior where clearing a View's background drawable left its separate background-color field untouched. | bisect |
| MACAPK_BITMAP_ARGB_VIEW | On (2026-09-29) -- Canvas draws an argb-backed Bitmap (no AWT backing: BitmapFactory decodes, AndroidBitmap_lockPixels writes) from a zero-copy TYPE_INT_ARGB view of its own int[] | `0` | Restores the per-draw BufferedImage + setRGB copy (~32 ms per draw of a 1636x3362 bitmap vs 4 us; output byte-identical, debug/argbview_gate.sh). Telegram MotionBackgroundDrawable wallpaper. Kept as a bisect arm (not deleted) because the draw source now ALIASES the bitmap's live pixels instead of a per-draw snapshot: a future caller that WRITES into the image `Canvas.bitmapAsImage` returns would write the Bitmap, and `=0` is the one-line way to tell that apart from a real draw bug. Delete the flag once the aliasing has shipped a few versions clean. | bisect |
| MACAPK_BITMAP_SCALE_FAST | On (2026-10-01) -- a FILTERED drawBitmap under a positive axis-aligned scale (Rect and scale+translate Matrix overloads; translation-only CTM, rectangular clip, stock ARGB source and target, SrcOver or a BlendComposite) is drawn by `Canvas.macapkScaledBlit`: one separable bilinear pass into the target int[] with Java2D's own arithmetic (8-bit weights from 32.32 coordinates, IntArgbPre interpolation, edge clamp) | `0` | Restores Java2D's generic TransformHelper + per-tile BlendComposite pipe. Output agrees within 2/255 (mostly byte-identical: debug/bitmapscale_gate.sh). Telegram tablet chat list 131-139 -> 37-53 ms per frame (load-dependent; final build 49-53 at load 14, 37-47 at load 3; fin_A1/fin_B1 and fix_on1/off1/on2 logs in lanes21/telegram-animation-perf). A real behaviour change (pixels may differ by 1/255), hence the bisect arm. | bisect |
| MACAPK_BITMAP_SCALE_THREADS | Half the cores, at most 4 -- a scaled blit of >= 256K destination pixels is split into row bands on a small daemon pool (`macapk-raster-N`); the draw thread runs one band | integer; `1` = serial | Output is identical for any band count (per-pixel arithmetic, disjoint rows). Live Telegram: pattern 52 -> 21 ms per draw with 4 vs 1 (2026-10-01, a loaded box). | tuning |
| MACAPK_BLOB_MADVISE | On -- fully-covered interior pages of a loaded Stage-6 blob get anonymous-remapped (discarded) | `0` | Falls back to a plain MADV_DONTNEED deactivation instead of replacing the blob's interior pages with anonymous PROT_READ memory after load. | bisect |
| MACAPK_CARD_CLIP | On -- a CardView's children are clipped to its rounded outline before dispatchDraw | `0` | Disables the save/clip-to-rounded-outline applied around a CardView's children. | bisect |
| MACAPK_CARD_GAP_PX | 16 (px, 8dp at density 2) | a non-negative integer (px) | Sets the inter-card gap used by the manual grid-layout fallback for RecyclerView grids, for A/B testing the spacing. | bisect |
| MACAPK_CHOREO_THREADDIAG | Off -- no thread-identity log for the Compose window recomposer's Choreographer | any value (presence check) | Confirms which thread a Compose AndroidUiFrameClock's Choreographer lives on and whether its postFrameCallback takes the drained main-vsync path or the dead per-thread timer path. | diag |
| MACAPK_CHOREO_TIME | Off (MACAPK_UHOOK no longer turns it on: that coupling went with the il2cpp diag kit, 2026-09-27) | any value (presence check) | Logs the frameTimeNanos delta Unity actually receives each frame, since Unity derives Time.deltaTime from it. | diag |
| MACAPK_CHOREO_NANOTIME_BASE | On (since 2026-09-27) -- every Choreographer frame time (doFrame, onVsync FrameData, getFrameTimeNanos, the main-loop drain, expected presentation time) is in the System.nanoTime() timebase, AOSP's contract. MEASURED on Crossy: unscaledDeltaTime correct (0 clamp-floor hits), age gate fine; RESOLVED (2026-09-28, MACAPK_GUEST_CLOCK_NANOTIME): Time.unscaledTime ~ host uptime for the Crossy family (Unity 6000) is fixed by unifying the guest clock, not by this flag -- see docs/guest-clock-one-monotonic-base_2026-09-28.md | `0` | Restores the old process-relative rebasedNanos() frame clock. Also decides the default of MACAPK_SWAPPY_BRIDGE (installed only under =0), and through the bridge the default of MACAPK_FRAME_PACING_CHOREO_KEY (old key). ⚠ `=0` ALONE IS NOT THE OLD REGIME: the per-thread Choreographer stays vsync-driven (a mixed regime), AND (since 2026-09-28) the guest clock stays unrebased/UPTIME_RAW under MACAPK_GUEST_CLOCK_NANOTIME's separate default -- so `=0` alone now mixes an unrebased guest CLOCK_MONOTONIC against process-relative frame times, disagreeing by the host uptime with the opposite sign from the pre-2026-09-28 defect. The full old regime is `MACAPK_CHOREO_VSYNC_THREADS=0 MACAPK_CHOREO_NANOTIME_BASE=0 MACAPK_GUEST_CLOCK_NANOTIME=0`. See docs/unity-choreographer-vsync-nanotime-contract_2026-09-27.md section 4 for the Crossy-family clock measurement. | bisect |
| MACAPK_CHOREO_VSYNC_THREADS | On (since 2026-09-27) -- a per-thread Choreographer (prepared Looper, e.g. Unity's "UnityChoreographer") schedules ONE display vsync on its thread's native looper and runs doFrame when the drive tick delivers it, AOSP's FrameDisplayEventReceiver shape | `0` | Restores the old Handler.postDelayed(one refresh interval) timer per callback (not vsync). Needs the runtime's soft-registered NativeLooper.scheduleVsync/takeVsync; without them the timer is kept automatically. | bisect |
| MACAPK_CLIP_CHILDREN | On -- ViewGroup clips overflowing children to its own bounds in dispatchDraw | `0` | Disables the per-child clip so children positioned past a ViewGroup's edge (e.g. an overflowing RecyclerView card) are no longer bounded, restoring the pre-v1.0.129 behavior. | bisect |
| MACAPK_CLIP_TO_PADDING | ON (2026-09-30): ViewGroup.dispatchDraw clips children to the padding rect when clipToPadding is on and some padding is non-zero (AOSP CLIP_TO_PADDING_MASK), and the ViewGroup constructor reads android:clipChildren / android:clipToPadding from XML and styles through the virtual setters (AOSP initFromAttributes) | `0` | `=0` restores the pre-change framework: no padding clip, and both XML attributes ignored (clipChildren="false" groups still clipped their children). Gate: `debug/cliptopad_gate.sh`. | bisect |
| MACAPK_CLIP_OUTLINE | On -- `clipToOutline` + a round-rect provider clips the view during layout() | `0` | Reverts the outline-clip short-circuit so clipToOutline views (e.g. circular icon buttons) stop being clipped, painting a full square instead. | bisect |
| MACAPK_COMPOSE_FALLBACK | On -- the Compose text/tree overpaint fallback runs | `0` | Disables the ComposeViewFallback overpaint mechanism entirely. | bisect |
| MACAPK_DEBUG_DUMPFRAME | Off -- no frame dumps | a comma-separated list of frame numbers (or one number) | glReadPixels the frame at each listed frame number and writes a raw PPM to /tmp, for headless visual diagnosis when screen capture isn't available. | diag |
| MACAPK_DIRECTOR_OFF | RETIRED 2026-09-27: ignored by default (MACAPK_REGISTERNATIVES_VERBATIM); under =0, on -- the per-frame Unity DirectorManager update tick runs | any value (presence check), read only under MACAPK_REGISTERNATIVES_VERBATIM=0 | Under MACAPK_REGISTERNATIVES_VERBATIM=0 only: disables the per-frame DirectorManager update driver for A/B isolation. By default nothing calls the driver (the engine's own player loop runs DirectorManager::Update; the v1.0.233 premise was refuted by the v1.0.251 frida and v1.0.322 census measurements). | bisect |
| MACAPK_DIRECTOR_RUNNING_SCENE_OFF | 0x94 | a hex/decimal integer (byte offset) | Overrides the CCDirector field offset used to read the running-scene pointer for the scene-tree/touch diagnostics. | diag |
| MACAPK_DIRECTOR_VADDR | 0 -- feature inert, no guest memory touched | a hex/decimal guest vaddr | Supplies the per-build CCDirector instance address the shim's scene-tree dumper and touch diagnostic walk from (bionic_shim.cpp only; the runtime's MACAPK_DUMP_DIRECTOR reader was RETIRED 2026-09-27); deliberately has no baked-in default so it can never fire on the wrong binary. In a cocos2d-x <= 2.0.3 build (HCR 1.8.1 is 2.0-rc2-x-2.0.1) the address is the static .bss director object itself; in a 2.0.4+ build it holds a heap pointer, so pass the pointed-to value (read it with MACAPK_POLL_GUEST) -- only valid if that pointer value is identical across runs; read it on two runs and compare before trusting it. | diag |
| MACAPK_DISPATCH_TOP_INSET | 0 -- dispatched top inset is always 0 | `1` | Dispatches the real status-bar height as the WindowInsets top inset instead of always 0; measured a no-op for F-Droid since the AppBarLayout inset machinery doesn't consume it. | dev |
| MACAPK_DRAW_LETTER_SPACING | On -- letter-spaced text draws as separately-advanced glyph runs | `0` | Reverts to drawing text as one unspaced AWT run, letting a fleet delta be attributed to this change specifically. | bisect |
| MACAPK_DUMP_BITMAP_AT | Off -- no PNG dump | a frame number, or `0` for every frame | Writes the current canvas bitmap to `/tmp/macapk_frame_<N>.png` (plus a downscaled `_chat.png` companion) for visual verification. | diag |
| MACAPK_DUMP_CL_WIDGETS | Off -- no widget-graph dump | any value (presence check) | Dumps every ConstraintLayout's solved widget graph (position/size/constraint targets) at the checkpoint dump frames. | diag |
| MACAPK_DUMP_MOTION | Off -- no MotionLayout dump | any value (presence check) | At frame 60, reports each MotionLayout ConstraintSet's constraint-map size to show whether ConstraintSet.readFallback populated it. | diag |
| MACAPK_DUMP_SLOTS | Off -- no Compose SlotTable/navstack verbose dump | `1` | Decodes and dumps Compose composition SlotTables (per-group class names) plus the Reddit navstack entry-visibility probe. | diag |
| MACAPK_DUMP_TREE_EVERY | View-tree dump only at the fixed checkpoint frames (1/60/300) | an integer N (frames) | Also dumps the full view tree every N frames, for inspecting screens reached long after boot (e.g. after a tap navigates deeper). | diag |
| MACAPK_EDGE_TO_EDGE | Off -- legacy SYSTEM_UI_FLAG edge-to-edge bits are never synthesized | `1` | Synthesizes SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_LAYOUT_HIDE_NAVIGATION/FULLSCREEN on the legacy systemUiVisibility path; kept opt-in because enabling it by default previously measured a catastrophic regression. | dev |
| MACAPK_EGL_LAYER_IDLE_HIDE | Off -- an idle guest EGL layer is still re-asserted visible | `1` | Hides the guest's native EGL layer instead of forcing it visible when it hasn't presented a drawable recently, so it stops occluding the framework canvas underneath. | bisect |
| MACAPK_EGL_LAYER_IDLE_SECS | 2.0 (clamped 0.2-30.0) | a float (seconds) | Sets how long the guest EGL layer must be idle before MACAPK_EGL_LAYER_IDLE_HIDE hides it. | dev |
| MACAPK_FAIR_SWAPPY_LATCH | On -- the mutex-fairness boot window latches closed once the guest is in a vsync-paced Unity loop | `0` | Reverts to the pure wall-clock 90s fairness boot window. Since 2026-09-27 (Swappy bridge not installed by default) the latch closes at the first display vsync a per-thread Java Choreographer takes while libunity is loaded (Unity's UnityChoreographer; never MCPE), or on the bridge tick under MACAPK_SWAPPY_BRIDGE=1. 2026-09-29: inert under the retired default; applies only under `MACAPK_FAIR_BOOT_RETIRED=0` or an explicit `MACAPK_FAIR_BOOT_MS` (docs/mutex-fair-boot-window-retired_2026-09-29.md). | bisect |
| MACAPK_FAIR_LATCH_CHOREO | Off -- the MUTEX_FAIR boot latch closes on the first per-thread Java Choreographer vsync only while libunity is loaded (see MACAPK_FAIR_SWAPPY_LATCH) | `1` | Opt-in generic key for an MCPE boot A/B: drop the libunity condition, so any app's first per-thread Java Choreographer vsync closes the latch. Not the default until MCPE is measured (docs/refuted-approaches.md, present-rate key 0/8). 2026-09-29: inert under the retired default; applies only under `MACAPK_FAIR_BOOT_RETIRED=0` or an explicit `MACAPK_FAIR_BOOT_MS` (docs/mutex-fair-boot-window-retired_2026-09-29.md). | experiment |
| MACAPK_FILTER_BITMAP | On -- bitmap blits use bilinear filtering when Paint.isFilterBitmap() | `0` | Restores nearest-neighbour filtering everywhere so the fleet effect of bilinear filtering can be A/B'd. | bisect |
| MACAPK_FONT_REAL_STYLE | On -- real per-style face lookup is used | `0` | Falls back to serving every text request from one bundled Roboto-Regular face with AWT's algorithmic bold/italic, while still keeping family lookup for Typeface weight. | bisect |
| MACAPK_FORCE_REMEASURE | Off -- no forced remeasure | `1` | Forces every attached AndroidComposeView's root LayoutNode to re-measure+re-place each frame, confirming whether a stalled Compose animation is a missed requestRemeasure. | diag |
| MACAPK_FORCE_REMEASURE_MODE | Only the root LayoutNode is marked dirty | `all` | Marks every LayoutNode in the subtree dirty instead of just the root, so deep animating nodes aren't skipped. | diag |
| MACAPK_FRAMEWORK_PRESENT_WHEN_GUEST_IDLE | Off -- the framework canvas present stays skipped once any guest EGL surface goes live | `1` | Resumes presenting the framework's own canvas when the guest's EGL surface is live but has stopped actually producing frames, so an app whose real UI is drawn by ViewCanvasDriver (not the guest engine) isn't left blank. | dev |
| MACAPK_FRAME_DT | Off -- no per-frame dt trace | any value (presence check) | Logs any frame whose delta exceeds a threshold (a dropped-frame hitch) plus a 5s worst-dt summary, since the 5s-averaged fps line hides single-frame hitches. | diag |
| MACAPK_FRAME_DT_MS | 20 (ms) | a float (ms) | Sets the per-frame delta threshold that counts as a "hitch" for MACAPK_FRAME_DT. | diag |
| MACAPK_FRAME_INTERVAL | 0 -- the auto-swap-interval ratchet decides | an integer 1-8 | Pins the Swappy-shaped swap interval (1=locked 60fps, 2=locked 30fps) and disables the ratchet, isolating "the grid quantizer works" from "the ratchet picked the right interval". | diag |
| MACAPK_FRAME_PACING | On -- the Swappy-shaped frame-pacing grid wait is active | `0` | Disables the frame-pacing grid wait outright, which otherwise blocks eglSwapBuffers until the pacing grid allows the next frame. | bisect |
| MACAPK_FRAME_PACING_CHOREO_KEY | unset: FOLLOWS MACAPK_SWAPPY_BRIDGE (since 2026-09-27) -- the old key while the bridge is installed; otherwise (the default) FramePacer engages when a per-thread Java Choreographer takes a display vsync WHILE LIBUNITY IS LOADED (the shim is told once per display tick), and stamps the render start at every return of a cond_wait on a condvar that such a thread's vsync callback signalled (the old key's stamp, found from the platform instead of the bridge's shape probe; NOT the vsync itself, which made Crossy's ratchet flap 1<->3) | `0` / `1` / `generic` | `0` forces the old key: engagement on a published SwappyCommon (g_swappy_gen_mutex) and the stamp at a cond_wait return on its M+0x28 (with the bridge off that key never fires). `1` (any other non-empty value) forces the choreo key, libunity only. `generic` drops the libunity condition, so an ad SDK's ExoPlayer frame-release Choreographer on a cocos game engages Swappy's interval ratchet -- opt-in only, real Android never paces a non-Swappy app. Gate: debug/geh_unity-swappy-frame-pacing-bridge_gate.sh row R3. | bisect |
| MACAPK_FRAME_PHASE_TRACE | Off -- no per-phase timing trace | `1` | Traces measure/layout/draw phase timings within a single traversal, at zero cost when unset. | diag |
| MACAPK_FRAME_SCORE_EVERY | 120 | an integer (0-999999) | Sets the frame cadence at which the self-capture harness scores the presented frame. | diag |
| MACAPK_FRAME_STATS | Off -- no guest-present pacing probe | `1` | Logs the guest's real presents-per-second (and worst/min frame delta) at the one shim every guest-owned EGL present passes through, to diagnose an engine running proportionally too fast. | diag |
| MACAPK_GLES1_CONTEXT | Decided by the runtime's own scan of the guest's imported GLES1 symbols | `0` or `1` | Overrides the auto-detected choice between an ES1 and ES2 EGL context, which are mutually exclusive. | dev |
| MACAPK_GLES_TEXIMAGE_PASSTHROUGH | On -- Java GLES20/GLES10/GL10 glTexImage2D(Buffer) calls glTexImage2D verbatim, like AOSP's android_opengl_GLES20.cpp glue | `0` | Restores the pre-v1.0.1655 substitution (MIN=LINEAR for any *_MIPMAP_* filter, WRAP=CLAMP_TO_EDGE for any REPEAT, before every upload), byte for byte. Pair it with MACAPK_GL_OFF=texparam/texfilter/texwrap to reproduce pre-v682 rendering, since those groups now drop the app's filters with no safety net. Gate: `debug/gates.sh glteximage`; note docs/gles20-teximage-param-substitution-retired_2026-09-27.md. | bisect |
| MACAPK_GLTHREAD_DRAIN | On -- a dead GLSurfaceView render thread's queued Runnables are still drained on the main thread | `0` | Reverts the drain-on-death behavior for an A/B. | bisect |
| MACAPK_GLTHREAD_DRAIN_EMPTY | ON: the forked-GLSurfaceView queue drain (SurfaceView.macapkDrainForkedGlQueue) runs EVERY pending event before the draw, including events a running event queues, and re-reads the view's GL-thread field each frame so a re-attached successor's queue is drained too | "0" restores the v1.0.834 drain token for token (macapkDrainForkedGlQueueOnePerFrame: resolve once, ONE event per frame) | AOSP GLThread.guardedRun takes an event, runs it and `continue`s to the loop head without drawing (android14 GLSurfaceView.java:1330-1331, :1501-1504; HCR 1.8.1's own FSGLSurfaceView$GLThread.guardedRun bci 425-515 -> goto 368), so every queued event runs before the next draw; one-per-frame built a backlog of stale touch MOVE/UP runnables on HCR. Also the prerequisite for MACAPK_TOUCH_APP_QUEUE_ONLY (=0 here switches that off too). Safety valve: 4096 runnables per frame, one WARN line. Java-only. Offline gate: `debug/geh_cocos2dx-touch-renderer-direct_gate.sh`; `docs/cocos2dx-touch-renderer-direct-retired_2026-09-27.md`. | bisect |
| MACAPK_GLTHREAD_DRAIN_TRACE | Off: "1" logs every runnable the forked-queue drain runs (`forked GL-thread queue: ran #N <class> (thread ... dead, queue left=K)`), not just #1/#10/#100, each with the runnable's own non-static int/float/int[]/float[] field values appended (` fields: val$xDown=..`), and lifts TouchDispatcher's 64-line LOG_CAP on `renderer-direct ...` lines | "1" enables | Diagnostic for the touch A/B: counts app-queued touch runnables (HCR: Cocos2dxGLSurfaceView$6..$11) one to one against `renderer-direct handleAction*` lines. Default-path drain only; changes no behaviour. `debug/geh_cocos2dx-touch-renderer-direct_gate.sh --log FILE` does the counting. | diagnostic |
| MACAPK_TOUCH_APP_QUEUE_ONLY | ON: TouchDispatcher's renderer-direct call (handleActionDown/Up/Move reflected onto a cocos2d-x-shaped renderer) is skipped, per event, while the bound host is a vendored GLSurfaceView fork whose CURRENT GL thread is TERMINATED, not an EGL survivor, bound by the drain, and the drain still reached within one frame (SurfaceView.macapkForkGlQueueServiced): the app's own onTouchEvent -> queueEvent already delivers the touch, as on Android | "0" restores the v1.0.1639 rule (a fork host always gets the direct call as well) | Every HCR touch was delivered TWICE (renderer-direct plus the drained Cocos2dxGLSurfaceView$7: hcr_run.log 2026-09-25 :46444-46445). Direct delivery still runs where nothing drains the queue: a live or survivor GL thread (HCR's first seconds), a re-attach the drain has not re-bound yet, MACAPK_GLTHREAD_DRAIN=0 or MACAPK_GLTHREAD_DRAIN_EMPTY=0. One `renderer-direct: SUPPRESSED` / `delivering` line per change of answer. Java-only. Offline gate: `debug/geh_cocos2dx-touch-renderer-direct_gate.sh`. | bisect |
| MACAPK_SV_RESUME_ONCE | ON: a reflected renderer (a vendored GLSurfaceView fork, e.g. cocos2d-x v2 on HCR's FSGLSurfaceView) is NOT re-sent `handleOnResume()` after its first onSurfaceCreated; the app's own queued call is delivered once, by the fork-queue drain | "0" restores the old re-fire (`re-fired handleOnResume after surface ready`), code token for token | Retires an engine-shaped lifecycle hook (c20c9434) whose premise was false: AOSP GLThread.guardedRun runs a queued event before any readiness test, so on a device the first-launch handleOnResume runs before the surface and nativeOnResume's getOpenGLView() guard makes it a no-op. Since v1.0.834 MACAPK_GLTHREAD_DRAIN already delivers the app's own call after the surface, so with the re-fire every HCR build got handleOnResume (and applicationWillEnterForeground) TWICE. Interplay: MACAPK_GLTHREAD_DRAIN=0 with this ON means no post-surface handleOnResume at all, a device's first-launch behaviour. Java-only (no cache to clear). Offline gate: `debug/geh_cocos2dx-handleOnResume-refire_gate.sh`; evidence `docs/cocos2dx-handleonresume-refire-retired_2026-09-27.md`. | bisect |
| MACAPK_GL_EMPTY_EXT | Off -- glGetString(GL_EXTENSIONS) returns ANGLE's real extension string | `1` | Restores the pre-v682 behavior of reporting an empty GL_EXTENSIONS string, to test whether an engine's extension-driven feature probing caused a regression. | diag |
| MACAPK_GL_OFF | Every v682 GLES bridge group is active | a comma-separated list of group names (state/stencil/fbo/del/uniform/tex/query) | Makes the named GLES bridge groups behave like their pre-v682 empty-body stubs, so a rendering regression can be bisected at runtime without a rebuild. Since v1.0.1655 texparam/texfilter/texwrap leave textures at the GLES defaults (black when unmipmapped) because glTexImage2D no longer substitutes; add MACAPK_GLES_TEXIMAGE_PASSTHROUGH=0 for the pre-v682 picture. | bisect |
| MACAPK_GL_RENDERER_THROW_FATAL | Off -- a renderer that threw is still called again next frame | `1` | Adopts AOSP's "GLThread is dead" semantics: after a renderer's first uncaught throw, it is marked dead and never driven again. | dev |
| MACAPK_GL_THREAD_NO_LOOPER | On (2026-10-01) -- while a GLSurfaceView renderer callback runs (onSurfaceCreated / onSurfaceChanged / onDrawFrame, each queueEvent runnable, and the reflected SurfaceView renderer path incl. the app fork's GLThread queue) the thread is the app's Looper-less GLThread, as AOSP's GLSurfaceView.GLThread (never Looper.prepare()d): Looper.myLooper() is null (or the Looper the app prepare()d there), `new Handler()` / `new Handler(Callback)`, every Dialog (ctor and show()) and MacApkWindowManager.addView / WindowManagerGlobal.addView throw AOSP's "Can't create handler inside thread ... that has not called Looper.prepare()" with a `Looper: GL-THREAD-NO-LOOPER ... app=<frame>` line, `new Toast` throws AOSP's NullPointerException (Toast.getLooper), and runOnUiThread/PlayCore post instead of running inline. Jetpack Joyride's billing fallback no longer shows its QUIT dialog | `0` | myLooper() answers MAIN inside renderer callbacks again, implicit Handlers bind the main Looper, Dialog/addView/Toast make no demand, queueEvent runnables are enqueued unwrapped: the pre-change path (the Overlay log keeps its new thread=/gl=/app= fields). docs/gl-thread-has-no-looper_2026-10-01.md; gate debug/glthread_gate.sh (C1 is this arm) | bisect |
| MACAPK_GL_WAIT_FRAME | On -- a renderer's FIRST onSurfaceCreated (setRenderer path and the reflected path) waits for the view's first layout, AOSP mHaveFrame; a bare content root (libGDX/Arc) lays itself out at the renderer size on the owner thread; a view nothing lays out starts after `MACAPK_GL_WAIT_FRAME_VALVE_MS` (WALL-CLOCK ms from the first RESUMED wait tick, default 2000; loud `[frame-gate] VALVE`) and, 1.5x that (3000 ms) at the latest, WITHOUT a frame (`[frame-gate] VALVE OPEN`: a cocos2d-x/axmol renderer then gets nativeInit 0x0, pinned by the gate's VALVE-HARD row); no by-name `setScreenWidthAndHeight` call | `0` | "0" restores the old order (onSurfaceCreated on the first native tick, before any layout) and the by-name reflective `setScreenWidthAndHeight(w,h)` call (every frame on the setRenderer path, and on the reflected path's first delivery and window-box re-publish), byte for byte. With the gate ON the app's own `Cocos2dxGLSurfaceView.onSizeChanged` sizes nativeInit, for any engine. Offline gate: `debug/geh_cocos2dx-setScreenWidthAndHeight-reflect_gate.sh`; evidence `docs/cocos2dx-setscreensize-reflect-retired_2026-09-27.md`. | bisect |
| MACAPK_GL_WAIT_FRAME_VALVE_MS | 2000 -- milliseconds (wall clock, `System.nanoTime`, counted from the first RESUMED wait tick) before `MACAPK_GL_WAIT_FRAME`'s VALVE lets a renderer whose view has no frame start; the hard open is 1.5x this. Not render ticks: the tick cadence depends on the display (120 fps preferred, ~74/s free-run) | a positive integer | Tuning / diagnosis only: the offline gate runs it at 400 so its valve rows take under a second. Has no effect with `MACAPK_GL_WAIT_FRAME=0`. Evidence `docs/cocos2dx-setscreensize-reflect-retired_2026-09-27.md`. | tuning |
| MACAPK_GLSV_RENDER_MODE | On (2026-10-01) -- GLSurfaceView honours AOSP readyToDraw's render-mode term (GLSurfaceView.java :1628-1632): `setRenderMode` is range-checked (IllegalArgumentException, :1634-1642) and READ; RENDERMODE_WHEN_DIRTY calls onDrawFrame only when a request is pending -- `requestRender()` (:1650-1655), `onResume()` (:1747), every surfaceChanged delivery (onWindowResize :1763-1769, size change or not), and the initial request (:1258) -- and every frame taken consumes it, whatever the mode (guardedRun :1467). A withheld tick still runs queued GL events, drives the tree/overlays, presents nothing (the last frame stays) and tells the native frame end (NativeCocoa.beginWithheldTick) so AngleEndFrame skips the default-framebuffer readbacks (self-capture "gl", MACAPK_DEBUG_DUMPFRAME); a pending self-capture makes it draw instead, unless the game's Activity is stopped under another. Logs `RENDERMODE_WHEN_DIRTY: withholding ...` once per view. Stops a PAUSED cocos2d-x game (Cocos2dxGLSurfaceView.onPause = setRenderMode(WHEN_DIRTY), no super.onPause) from drawing under the activity that covers it | `0` | Restores the old path exactly: the mode is stored, never read or validated, requestRender() does nothing (every tick draws). docs/gd-meltdown-interstitial-hidden-window_2026-10-01.md; gates debug/glcover_gate.sh, debug/glcover_live.sh | bisect |
| MACAPK_WEBVIEW_BACKDROP | On (2026-10-01) -- a WebView with a TRANSLUCENT page base (setBackgroundColor(0), every ad interstitial, King's popups) tells native what its window drew beneath it before the page, as a flat colour: the frame's clear for its root (the decor windowBackground on a canvas-path window, 0 on a GL host) then every background from the root's child down to the WebView, SRC_OVER with view alpha (`WebView.macapkBackdropFor`); any non-ColorDrawable in that chain, or a root the last frame was not drawn for, is "unknown". webview_bridge.mm WvApplyBase then makes the page base transparent over a painted base = (page base over backdrop), so a transparent page shows its OWN window (Theme.NoTitleBar black) and never what lies under the window (unknown when an earlier below-window SurfaceView sibling punches a hole, and WvNativeBeneath is tested first); logs `base colour ... -> translucent over the window's own pixels beneath the WebView` | `0` | Java never sends a backdrop, so the native decision is the pre-change one: a translucent page on a canvas-path host keeps WebKit's opaque base (white, or #1e1e1e in dark appearance -- the interstitial's white Skip box vanishes into it). docs/gd-meltdown-interstitial-hidden-window_2026-10-01.md; gates debug/glcover_gate.sh, debug/glcover_live.sh | bisect |
| MACAPK_WINDOW_TREE_DUMP | Off (diagnostic, 2026-10-01) -- `=1`: 3 s and 8 s after every content-view swap, dump every NSWindow's view tree with each view's CALayer tree (class, frame, hidden, opacity, zPosition, opaque, contents), each MTKView's draw-tick count and role (ACTIVE / LAST-PATH-A / LAST-GL), the layer the framework EGL surface is bound to (EGL-BOUND), and every ORPHAN MTKView that left all windows (with its ticks); `[wtree] drawInMTKView on a WINDOWLESS view` if one ever ticks. What the WINDOW SERVER is given, which no self-capture sees | unset | -- | diagnostic |
| MACAPK_GLSV_PAUSE | On (2026-09-27) -- GLSurfaceView honours AOSP's pause/exit contract for a view with a Renderer: `onPause()` sets the pause and waits (bounded, 2000 ms, loud `renderer call still in flight` line on timeout) until no renderer call is in flight on the render thread (AppKit main); while paused, and after `onDetachedFromWindow()`, macapkDrawFrame still runs queued GL events and drives the view tree / overlays but calls no onSurfaceCreated / onSurfaceChanged / onDrawFrame, and a live resize (macapkSurfaceChanged / SurfaceHolder surfaceChanged) only records the size, delivered as one onSurfaceChanged on the first tick after `onResume()` (AOSP GLThread.onWindowResize :1763-1795); `onResume()` and a re-attach clear it. A view with no Renderer (cocos2d-x v2's reflected path) is untouched | `0` | Restores the no-op onPause/onResume and a renderer that keeps being driven after detach, exactly, AND brings the `org.andengine.b.c` shadow back (AppClassLoader couples MACAPK_RETIRE_ENGINE_SHADOWS to this knob): the app's own EngineLock under the old no-op onPause wedges AppKit main after Engine.onDestroy (the offline gate's HALF arm, built from the pre-change GLSurfaceView class, reads HANG), so =0 alone restores the shipped pre-change pair and never that wedge (gate arm P0 == OFF). docs/andengine-lock-shadow-retired_2026-09-27.md; gate debug/geh_andengine-lock-shadow_gate.sh | bisect |

| MACAPK_STUB_UNITY_ONLOAD | (DELETED 2026-09-27 with the code it gated) | -- | Until 2026-09-27, ANY set value (a presence check, so `0` too; this row used to say `1`) made the guest's dlsym(h, "JNI_OnLoad") / dlvsym return `__macapk_stub_jni_onload`, which returned JNI_VERSION_1_6 without running the library's own JNI_OnLoad. A v1.0.155 diagnostic for a libunity allocator crash that was gone by v1.0.157; with it set, libmain accepted the load but Unity's natives were never registered (UnsatisfiedLinkError on initJni, black window). Default OFF and set by no harness. The stub and the intercept are deleted, a set value is ignored, and the default path is unchanged: libunity's own JNI_OnLoad registers its 34 natives, as Bionic's do_dlsym and ART's LoadNativeLibrary do (live: `[jni-reg] nativeRender()Z @ libunity+0x8ad4bc` on Crossy). To A/B it, revert the retirement commit. docs/unity-stub-jni-onload-retired_2026-09-27.md; gate debug/geh_unity-stub-jni-onload_gate.sh. | diag (retired) |
