| MACAPK_UHOOK | RETIRED 2026-09-27: deleted with the Crossy 7.11.1 il2cpp diag kit; setting it prints one `[il2cpp-diag] ... RETIRED and IGNORED` line, which adds that the Time.* `[time-probe]` this knob fed is now MACAPK_IL2CPP_TIME_PROBE=<N> | any value (presence check) | Installs a family of native Unity/il2cpp diagnostic hooks (worker thread-name resolution, null-MethodInfo invoke logging, GetCurrentJobHandle checks, PlayableGraph::Evaluate counting) plus the matching Choreographer Unity-render-init trace. | diag |
| MACAPK_UNITY_DRIVE_HZ | 0 -- falls back to the measured panel refresh rate | a float 1.0-480.0 | Force-overrides the Hz used to drive Unity's per-frame Update loop, for diagnosis (must match what the guest is told or game speed breaks). | diag |
| MACAPK_UNITY_ARENA_ALIGN16 | ON (since 2026-09-29): the libunity low-address arena returns 16-byte-aligned payloads (Bionic LP64 contract), honours any power-of-two memalign/posix_memalign/aligned_alloc alignment, tags a shifted memalign payload so free decodes it, fails a SIZE_MAX-class request with ENOMEM instead of wrapping to a tiny block, ignores (reports once) a double free, gives aligned_alloc Bionic's EINVAL rejects, and routes libunity's `memalign` import into the arena | `0` | `=0` restores the v0 allocator byte-for-byte (`*_v0` functions): 8-mod-16 payloads, alignment capped at 4096, the memalign back-offset of a SHIFTED payload mis-decoded as a size on free (a fake overlapping free chunk; the gate SIGSEGVs it), and the size-overflow / double-free holes open. Offline gate `debug/unityarena_gate.sh` (its =0 arm is the positive control); docs/unity-arena-alignment-and-memalign-free_2026-09-29.md | bisect |
| MACAPK_UNITY_NOABS_OFF | Off -- the libunity GetResolvedPath absolutize-branch NOP patch is applied | any value (presence check, kill-switch) | Disables the signature-anchored patch that keeps libunity's asset-path resolver relative (matching the data.unity3d archive VFS mount), which otherwise fails engine init. | bisect |
| MACAPK_UNITY_PRESENT | (DELETED 2026-09-27 with the code it gated) | -- | Until 2026-09-27, `=1` installed the Unity present injection: the shim swapped the guest's window surface ON ITS BEHALF at unbind (`__macapk_arm64_eglMakeCurrent`), at glFlush / glFinish / glClear, and after nativeRender (the RegisterNatives wrap, which read ANY value as on). Default OFF since its v1.0.184-191 bring-up and never set by a harness. Its premise ("Unity never calls eglSwapBuffers") was wrong: libunity calls it directly and the v1.0.196 ALooper_forThread fix restored that; the guest's own eglSwapBuffers is the only present, as on a device. The code is deleted, a set value is ignored and named once (`[egl] MACAPK_UNITY_PRESENT: RETIRED and IGNORED`), and the default path was measured identical before and after (gate `debug/geh_unity-present-injection_gate.sh`, docs/unity-present-injection-retired_2026-09-27.md). To A/B it, revert the retirement commit. | dev (retired) |
| MACAPK_UNITY_RESERVE_STRICT | Off -- a huge libunity arena reserve with no low-address hole is still handed a high address | `1` | Refuses (returns MAP_FAILED) instead of handing back a high address above the 16 GiB ceiling the guest cannot represent, to measure whether the guest degrades gracefully. | dev |
| MACAPK_UNITY_SWAPPY_PRESENT_OFF | RETIRED 2026-09-27: ignored by default (MACAPK_UNITY_SWAPPY_PRESENT_RETIRED); under =0, off -- the libunity SwappyWrapper present b.ne is rewritten to an unconditional b | any value (presence check, kill-switch), read only under MACAPK_UNITY_SWAPPY_PRESENT_RETIRED=0 | Under =0, disables the v1.0.196 rewrite of libunity's `b.ne <eglSwapBuffers>` (Crossy 7.11.1 file 0x8856c8, the only match in the corpus) into `b`. The "NULL fn-ptr, render thread hangs" state it guarded is unreachable unless dlopen("libswappywrapper") succeeds, and no corpus APK ships that library. docs/unity-swappywrapper-present-dispatch_2026-09-27.md | bisect |
| MACAPK_UNITY_SWAPPY_PRESENT_RETIRED | On (since 2026-09-27) -- the Stage-6 loader leaves libunity's SwappyWrapper present dispatch as the app shipped it; no .text write, no `[unity-swappy]` line | `0` | Restores the v1.0.196 `b.ne -> b` rewrite byte for byte (prints `[unity-swappy] libunity.so: forced N ...`; MACAPK_UNITY_SWAPPY_PRESENT_OFF still suppresses it). The flag it bypassed is set only after dlopen("libswappywrapper") returns a handle, which Bionic and our dlopen both refuse for an unshipped library; the matcher hit 1 of 8 corpus libunity builds. Gate: debug/geh_unity-swappy-present-null-guard-patch_gate.sh. docs/unity-swappywrapper-present-dispatch_2026-09-27.md | bisect |
| MACAPK_VIDEO_SINK_TRACE | Off -- no periodic dump | `1` | Periodically dumps the video sink's player subtree (visibility/alpha/bounds/text of every ancestor/sibling/descendant), separating a still-visible app shutter from a stale-repaint bug. | diag |
| MACAPK_VIEWGROUP_MUTATION_RELAYOUT | On -- ViewGroup addView / removeView / removeViewAt / removeAllViews call the group's overridable requestLayout() + invalidate(), as AOSP ViewGroup.java:5063-5076 / 5474-5479 / 5519-5523 / 5730-5734 do | `0` (only "0" disables) | Lets a subclass that caches child structure behind its requestLayout() override (androidx GridLayout.invalidateStructure) hear about a new or departed child; without it F-Droid's donate card reached onLayout with an undefined span (AIOOBE Index -2147483648), unwinding RecyclerView layout and killing the download-status receiver (NOT the Install path itself -- see docs/jobwork-autofinish-on-drain_2026-09-28.md for the actual "Install does nothing" cause). =0 is the byte-identical old path (no request from any of the four); the *InLayout variants and removeViews are unaffected either way. Gate: debug/vgmutation_gate.sh; docs/viewgroup-mutation-requestlayout_2026-09-28.md. | bisect |
| MACAPK_JOB_AUTOFINISH_ON_DRAIN | On -- JobParameters.dequeueWork() auto-finishes the job once the queue is drained AND nothing dequeued earlier is still outstanding, as AOSP JobServiceContext.doDequeueWork (android13-release :495-515, "This will finish the job") does | `0` (only "0" disables) | Without it, a JobWorkItem-based job whose consumer never calls jobFinished itself (androidx.core.app.JobIntentService -- F-Droid's DownloaderService) leaves MacApkJobScheduler.JobRecord.running true forever after its first execution, so every LATER enqueue() on the same job id in that process is silently swallowed (scheduleInternal's `if (!rec.running) armLocked(...)`) -- the owner's real "Install does nothing after the first download" report. =0 is the byte-identical pre-fix path (dequeueWork() still returns null the same way, nothing calls jobFinished). Gate: debug/jobautofinish_gate.sh; docs/jobwork-autofinish-on-drain_2026-09-28.md. | bisect |
| MACAPK_JOB_SERVICE_UNBIND | ON (v1.0.1658): a job's Service is bound for exactly one execution (AOSP JobServiceContext BIND_AUTO_CREATE / closeAndCleanupJobLocked unbind) and destroyed when its last execution ends unless it was started or a client bound it (ActiveServices.isServiceNeededLocked), so the next job gets a fresh instance | `0` | `=0` restores the keep-forever instance exactly. Fixes androidx JobIntentService's second job in a process never dequeuing its work (F-Droid "can install apps, but not uninstall"). Gate: `debug/jobsvcunbind_gate.sh`. | bisect |
| MACAPK_SVC_STOP_ASYNC | ON (2026-09-30): Service.onDestroy runs as its own main-looper message, the AOSP H.STOP_SERVICE -> handleStopService shape, never inside stopSelf / stopSelfResult / Context.stopService / the job unbind; the registry record still drops synchronously, a same-component start queued behind a pending onDestroy is posted after it, a synchronous re-create (bind / jobBind) on the main thread first runs the queued onDestroy (2026-10-01 review), and a stopSelf with no live record does nothing | `0` | `=0` restores the synchronous onDestroy byte for byte. Fixes Organic Maps' in-app map download aborting with std::bad_function_call (DownloaderService.onStatusChanged -> stopSelf -> onDestroy -> nativeUnsubscribe mutated Storage's observer vector under its own walk). Gate: `debug/svcstop_gate.sh`. | bisect |
| MACAPK_INPUT_UI_THREAD | ON (2026-09-30): host input (clicks, keys, and injected harness input) for a tree with a SurfaceView goes to the Android UI thread like any view's (AOSP ViewRootImpl WindowInputEventReceiver on Looper.myLooper()), unless our OS-main frame callback drives that tree's renderer (a GLSurfaceView Renderer or a resolved reflected renderer that OS-main does not decline under MACAPK_GL_SINGLE_OWNER, 2026-10-01 review), which keeps the AppKit thread | `0` | `=0` restores the old "any SurfaceView in the tree -> AppKit thread" rule byte for byte. Fixes Organic Maps (search/menu "Cannot invoke setValue on a background thread", Download button abort in Storage CHECK(CalledOnOriginalThread)); changes the thread for every app that renders into its SurfaceView from its own threads (Unity, SDL, GameActivity, video players). Gate: `debug/inputthread_gate.sh`. | bisect |
| MACAPK_TRACKPAD_MULTITOUCH | ON (2026-10-01): MacApkMetalView ADDS indirect (trackpad) touches to allowedTouchTypes and TouchDispatcher maps a settled (100 ms) two-finger contact to a real multi-pointer stream (DOWN, POINTER_DOWN, MOVE, POINTER_UP, UP / CANCEL) with the pointers PLACED around the cursor the way AOSP pointer-mode FREEFORM places them (TrackpadGestureMapper); TWO pointers for a STILL contact and the wait for the settle are Mac.apk choices, NOT AOSP (pointer mode reports ONE pointer, a PRESS, TouchInputMapper.cpp:3068-3072); a recognized scroll / pinch or a left / right / other mouse button claims the fingers (a physical two-finger click cancels / suppresses the contact, never a tap); a contact AppKit stops reporting is CANCELled; the synthesized pinch rides the same delivery | `0` | `=0`: the view never opts into touches (no NSTouch ever reaches us), rightMouseDown: / otherMouseDown: / touches*WithEvent: fall straight to [super] and dispatchPinch runs its old body unchanged -- the pre-2026-10-01 behaviour. **The default-ON pinch path is an intended behaviour change for EVERY app** (Activity.dispatchTouchEvent instead of the content root, so a status-bar app's focal point moves up by the bar height; the routeToLoop thread choice; renderer-direct per pointer), measured byte-identical ON vs =0 on Organic Maps ONLY (no status bar). Fixes PianOli settings (two keys held at once). LANDED; the real NSTouch decode awaits the owner hand test. Gate: `debug/trackpad_gate.sh`. | bisect |
| MACAPK_VSYNC_DRIVE_CB | Off -- a Unity native-vsync pipe fd is not fed from the owner thread's poll loop | any value (presence check) | Writes a vsync event to a Unity native-vsync pipe so the owner thread's poll loop drives Unity's registered FD callback on that thread, for A/B during bring-up of looper-orphaned vsync fds. | bisect |
| MACAPK_VSYNC_MATCH_PANEL | On -- MTKView's preferredFramesPerSecond and the drive cadence use the screen's measured refresh rate | `0` | Restores the old unconditional 120Hz; measured neutral (not a frame-pacing fix), kept as defence for a panel whose refresh isn't a multiple of 60Hz. | bisect |
## Audio & media

| Flag | Default (unset) | Accepted values | Effect | Audience |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| MACAPK_AAUDIO_ONE_PATH | ON (every Stage-6 (arm64) guest dlopen of libaaudio.so gets the real AAudio implementation, whose data callback runs after requestStart; MACAPK_FMOD_AUDIO and MACAPK_FMOD_AAUDIO_STUB are ignored, with one RETIRED line if either is set) | `0` | `=0` restores the v1.0.1116..v1.0.1653 gate exactly: MACAPK_FMOD_AUDIO=0, or MACAPK_FMOD_AAUDIO_STUB set to any value, routes libaaudio.so to the inert kAAudioStub266 set again (AAUDIO_OK to create/open/start, the data callback never runs). Kept for one sweep cycle, then the stub set and this gate are deleted. ARM32 guests have no libaaudio.so interception at all (unchanged). v1.0.1654; gate debug/geh_fmod-aaudio-callback-gate_gate.sh; docs/fmod-aaudio-one-path_2026-09-27.md. | bisect |
| MACAPK_AMEDIA_SELFTEST | self-test does not run | any value (presence-only check) | When set, drives the NDK AMedia* C API (AMediaExtractor + AMediaCodec) through the shipped bionic shim exactly as an NDK decode loop would, decoding MACAPK_AMEDIA_SELFTEST_FILE and logging the resulting frame count as PASS (>0 frames) or FAIL. | diag |
| MACAPK_AMEDIA_SELFTEST_FILE | falls back to /tmp/mdtest.m4a | path to an .m4a media file | Selects the media file MACAPK_AMEDIA_SELFTEST decodes; only read when that self-test is enabled. | diag |
| MACAPK_AUDIOTRACK_TIMESTRETCH | time-stretch splicing enabled | 0 disables; unset/other enables | Default-on bisect gate for AudioTrack.setPlaybackParams's time stretch: while enabled a non-1.0 speed splices an AVAudioUnitTimePitch node into the live AVAudioEngine graph, and =0 instead rejects a non-1.0 speed with BAD_VALUE rather than silently ignoring it. | bisect |
| MACAPK_AUDIO_DUMP | no WAV tap installed | path to a .wav output file | Installs a tap on the AVAudioEngine mixer's output bus and writes the exact mixed PCM the device receives as 16-bit WAV, so a reviewer can verify non-silent audio actually reached the output. | diag |
| MACAPK_AUDIO_HONEST_OPEN | ON (when the host output cannot open -- the runtime's AVAudioEngine did not start, or the process has no audio service -- every audio API reports it the way a device does: macapk_audio_stream_create returns 0; AAudioStreamBuilder_openStream returns AAUDIO_ERROR_INVALID_STATE (-895), or AAUDIO_ERROR_NO_SERVICE (-881) with no service, and *stream = NULL; an OpenSL ES player's Realize returns SL_RESULT_CONTENT_UNSUPPORTED (9) and stays UNREALIZED; AudioTrack is STATE_UNINITIALIZED, AudioTrack.Builder.build throws UnsupportedOperationException("Cannot create AudioTrack"), play/pause/stop throw IllegalStateException, every write returns ERROR_INVALID_OPERATION; so OpenAL Soft's alcOpenDevice returns NULL on its own. Success paths unchanged.) | `0` | `=0` restores the old always-succeed answers exactly: a live runtime handle whose writes are swallowed, AAUDIO_OK with a stream, SL_RESULT_SUCCESS + REALIZED, a Builder that returns the dead track, silent play/pause/stop and ERROR_DEAD_OBJECT / sizeInBytes writes. The MACAPK_AAUDIO_ONE_PATH=0 never-open arm is untouched either way. 2026-09-28; gate debug/audio_honest_open_gate.sh (`audiohonest`); docs/audio-honest-open_2026-09-28.md. | bisect |
| MACAPK_AUDIO_PROBE | probe disabled, zero cost | 1 enables | Aggregates a machine-readable "is sound actually playing" signal across all voices (render calls with a real peak, plus the window peak) and prints it about every 5 seconds; separate from the far chattier per-voice MACAPK_AUDIO_TRACE. | diag |
| MACAPK_MEDIA_PROBE | probe disabled: every hook is a null check, no output | 1 enables | Playback-ATTEMPT counters (2026-10-01, owner-issue class h): `[media-stats]` lines for MediaCodec start / first-output / "no-output for 5.0s" (fed >= 2 input buffers, no output buffer) / end(stop,reset,release) totals (a release runs the 5 s stall check first; an end line with out=0 alone is NOT a finding, a healthy decoder needs ~8 inputs), AudioTrack first-write, and a 5 s window of codec_in / codec_out / rendered / polls / track_writes / track_bytes. debug/fleet_ab.sh sets it for our side; owner_issue_scan reads MEDIA-NOOUTPUT. Gate `mediastats`. | diag |
| MACAPK_AUDIO_RMS | RMS measurement skipped | any value (presence-only check) | In the AAudio feeder callback, measures peak/RMS of the guest mixer's (FMOD's) own output buffer to bisect whether inaudible music is FMOD producing silence (RMS~=0) or a downstream loss further in audio_backend.mm (RMS>0). | bisect |
| MACAPK_AUDIO_TEST_NO_OUTPUT | Off -- the runtime starts its AVAudioEngine normally | `1` | TEST HOOK, never for users: the runtime takes the failed-AVAudioEngine-start branch ("[audio] AVAudioEngine start FAILED: forced by MACAPK_AUDIO_TEST_NO_OUTPUT=1"), i.e. behaves as a Mac with no output device, so the MACAPK_AUDIO_HONEST_OPEN answers can be seen offline and live (OpenGD: alcOpenDevice fails with ErrorInvalidState and the game runs on silent). 2026-09-28; used by debug/audio_honest_open_gate.sh. | test |
| MACAPK_AUDIO_TRACE | tracing off | any value (presence-only check); `all` (2026-09-29) also logs EVERY Java hit instead of #1-3 + every 500th, so a harness can count one SoundPool.play per game action | Enables permanent, verbose audio-subsystem tracing, both in Java (AudioTrace's per-tag hit counts for AudioTrack/SoundPool/MediaPlayer) and natively (OpenSL/AAudio dlsym and IID-resolution logging), mirroring the MACAPK_NET_TRACE convention. | dev |
| MACAPK_CAMERA_TRACE | camera tracing off | any value (presence-only check) | Turns on the whole camera2 subsystem's narration, in both the Java layer (CameraBackend/CameraCaptureSessionImpl, "[camera2]") and the native AVFoundation layer (camera_capture.mm, "[camera]"), so the two interleave in one log. | dev |
| MACAPK_CAM_VIDEO_RANGE | full-range '420f' capture format | 1 selects '420v'; unset/other = '420f' | Selects the AVCaptureVideoDataOutput pixel range for camera capture: default full-range '420f' matches the Android/JFIF YUV convention apps assume, while =1 requests limited-range '420v' for a one-run A/B if a preview looks washed out. | bisect |
| MACAPK_FMOD_AAUDIO_STUB | RETIRED 2026-09-27: ignored by default (MACAPK_AAUDIO_ONE_PATH) | any value (presence-only check), read only under MACAPK_AAUDIO_ONE_PATH=0 | Under MACAPK_AAUDIO_ONE_PATH=0 only: forces the AAudio output back to the old inert no-op stub (fixed sample_rate/channels/frames_per_burst, AAUDIO_OK on everything) instead of the real driven callback. | bisect |
| MACAPK_FMOD_AUDIO | RETIRED 2026-09-27: ignored by default (MACAPK_AAUDIO_ONE_PATH) | 0 disables; other/unset enables, read only under MACAPK_AAUDIO_ONE_PATH=0 | Under MACAPK_AAUDIO_ONE_PATH=0 only: force-overrides in either direction whether the AAudio callback-mode output (FMOD/Unity built-in audio) is driven at all. | bisect |
| MACAPK_FMOD_BIND_BY_NAME | ON (an FMOD-named import (`_ZN4FMOD*`, `FMOD_*`) of any Stage-6 image binds BY NAME like every other import, landing in the app's own libfmod.so, as bionic's SymbolLookupList does; the loader has no FMOD test, no coverage scan and no reloc-memo FMOD bypass; one note line per process, which also says IGNORED if MACAPK_FMOD_STUB/_REAL/_TRACE is set) | `0` | `=0` restores the v1.0.1111..v1.0.1653 stub routing, binding-identical (not log-identical: its two routing lines now print only when true): the 28 FMOD stubs, now exported as `__macapk_fmodstub_<name>` (were `__macapk_arm64_<name>`), are found again by resolve_external (coverage-gated real routing via scan_fmod_coverage, MACAPK_FMOD_REAL/_STUB/_TRACE honoured), by resolve_external_silent (a WEAK FMOD definition is stubbed again, the old leak) and by the three guest by-name dlsym paths (macapk_fmodstub_by_name_legacy). Unrelated to MACAPK_FMOD_GENERIC_ROUTING (libfmod's own malloc/pthread imports): bisect them separately. v1.0.1654; gate `fmodbind`; docs/fmod-stub-routing-retired_2026-09-27.md. | bisect |
| MACAPK_FMOD_GENERIC_ROUTING | ON (a libfmod image resolves like every other image) | `0` | The Stage-6 loader no longer tags an image whose name contains "libfmod": its malloc/calloc/realloc/free/pthread_create resolve to the generic __macapk_arm64_* shims (Bionic's one global lookup list), so FMOD's threads keep DETACHED + their requested stack and go through the guest-thread trampoline. Needs MACAPK_GUEST_STACK_JVM_RESERVE ON (else FMOD's 96/112 KiB threads fail AttachCurrentThread and FMOD init hangs). `=0` restores the __macapk_arm64_libfmod_* bindings (NULL attr, macOS 512 KiB default, no trampoline). v1.0.1654; gate `fmodtag`; docs/fmod-image-tag-retired_2026-09-27.md. Unrelated to MACAPK_FMOD_BIND_BY_NAME (this one covers libfmod's OWN malloc/pthread imports; that one covers FMOD-named imports of OTHER images): bisect them separately. | bisect |
| MACAPK_FMOD_MUTE | (DELETED 2026-09-27 with the code it gated) | -- | Until 2026-09-27, `=1` restored the historical mute of FMOD's createSound/createStream (entries patched to `mov w0,#18; ret`, FMOD_ERR_FILE_NOTFOUND) that was the default before the v1.0.1111 flip. The function (`apply_libfmod_createSound_disable`) is deleted, so a set value is ignored; the default path was measured byte-identical before and after (docs/fmod-createsound-mute-retired_2026-09-27.md). To A/B it, revert the retirement commit. | bisect (retired) |
| MACAPK_FMOD_OWN_JNI_ARGS | ON (libfmod's own AudioDevice.init args reach Java unmodified; sites A/B only detected and logged, one RETIRED line per MATCHING image) | `0` | =0 restores the Stage-6 loader's constant-forcing rewrite of libfmod's two AudioDevice.init(IIII)Z call sites (site A in place, site B via a trampoline) to (2, 22050, 256, 4) byte for byte, and the old siteC SKIPPED line printed for every image. Only GD Lite 2.2.11 arm64 libfmod matches; the default lets the Bionic JNI bridge deliver FMOD's own format (GD: 24000, or 44100 with its "0022" option). Gate: debug/geh_fmod-jni-audio-args-patch_gate.sh; docs/fmod-jni-audio-args-retire_2026-09-27.md. | bisect |
| MACAPK_FMOD_REAL | only per-image-scanned FMOD imports route to real libfmod | any value (presence-only check) | Manual override that forces every FMOD-shaped symbol on every loaded image to resolve directly to real libfmod, bypassing the stub table, regardless of the automatic per-image coverage scan; kept as an escape hatch after that scan made per-app curation unnecessary. Read only under MACAPK_FMOD_BIND_BY_NAME=0; ignored with a note otherwise (2026-09-27). | experiment |
| MACAPK_FMOD_SITEC | old site-C JNI patch skipped | any value (presence-only check) | Restores the old per-image "site C" binary patch to libfmod's JNI variadic-argument handling for an A/B; superseded by the generic Bionic va_list JNI bridge fix. | bisect |
| MACAPK_FMOD_STUB | FMOD resolves to real libfmod unconditionally | any value (presence-only check) | Restores the old coverage-gated behaviour (route to real libfmod only when an image's FMOD imports exceed the stub table) for an A/B, since a complete set of silent stubs passes coverage without producing audio. Read only under MACAPK_FMOD_BIND_BY_NAME=0; ignored with a note otherwise (2026-09-27). | bisect |
| MACAPK_MEDIA_SELFTEST_FILE | falls back to /tmp/mdtest.m4a | path to an .m4a media file | Selects the input file MediaSelfTest decodes end-to-end (MediaExtractor -> MediaCodec -> NativeMedia -> AudioToolbox) when its own MACAPK_MEDIA_SELFTEST gate is on. | diag |
| MACAPK_MIC | mic capture enabled (subject to normal TCC prompt) | 0 disables capture; unset/other = enabled | MACAPK_MIC=0 hard-disables microphone capture so opening it always fails, for an environment where opening the mic is undesirable or lacks NSMicrophoneUsageDescription. | dev |
| MACAPK_MIC_DUMP | no WAV dump written | path to a .wav output file | Writes the converted captured microphone PCM, exactly what the app reads after sample-rate conversion, to a 16-bit WAV file, the microphone analog of MACAPK_AUDIO_DUMP. | diag |
| MACAPK_MIC_PERMISSION_TIMEOUT_MS | 15000 ms timeout | a positive integer (milliseconds) | Bounds how long the first AudioRecord open blocks waiting for the user to answer the system microphone permission prompt; on timeout the open fails instead of hanging, mirroring Android's STATE_UNINITIALIZED for an ungranted RECORD_AUDIO. | dev |
| MACAPK_MIC_TRACE | no lifecycle/level-meter logging | any value (presence-only check) | Enables per-microphone-stream lifecycle logging plus a roughly 2-second level meter (peak reading), the cheap machine-readable answer to whether real audio is arriving. | dev |
| MACAPK_OPENAL_STUB | (DELETED 2026-09-27 with the code it gated) | -- | Until 2026-09-27, any set value restored the NULL patch of libopenal.so's alcOpenDevice that was the default from c20c9434 (2026-05-16) until v1.0.268 (`mov x0,#0; ret` over its entry, so OpenAL Soft reported no device and the app ran silent). Its 36-byte anchor matched OpenGD v0.1.0-beta's libopenal and no other build (the only corpus library that defines alcOpenDevice), so it could never serve its stated purpose of covering "a future OpenAL build". The function (`apply_libopenal_alcopendevice_patch`) is deleted; a set value prints one `[arm64-native] MACAPK_OPENAL_STUB=<v>: RETIRED and IGNORED` line and changes nothing else. The default path was measured byte-identical before and after (the whole loaded r-x PT_LOAD of OpenGD's libopenal). The honest bisect point for a future OpenAL crash is the AAudio / OpenSL ES shims in bionic_shim.cpp, not the app's entry point; to A/B the patch itself, revert the retirement commit. docs/libopenal-alcopendevice-retired_2026-09-27.md; gate debug/geh_libopenal-alcopendevice-patch_gate.sh. | bisect (retired) |
| MACAPK_SENSOR_ZERO_MOTION | motion sensors report a synthesized 1g-at-rest pose | exactly "1" | Forces the ACCELEROMETER/GRAVITY/LINEAR_ACCELERATION/ROTATION_VECTOR family back to literal (0,0,0) instead of the default synthesized gravity-at-rest pose, as a one-env-var bisect for whether an app's layout is driven by the accelerometer. | bisect |
| MACAPK_VIDEO_IMAGE_SELFTEST_FILE | falls back to /tmp/goi_test.h264 | path to a raw Annex-B .h264 file | Selects the raw Annex-B H.264 elementary stream VideoImageSelfTest feeds one access unit at a time into MediaCodec.getOutputImage(), exercising the real public buffer-index protocol. | diag |
| MACAPK_VIDEO_IMAGE_SELFTEST_OUT | falls back to /tmp/goi_mine.png | path to a .png output file | Where VideoImageSelfTest writes its last decoded frame, reconstructed from getOutputImage()'s own planes back to RGB, for visual inspection. | diag |
| MACAPK_VIDEO_IMAGE_SELFTEST_REF | falls back to /tmp/goi_ref.png; if absent, only the motion check runs | path to a reference .png file | Optional reference frame decoded independently by ffmpeg (not VideoToolbox) that VideoImageSelfTest compares its own derived-YUV output against, instead of only checking that decode does not crash. | diag |
| MACAPK_VIDEO_SELFTEST | self-test layer never created, zero impact | path to a video file | Creates an on-screen CAMetalLayer and loops the given clip through the real decode -> CVPixelBuffer -> MTLTexture -> present pipeline, proving that chain visually since no movie-texture app is in the protected sweep. | diag |
| MACAPK_VIDEO_TOOLBOX_SELFTEST | self-test tick never runs | path to a raw Annex-B elementary stream | Decodes one more NAL each drawn frame (looping) through the real MediaCodec-facing VideoToolbox pipeline and presents it on-screen via CocoaPresentVideoFrame, exercising the exact functions android.media.MediaCodec itself calls end to end. | diag |
| MACAPK_VIDEO_TOOLBOX_SELFTEST_MIME | defaults to video/avc | video/avc or video/hevc | Selects the MIME type MACAPK_VIDEO_TOOLBOX_SELFTEST decodes as. | diag |
| MACAPK_VIDEO_VIEW_RECT | video plane clipped to its own view rect (current behaviour) | "0" or "false" disables; unset/other enables | Bisect gate defaulting on; =0 (or "false") restores the pre-v1.0.996 behaviour of the video plane always covering the whole window instead of being pinned to the TextureView/SurfaceView's own rect. | bisect |
## Native code & ABI

| Flag | Default (unset) | Accepted values | Effect | Audience |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| MACAPK_ABI_REMARSHAL | enabled (ABI re-marshalling ON) | 0/1 | ABI re-marshalling of JNI calls is default-ON; setting this to "0" A/B-disables it. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ALLOW_OVERSIZE_ALLOC | disabled (oversize allocs rejected, return 0/OOM) | 0/1 | Disables the guest-heap oversize-allocation rejection (>1/4 heap size) so a suspected lifter garbage-size bug can be reproduced and traced with the watchpoint infra instead of being silently caught. | diag |
| MACAPK_ALLOW_UNSAFE_X18 | refuses to load arm64 guest code when x18 preservation is unavailable | any set value | Overrides the hard x18-capability-probe refusal and continues loading arm64 guest code anyway, at the cost of silent memory corruption ("Expect corruption"). | bisect |
| MACAPK_ALU_PC_WRITE_DEAD | new behavior: a non-flag-setting ALU write to R15 lowers as an indirect branch (ALUWritePC) | 1 (any other value/unset keeps new behavior) | Restores the old, incorrect dead-store lowering for an ALU write to PC instead of treating it as an architectural indirect branch. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ANDROID_ENVIRON | enabled (guest sees a synthesized Android-shaped `environ`) | 0/1 | Serves the guest a measured, Android-platform-shaped `environ` (18-ish zygote-style entries) instead of the host developer's shell environment; "0" restores the old host-environment leak. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ARENA_PREFAULT | enabled once libunity is present (Unity guests only) | 0/1 | Touches (commits) every page of a fresh guest-heap-arena allocation so the guest's first write into it never takes the deterministic x18-losing minor-page-fault; force-overrides the Unity-presence auto-gate either way. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ARENA_PREFAULT_CAP | 64 MiB (allocations above this size are not prefaulted) | bytes (0 = no cap) | Diagnostic override of the prefault size cap, used to test whether an x18-trap loop's buffer is a large arena allocation the default cap was skipping. | diag |
| MACAPK_ARENA_WX | heal (seal + log + re-heal on a guest write fault into a W^X-sealed page) | 0 / strict / (anything else = heal) | Controls the guest-heap arena's W^X-violation recovery: "0" turns healing off (arena stays fully RW), "strict" lets the fault fall through to normal SIGSEGV handling for a deliberate hunt, anything else seals+logs+heals. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ARM32RC_COPROC_UNDEF | off (an unimplemented ARM coprocessor instruction lowers to a no-op) | 0/1 | Makes an unimplemented-coprocessor instruction (LDC/STC/CDP/MRC/MCR on a PE with no such coprocessor) lower to A64's permanently-undefined encoding (a real fault) instead of the incumbent no-op. | experiment |
| MACAPK_ARM32RC_GENERIC_FP_JNI | ON: every ARM32 JNI export except JNI_OnLoad gets the generic, signature-driven thunk (floats reach the shim's descriptor walk from d0-d7) | "0" restores the name-keyed cocos2d-x nativeTouchesBegin/End "typed float" thunk, byte for byte | Retires an engine-shaped, name-keyed JNI thunk: ART marshals every native from its shorty (32-bit ARM JNI is soft-float), and with MACAPK_ARM32_JNI_SIG_FP + MACAPK_ARM32_JNI_FLOAT_BITS ON the generic thunk hands the guest the same (IFF)V image. Read at TRANSLATION time and the dylib cache is keyed on NATIVE_ID only, so an A/B must re-translate (`MACAPK_SWEEP_CLEAR=dylib`) with the variable in the RUNTIME's environment; only correct with both shim gates ON. Offline gate: `debug/geh_cocos-touch-typed-float-thunk_gate.sh`; evidence `docs/arm32-jni-float-args_2026-09-27.md`. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ARM32RC_MULTI_ENTRY | follows MACAPK_ARM32RC_TAIL_LIVE (effectively ON) | 0/1 | Mints a second, alternate entry stub for every guest address that discovery over-fragmented interior to a function but that some OTHER function actually branches/calls into, so those calls resolve to real code instead of a return-0 panic stub. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ARM32RC_PLT_LOCAL | enabled (a PIC .so's calls to its own exported functions through its own PLT resolve locally) | 0/1 | Resolves a PIC-built .so's self-referential PLT stubs (R_ARM_JUMP_SLOT naming a LOCAL symbol) to the local function instead of falling through to a silent return-0 panic stub; "0" restores the old panic-stub behaviour. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ARM32RC_PLT_RELOC_FIRST | ON | `0` | arm32rc: a call to a PLT stub resolves through the stub's R_ARM_JUMP_SLOT relocation (local body / import shim) even when discovery also lifted the stub's bytes as a function -- on Android `foo@plt` jumps through the GOT word Bionic bound; an import's LUT thunk is registered over a lifted stub instead of being shadowed by it. A stub whose relocation cannot be honoured (local target not lifted, import with no host symbol) keeps the lifted stub for BOTH the call and the thunk. Deliberate difference: Android reads the GOT word at CALL time, this binds statically, so a guest rewriting its own LOCAL .got.plt slot (PLT hooking) is not honoured at these sites -- the policy arm32rc already applies to every non-lifted stub (libnhook.so has 253 moved sites). `=0` restores ITS OWN part (the link targets: `vaddr_to_sym` first, the thunk shadowed); BOTH this and MACAPK_ARM32_BIONIC_THUMB_BIT =0 together are byte-identical to the pre-change translator on 235/235 corpus libs (the Thumb-carrying libs keep their odd words with only this one =0). Moves every lifted-stub call site in the corpus to a direct branch: 740,452 sites on 138 of 235 libs (libarc 605); 0 import thunks were shadowed in the corpus. Translation-time: toggle with MACAPK_SWEEP_CLEAR=dylib. docs/arm32-bionic-reloc-exact_2026-09-28.md; gate debug/arm32reloc_gate.sh. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ARM32RC_SIBLING_LINK | enabled (cross-guest-library symbol resolution against sibling .so's in the same app) | 0/1 | Lets arm32rc resolve an unresolved import against a SIBLING guest .so's exported dynsyms (e.g. Firebase Analytics calling into libFirebaseCppApp) instead of a panic stub; "0" disables this resolution tier entirely. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ARM32RC_STUB_TRACE | off (a refused/un-lowered instruction is silently tallied, not named) | any set value; a path writes to that file instead of stderr | Dumps one line per instruction the arm32rc backend refused to lower, naming the address, mode, mnemonic and reason, so a class of unexplained instruction-refusal can be traced to specific sites. | diag |
| MACAPK_ARM32RC_TAIL_LIVE | enabled since v1.0.1086 (implies MULTI_ENTRY) | 0/1 | Turns a guest tail-branch into a real `bl`+epilogue+`ret` (instead of `epilogue; b target`) and lets an alternate entry inherit live guest registers rather than zeroing them, fixing shared-tail functions (e.g. zlib crc32) that used to always return 0; "0" reverts and is the bisect lever for a future deep-tail-recursion regression. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ARM32RC_TRACE_STORES | off (no per-store watchpoint instrumentation) | any set value; "multi" restricts to multi-word (STRD/STM) stores only | Emits a `__macapk_trace_store` call after each instrumented guest store so a watchpoint harness can see writes to a specific address; "multi" narrows instrumentation to multi-word store shapes to keep single-word-STR-heavy apps from timing out before reaching the bug. | diag |
| MACAPK_ARM32RC_TRACE_STUBS | off (an unresolved call target is not named) | any set value | Names, up to 400 times, the real guest address of every call arm32rc could not resolve and had to stub, since the panic trampoline itself only ever sees whatever register the guest left behind, not the true target. | diag |
| MACAPK_ARM32_ARM_EXTENT_ALIGN | enabled (an ARM-mode function's lifted extent may only end on a 4-aligned address) | 0/1 | Refuses to clip an ARM32 function's lift extent at a discovery candidate that lands on a non-4-aligned address (architecturally impossible as an ARM boundary), preventing a phantom mid-instruction Thumb "start" from truncating a real function and stranding its epilogue; "0" restores the pre-v1.0.1088 behaviour. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ARM32_BIONIC_DATA_RELOC | ON | `0` | ARM32 loader: R_ARM_ABS32 against a DEFINED symbol writes exact S + the in-place addend, R_ARM_GLOB_DAT writes S (bit 0 of an STT_FUNC: kept since 2026-09-28 by MACAPK_ARM32_BIONIC_THUMB_BIT, masked when that is =0), as Bionic does (linker_relocate.cpp :227-231, :326-341). Fixes libc++abi typeinfo vptrs (were 8 bytes early) and typeinfo names (1 byte early). `=0`: the old addend-dropping, bit-masking write. Translation-time: toggle with MACAPK_SWEEP_CLEAR=dylib. v1.0.1653. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ARM32_BIONIC_THUMB_BIT | ON | `0` | ARM32 loader: a relocation against a DEFINED symbol keeps bit 0 (the Thumb flag) of st_value, as Bionic's resolve_symbol_address (`st_value + load_bias`, no mask) does -- local R_ARM_JUMP_SLOT GOT words and R_ARM_GLOB_DAT/ABS32 against an STT_FUNC. Carve-out: a local operator new/delete (`_Znw*`/`_Zna*`/`_Zdl*`/`_Zda*`) JUMP_SLOT is routed to the guest-heap shim and its GOT word keeps the file value, where Bionic writes the definition. `=0` restores ITS OWN part (the GOT/data words: `st_value & ~1` + bias + addend); BOTH this and MACAPK_ARM32RC_PLT_RELOC_FIRST =0 together are byte-identical to the pre-change translator on 235/235 corpus libs (with only this one =0, 138 libs still move by RELOC_FIRST). Changes 94,417 JUMP_SLOT + 67,040 data words across 150 of 244 corpus libs; indirect calls unaffected (the LUT lookup masks). Translation-time: toggle with MACAPK_SWEEP_CLEAR=dylib. docs/arm32-bionic-reloc-exact_2026-09-28.md; gate debug/arm32reloc_gate.sh. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ARM32_BLX_LR_TEMP | ON | `0` | arm32rc: `blx lr` copies the target into a scratch register before the return address is written to LR. Guest r14 is pinned to ONE host register (x10), so the v1.0.833 "read Rm first" hoist was a no-op and `blx lr` called its own return address (ARM ARM A8.8.26: `target = R[m]` precedes `LR = ...`). Victim: OpenTTD v7a liblzma `lzma_code` (`blx lr` at 0xe2c8) -> "LZMA error 1360" in data unpack. Only Rm == LR changes; every other BLX is byte-identical. Corpus: >= 150 `blx lr` sites in 31 of 246 ARM32 libs (declared functions only; `debug/blxlr_verify/census.py`); sweep libs changed: HCR 1.8.1 v7a, JJR, Mindustry libarc. `=0`: the old codegen byte for byte. Translation-time (re-translate to A/B). v1.0.1655. Gate `blxlr`. | fix (docs/arm32-pool-nv-blxlr-setenv_2026-09-27.md) |
| MACAPK_ARM32_CTYPE_TABLE | ON | `0` | ARM32 loader: a `_ctype_` DATA import (pre-API-21 ctype.h: `extern const char *_ctype_`, inline is<x>() = `(_ctype_ + 1)[c] & bit`) gets a pointer cell + bionic's `_C_ctype_[1+256]` in the synthetic ctype segment, beside `_toupper_tab_` / `_tolower_tab_`. Left at 0 before, so every inline isdigit/isspace read the ELF header (19 corpus libs incl. HCR 1.8.1, GD Lite). `=0`: unresolved. Translation-time. v1.0.1653. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ARM32_CXXABI_SIBLING_FIRST | ON | `0` | ARM32 translator: a PLT import that is part of the C++ runtime ABI no Android system library exports (`__dynamic_cast`, `__gxx_personality_v0`, `__cxa_*` minus libc's 3 / libstdc++'s guard+pure_virtual, `_ZSt*` / `_ZNSt*` / `_ZNKSt*` / `_ZT[ISV]St*` / `__cxxabiv1`), which `resolve_import` answers only from the HOST namespace, binds TWO-LEVEL and WEAK to the first library of the importer's DT_NEEDED BFS closure that exports it (Bionic's lookup order), instead of a flat bind that macOS libc++abi always won with 32-bit guest pointers. 33 corpus libs (OpenTTD `libapplication.so` 18 incl. `__dynamic_cast`, 30 Firebase C++ plugins, superjetpack, Fossify); no sweep row. `=0`: the old flat bind, byte for byte. Translation-time (re-translate: drop `lib_native`). v1.0.1653+. Gate `dataimport` rows S4/C3. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ARM32_DATA_IMPORT_BIND | all | unset/`1` = write every result; `sibling` = write only results whose provider is an ARM32 image; `0` = register nothing, write nothing | RUNTIME half of the ARM32 data-import link: which resolved slots `__macapk_arm32_link_image` writes. The resolver runs the identical Bionic-order search in every mode, so an A/B compares one linker. `=0` gives the guest memory of a pre-change build without re-translating (the `sibling` mode isolates cross-image effects from host-data / host-fn ones). v1.0.1653+. Gate `dataimport` row C1 (positive control). | bisect |
| MACAPK_ARM32_DATA_IMPORT_LINK | ON | `0` | ARM32 translator: every relocation against an UNDEFINED symbol with no PLT stub (formerly `skipped_no_plt`, left holding A or 0: 3,572 corpus sites, e.g. OpenTTD's 1,311 typeinfo vptrs into libc++_shared, `__stack_chk_guard`, `__sF`, `SL_IID_*`) is described in a per-image link descriptor + resolved at load time by `__macapk_arm32_link_image`, in Bionic's order (DT_NEEDED BFS over registered ARM32 images, then Mac.apk's system-library host data in guest memory, flat step for GLOBAL, weak = 0 + A, `cannot locate symbol` + late rebind). Also: a ctype synthetic segment that would cross the image's 64 KiB bias band becomes libc host data (4 corpus libs). `=0`: no descriptor, no thunks, no link call -- dylibs byte-identical to the pre-change translator (B1: 10 sweep-row libs). Translation-time; a translator/shim version skew is a hard dlopen failure (NATIVE_ID covers both). v1.0.1653+. Gate `dataimport`. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ARM32_DROP_VADDRS | inert (no discovered functions are suppressed) | comma/space-separated hex vaddrs | Diagnostic-only: suppresses emission of the named discovered function bodies (so calls to them get the usual return-0 panic stub instead), used to confirm whether a specific fabricated/over-eager discovery result is on the live execution path. | diag |
| MACAPK_ARM32_EXPORT_ALIASES | ON | `0` | ARM32 translator: export EVERY defined GLOBAL/WEAK `STT_FUNC` `.dynsym` name whose entry (`st_value & ~1`) is a translated function start, as an alias of that body -- the set Bionic's lookup can return (`linker_relocate.h` `is_symbol_global_and_defined`), each bound at `st_value + load_bias` whatever other name shares it. Discovery keeps ONE name per address (last-wins), so arm32rc used to export one of a C1/C2 ctor pair, a D1/D2 dtor pair or two identical-code-folded functions, and every import of another failed its non-lazy flat bind: OpenTTD's `libapplication.dylib` could not dlopen (13 names, incl. `__cxa_end_cleanup`), and 6 of `MACAPK_ARM32_CXXABI_SIBLING_FIRST`'s 18 weak two-level binds bound NULL. A JNI-shaped alias (`Java_*` / `JNI_OnLoad`) gets its own AAPCS64 thunk onto the same body. Only function STARTS qualify (not MULTI_ENTRY alternate entries); a name already in the Mach-O symbol table (e.g. a pending undefined) is skipped, never upgraded. Corpus: 17,179 alias names in 78 of 246 ARM32 libs. `=0`: output byte-identical to the pre-change translator (with `MACAPK_ARM32_SIZELESS_EXPORTS=0`: 24 libs, gate row B1). Translation-time: re-translate to A/B (drop `lib_native/*.dylib`; the dylib cache is keyed by NATIVE_ID, not env). 2026-09-27 (ALIAS lane). Gate `arm32alias`. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ARM32_LINK_TRACE | off | any value except `0` | One stderr line per ARM32 data-import resolution (`[arm32-link] trace <image> slot .. "name" kind=.. S=.. A=.. -> value via <provider>`). Diagnostic only. | dev |
| MACAPK_ARM32_FNPTR_ARG_WINDOW | off (BL-argument idiom look-ahead capped at 6 instructions) | 0/1 | Widens the ARM32 discovery BL-argument idiom's look-ahead window from 6 to 7 instructions so a callee whose other arguments need one more setup instruction still gets its address-taken function discovered (measured 7-seed effect, isolated to one binary). | experiment |
| MACAPK_ARM32_FNPTR_POOL_PRED | off ("Gate P" literal-pool-predecessor relief inactive) | 0/1 | Admits a non-authoritative, ARM-mode BL-argument function-pointer seed as a real function entry when it is immediately preceded by a provable literal-pool word AND opens with `push {..,lr}`, recovering cocos2d-x v2 `CCScheduler::scheduleSelector` targets that would otherwise silently no-op. | experiment |
| MACAPK_ARM32_GUEST_DYNAMIC_CAST | ON: an ARM32 image's own libsupc++ / libc++abi `__dynamic_cast` is translated from its own bytes, as a device runs it | "0" restores the old name-matched host RTTI walker override (`__macapk_shim_dynamic_cast`), byte for byte | Retires an engine-shaped override written for the deleted LLVM lifter's miscompile of HCR's copy. Measured against the guest bytes on an ARMv7 CPU, arm32rc is exact on HCR 1.8.1 / HCR 1.43 / GD Lite / JJ while the walker mishandles private bases and ARM EABI by-name type_info identity. Read at TRANSLATION time and the dylib cache is keyed on NATIVE_ID only, so an A/B must clear `lib_native/<lib>.dylib` or its `.nativeid` between arms (`MACAPK_SWEEP_CLEAR=dylib`). Offline gate: `debug/geh_cocos-dynamic-cast-override_gate.sh`; evidence `docs/dynamic-cast-override-retired_2026-09-26.md`. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ARM32_HEAP_MB | off (guest ARM32 heap stays the historic 256 MiB at 0x20000000) | integer MiB, clamped to [256,1024] | Relocates the ARM32 guest bump-heap UP into unused arena space (base 0x40000000) and enlarges it to the requested size, relieving the 256 MiB wall that asset-heavy ARM32 apps hit; every other address region keeps its default location. | experiment |
| MACAPK_ARM32_HEAP_RECLAIM | ON since `f8e3f143` (batch E, 2026-09-26; before it, off: freed ARM32 guest blocks were never reused) | "0" restores leak-forever | The ARM32 guest heap reuses blocks released via free()/operator delete (since batch F through the coalescing allocator, MACAPK_ARM32_HEAP_COALESCE). Reuse means a guest block can come back holding stale bytes, as on a device; a shim that relied on fresh zeroed memory breaks (MACAPK_ZLIB_STREAM_FIELDS below). Row corrected 2026-09-27. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ARM32_PRINTF_AAPCS | ON | `0` | ARM32 printf-family shims (sprintf, snprintf, printf, fprintf, __sprintf_chk, __snprintf_chk, the v* forms, __android_log_print / __android_log_vprint) read varargs the AAPCS32 way: r0-r3 then the guest stack, 64-bit values at an even register or an 8-aligned stack slot, doubles as core words, `*` width/precision, va_list 8-aligned for doubles. The plain four became WIDE imports (they now get the guest SP). `=0`: the old 5-slot formatter (stack varargs 0, %f 0.0, %lld one half). v1.0.1653. | bisect |
| MACAPK_SSCANF_BIONIC_INT_WIDTH | ON | `0` | The ARM32 `sscanf` shim stores each integer conversion at the width its length modifier names, as Bionic's vfscanf does (`%d` 4 bytes, `%hd` 2), and never reads the literal format text. `=0`: the retired cocos2d-x BMFont match that forced a 2-byte store for "xoffset=%d" / "yoffset=%d" / "xadvance=%d" (9ce85bad). On HCR 1.8.1 the two arms differ only in 2 padding bytes (+90..91 of the malloc(124) glyph element) that nothing reads. v1.0.1656. See `docs/cocos-bmfont-sscanf-shortfield-retired_2026-09-27.md`. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ZLIB_STREAM_FIELDS | ON (2026-09-27) | "0" restores the old ARM32 zlib shim | The ARM32 zlib shim writes the guest z_stream fields zlib owns, exactly when zlib writes them: msg (NULL at every init/reset, zlib's string on an error, the guest's own value otherwise), data_type, and state (non-NULL/NULL). "0" never writes them, so msg keeps a recycled block's stale bytes and minizip reads every successful inflate as Z_DATA_ERROR (HCR 1.8.1's 17 getFileDataFromZip Assert dialogs). Gate: `debug/w7arm32heap_gate.sh` section 5. docs/arm32-zlib-shim-stream-fields_2026-09-27.md | bisect |
| MACAPK_ARM32_JNI_FLOAT_BITS | ON: the ARM32 JNI descriptor walk decodes a jfloat as the low 32 bits of its AAPCS64 v-register (AAPCS64 C.1; ART soft-float AdvanceFloat) | "0" restores the pre-2026-09-27 `static_cast<float>(double)` decode | A NUMERIC conversion of the d-register HotSpot leaves after an S-register write (0x00000000_<floatbits>, a denormal) gives +0.0 for every jfloat: every float parameter of every ARM32 RegisterNatives native and of every name-bound native with a J/D in its signature (HCR onSensorChanged, box2d jniGetLocalPoint) arrived as 0.0. "0" is an A/B lever only. Offline gate: `debug/geh_cocos-touch-typed-float-thunk_gate.sh` (control CF). | bisect |
| MACAPK_ARM32_JNI_SIG_DETECT | off (a signature-declared primitive JNI argument is truncated, never pointer-detected) | any set value | Restores the old (incorrect) heuristic of pointer-detecting a declared-primitive JNI argument by its high 32 bits, which can mis-mint a functor-pointer-cast-to-int as a JNI handle; kept only as an A/B escape hatch. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ARM32_JNI_SIG_FP | ON: the JVMTI NativeMethodBind feed registers a name-bound ARM32 native's descriptor when it has a scalar J, D or F argument | "0" restores J/D-only registration | Without a registered descriptor the name-bound JNI marshaller falls back to a heuristic that reads x2-x7 and never the FP bank, so an F-only native such as cocos2d-x nativeTouchesBegin(IFF)V gets integer-register junk for its floats. Natives with no scalar J/D/F stay on the unchanged heuristic, bit for bit. Offline gate: `debug/geh_cocos-touch-typed-float-thunk_gate.sh` (control CS). | bisect |
| MACAPK_ARM32_JNI_WALK_CONTINUE | ON | `0` | The ARM32 JNI descriptor walk (macapk_arm32_jni_call_core) runs the WHOLE signature: an argument whose own bank is exhausted (FP >= 8, integer >= 6; a host-stack argument, not forwarded) is placed as 0 but keeps its AAPCS32 slot, so later REGISTER arguments of the other bank are no longer dropped (box2d jniCreateBody(JIFFFFFFFFZZZZZF)J's four booleans). `=0` restores the early exit. v1.0.1653. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ARM32_MUTEX_TYPE | enabled (a statically-initialized guest mutex's Bionic type bits are honoured) | 0/1 | Reads the Bionic mutex-type bits (NORMAL/RECURSIVE/ERRORCHECK) out of a statically-initialized guest `pthread_mutex_t` word and maps them (with the Bionic-vs-Apple code inversion corrected) instead of always treating the mutex as NORMAL; "0" restores the old always-NORMAL behaviour. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ARM32_NORETURN_REACH | ON: a libstdc++ throw helper counts as no-return only if no exit is reachable from its entry without passing `bl __cxa_throw`, the backward walk may not contradict a containing symbol, and a CONDITIONAL call to a helper is not a terminator | `0` restores the v1.0.24 classifier and v1.0.62 terminator checks, byte for byte | Discovery fact behind `bl <helper>`. The v1.0.24 classifier listed ordinary functions whose inlined throw path allocates near their start (__gnu_cxx::__scoped_lock on Jetpack Joyride, vector<float>::vector on GD Meltdown). No output change on the corpus except the helper set; the synthetic `debug/throwhelper_verify/` .so exercise all three rules. Translation-time: toggle with MACAPK_SWEEP_CLEAR=dylib. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ARM32_PICFN_THUMB2 | **ON since v1.0.1655** (was off) | 0/1 | Thumb-2 32-bit PIC function-pointer discovery scan: `ldr.w Rt,[pc,#imm]` (A8.8.65 T2) + `add Rt,pc` (A8.8.6 T2), hits fed into the normal (non-authoritative) data_fnptr_seed filter chain, recovering address-taken Thumb-2 callbacks discovery otherwise misses (GD Lite pugixml strconv_attribute 0x4b2ffc; HCR 1.8.1 libjpeg 0x1c74cc/0x1c74ec). `=0` restores the old default (no scan). Gate: debug/picfnthumb2_gate.sh | fix (docs/arm32-thumb2-pic-fnptr-lr-and-nopw_2026-09-27.md) |
| MACAPK_ARM32_PICFN_THUMB2_LR | ON (v1.0.1655) | 0/1 | The Thumb-2 PIC scan also accepts Rt = LR (r14); the old `rt >= 13` test discarded LR with SP/PC. GD Lite's libpng png_read_filter_row_sub (0x50db40, `ldr.w lr,[pc]; add lr,pc`) is the victim: unlifted, every Sub-filtered PNG row stayed raw (garbled loading-screen glyphs, transparent background). `=0` restores r0-r12. | fix (docs/arm32-thumb2-pic-fnptr-lr-and-nopw_2026-09-27.md) |
| MACAPK_ARM32_TERM_NOPW | ON (v1.0.1655) | 0/1 | For a Thumb-TAGGED data_fnptr seed only, the terminator-precedence walk-back also steps over the Thumb-2 `nop.w` (0xf3af 0x8000, A8.8.119 T2) alignment padding, as it already did for the Thumb-16 NOPs. ARM-mode candidates and every other caller are byte-identical. `=0` restores the old walk-back. | fix (docs/arm32-thumb2-pic-fnptr-lr-and-nopw_2026-09-27.md) |
| MACAPK_ARM32_PRO_SCAN_ARM_CONTEXT | ON (2026-09-30) | 0/1/blind | Both ARM32 prologue scans (the boundary pass and the prologue-lift pass) reject a 4-aligned candidate whose only prologue reading is a Thumb16 `push`/`sub sp` in the LOW halfword of an ARM word, on ARM-context evidence: (a) an A32 B/BL lands on it (A8.8.18/A8.8.25: B/BL A1 never change ISA), (b) an A32 `ldr Rt,[pc,#+imm]` within the 1024 words before it (LDR-literal reach, A8.8.64) loads a literal after it, or (c) >= 12 of the 16 words at va-32..va+28 are condition-AL (measured: true Thumb 4-aligned starts in GD Lite 4/12778, true ARM starts 662/796). Exempt: `.ARM.exidx`-named starts and addresses the dynsym Thumb ranges place in Thumb code (`lookup_is_thumb`), and A32-B sites in Thumb ranges are not decoded (GD Lite v7a lost 0x5fad20/0x6129b8/0x62934c to the blind version). Such coincidences minted false function starts mid-function, capping the real function; its own `b<cond>` to a cold block then became a TAIL CALL whose epilogue restored x19-x28 (guest r4-r11 + SP). `=0` restores the old scans byte for byte; `blind` is a labelled positive-control arm (no exemptions, no density) for the gate's L1/C8 rows, not a mode to ship. | fix (docs/arm32-false-split-thumb-coincidence_2026-09-30.md; gate arm32falsesplit) |
| MACAPK_ARM32_REACH_EXTENT | ON (2026-09-30) | 0/1 | ARM-mode synthetic functions are lifted over the extent their control flow REACHES (fall through, conditional branches, intra-extent `b`, stop at returns / unconditional `b` / `bl` to a no-return import / proven literal-pool data, extend over `ldr`/`vldr [pc,#imm]` literals) instead of "the distance to the next known start", and a function whose reachability is fully proven may exceed the old 0x4000 clamp (up to 0x8000). Any control flow the walk cannot follow (indirect jump that is not a call, path off the extent, or an instruction that STRADDLES the end of an extent bounded by a 2-mod-4 start) leaves the old extent; a function with `.ARM.extab` data is never trimmed below its own `.ARM.exidx` range (C++ landing pads are entered only by the unwinder, so the walk cannot see them), and a start strictly inside such a function is not trimmed at all. The two BL/branch-target loops also run to a fixpoint (cap 64 rounds, was 8 and 4; rounds beyond the old caps admit only ARM-dense 4-aligned targets) because over-extended lifts had been discovering callees breadth-first by accident. Over-extended lifts recorded foreign branch targets as function starts in the middle of real functions (Joyride 0xcabe0: a 0xe4-byte function lifted as 0x4000 bytes minted 0xcebe8/0xcf0c0, which capped the real 18 KB 0xcd9e0, whose join blocks then became starts). `=0` restores the old extents and round caps byte for byte. | fix (docs/arm32-false-split-thumb-coincidence_2026-09-30.md; gate arm32falsesplit R1/E1) |
| MACAPK_ARM32_DISCOVERY_ROUNDS | 64 (only read with REACH_EXTENT on) | int | DIAGNOSTIC cap for the two ARM32 discovery BL/branch-target loops (default 64 rounds, each ends early at a fixpoint). The arm32falsesplit gate's C7 positive control sets it to 2 to show the real callees 0x37fdc4/0x37f6ec/0x1932f8/... are then left unlifted. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ARM32_REACH_EH_FLOOR | ON (2026-10-01) | 0/1 | DIAGNOSTIC (not a shipping toggle; `MACAPK_ARM32_REACH_EXTENT=0` stays the old path): `=0` drops the .ARM.exidx EH-aware floor of the reach walk. The arm32falsesplit gate's C10 positive control sets it to show hcr181 v7a then loses 0x24aa80 / 0x24d8b0 again (two exidx-confirmed functions called only from basic_filebuf catch handlers). | bisect |
| MACAPK_ARM32_REACH_STRADDLE | OFF (2026-10-01) | 0/1 | DIAGNOSTIC (not a shipping toggle): `=1` restores the pre-review guard `pc >= cap` of the reach walk, so an instruction that straddles the end of an extent bounded by a 2-mod-4 start is READ (Joyride 0x464e30 grows 0x3e -> 0x40) instead of bailing; `seen[]` gets one spare slot so this arm is not itself out of bounds. The gate's C9 positive control. The default guard `pc + 4 > cap` fixes an out-of-bounds `seen[]` index (ASan heap-buffer-overflow, review of 6028ce2f). | bisect |
| MACAPK_PATH_CWD_ROOT | ON (2026-09-30) | 0/1 | A RELATIVE guest path whose "/" form lies inside the android root is resolved from "/" (an Android app process's cwd is "/": no chdir() in Zygote.cpp), not as a bare name under `${android_root}/data/data/<pkg>/`. Jetpack Joyride (Halfbrick Mortar) strips the leading '/' of getCacheDir(); the bare-name rule produced `<root>/data/data/<pkg>/Users/<u>/.../<root>/data/data/<pkg>/cache/...` so every texture-cache mkdir/fopen(w+b) failed ENOENT. Every other relative name keeps the old rule. The rule ends once the guest has chdir()/fchdir()'d (relative paths are then relative to THAT directory, as POSIX says; gate CW3, control CW4 = `MACAPK_RELPATH_CWD=0`, which never tracks the guest cwd so the rule stays unconditional). An ARM32 guest's `chdir` import is not shimmed (pre-existing), so only arm64 guests move that flag today. `=0` restores the old rule. | fix (docs/arm32-false-split-thumb-coincidence_2026-09-30.md; gate arm32falsesplit CW1-CW4) |
| MACAPK_ARM32_TERM_NORETURN_PLT | ON (2026-09-30) | 0/1 | data_fnptr seed site only: an unconditional ARM `bl` into the PLT stub of a no-return import (bionic `__noreturn`: abort, exit, _Exit, quick_exit, _exit, pthread_exit, __assert, __assert2, longjmp, _longjmp, siglongjmp; the compiler's __stack_chk_fail and _Unwind_Resume; libc++abi __cxa_throw/__cxa_rethrow/__cxa_bad_cast/__cxa_bad_typeid/__cxa_throw_bad_array_new_length/__cxa_pure_virtual) counts as the previous function's terminator in the walk-back, exactly as `bl <in-image throw helper>` already did. `=0` restores the old walk-back. | fix (docs/arm32-false-split-thumb-coincidence_2026-09-30.md; gate arm32falsesplit) |
| MACAPK_ARM32_TERM_POOL_WALK | ON (2026-09-30) | 0/1 | data_fnptr seed site only: once the walk-back's 8-step heuristic budget is spent, a PROVABLE ARM literal-pool word (our LDR-PC scan dereferences it) is stepped over without spending it, up to 1024 words (the 4 KB LDR literal reach). `=0` restores the old walk-back. | fix (docs/arm32-false-split-thumb-coincidence_2026-09-30.md; gate arm32falsesplit) |
| MACAPK_ARM32_FNPTR_ADD_WINDOW | ON (2026-09-30) | 0/1 | elf_reader's ARM PIC fnptr scan looks for the `add Rt,pc,Rt` up to 256 bytes after its `ldr Rt,[pc,#imm]` (was 36), stopping past the first 36 bytes at any branch, LDM/POP of Rt, or instruction naming Rt in bits 15-12 / multiply Rd, and at a function exit (`ldm ..,{..,pc}`, a data-processing or load instruction with Rd = PC) so the window never runs into the next function. Jetpack Joyride's member-function pointer 0x18c838 is materialised 56 bytes after its load. `=0` restores the 36-byte window. | fix (docs/arm32-false-split-thumb-coincidence_2026-09-30.md; gate arm32falsesplit) |
| MACAPK_ARM32_STAT_LAYOUT | ON (2026-09-30) | 0/1 | The ARM32 stat/lstat/fstat shims write bionic's `__arm__` struct stat (104 bytes, libc/include/sys/stat.h: st_mode @0x10, st_size @0x30, st_blocks @0x40, timespecs @0x48/0x50/0x58, st_ino @0x60; pinned by static_asserts) field by field. The old fill zeroed 96 bytes and wrote st_mode @0x0c and st_size @0x28, so every ARM32 guest read st_mode 0 and st_size 0. `=0` restores the old fill byte for byte. | fix (docs/arm32-false-split-thumb-coincidence_2026-09-30.md) |
| MACAPK_ARM32_POOL_ESCAPE_828 | enabled (the v1.0.828 literal-pool "escape" carve-out is active) | 0/1 | Turns off the v1.0.828 discovery carve-out that admits a candidate on the strength of "the ARM literal pool does not mark it", so the escape's false-positive rate can be measured by diffing discovery sidecars with it on vs. off. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ARM32_POOL_SKIP_NV | ON | `0` | ARM32 discovery: the ARM half of the literal-pool scan skips words whose cond field is 0b1111 (ARM ARM A5.1 Table A5-1: those are Unconditional instructions, never an LDR/LDRD/VLDR literal; the only literal load in that space is generic-coprocessor LDC2 (literal), which Android code never uses, and the VLDR mask's cond=1111 match is never a VFP load), so Thumb code seen through a 4-byte window stops marking real code as pool data. Victim: GD Lite 2.2.11 v7a `libcocos2dcpp.so`, where the Thumb-2 pair at 0x4737fc read as ARM "LDRD literal of 0x473758" and the static `splitWithForm` helper was lifted with a 4-byte extent (plus a bogus start at 0x473780), so every CCPoint/Size/RectFromString parsed an empty vector. Discovery delta on the sweep libs: GD Lite cocos 2/6/18 (removed/added/extent-changed), GD Lite libfmod 1/0/4 alone (see `MACAPK_ARM32_STRW_TAIL_NOT_POP`), the other 8 unchanged. `=0`: the old scan byte for byte. Translation-time. v1.0.1655. Gate `poolnv`. | fix (docs/arm32-pool-nv-blxlr-setenv_2026-09-27.md) |
| MACAPK_ARM32_SETENV_SHIM | ON | `0` | ARM32 translator binds `setenv` / `unsetenv` / `putenv` / `clearenv` to new pointer-aware `__macapk_shim_<name>` (bionic_shim.cpp, beside `__macapk_shim_getenv`), which write the ONE guest environment Stage 6 and Java `Os.setenv` share (`__macapk_arm64_setenv` & co; Bionic OpenBSD semantics; `MACAPK_GUEST_ENV_MUTABLE=0` falls back to the host calls; clearenv then empties the HOST environment, the one the ARM32 getenv reads in that mode). Before, libSystem got the raw 32-bit guest pointer: OpenTTD v7a `libsdl_main` nativeInit SEGV at host `setenv+0x1c`. `=0`: binds the bare libSystem names exactly as before (clearenv unresolved). Translation-time. v1.0.1655. Gate `setenv32`. | fix (docs/arm32-pool-nv-blxlr-setenv_2026-09-27.md) |
| MACAPK_ARM32_SIGJMP | ON | `0` | ARM32 translator binds the whole Bionic setjmp family (`setjmp`, `_setjmp`, `sigsetjmp`, `longjmp`, `_longjmp`, `siglongjmp`) to real `__macapk_shim_<name>_bionic` twins. They save the recompiled caller's state (guest r4-r11/sp on x19-x27, x28, the host x29/x30/sp/d8-d15, the guest d8-d15 + fpscr from GuestState) into the guest's OWN 256-byte Bionic arm `jmp_buf` (setjmp.S word layout, cookie-mangled, checksummed; host continuation in the reserved words 32-63), honour savemask, and on longjmp replay any skipped `__macapk_invoke_safe` returns and the libsigchain note. A setjmp not reached by an arm32rc direct call (`mov w4, w27 ; bl`) is recorded UNARMED and its longjmp takes the old abort-the-call path. Before: `sigsetjmp` bound libSystem's (OpenTTD v7a libcrypto init SEGV at `sigsetjmp+0x0`), and setjmp returned 0 / longjmp aborted the whole invoke_safe call. Importers (v7a corpus): setjmp 21, longjmp 19, sigsetjmp 6, siglongjmp 6 -- incl. HCR 1.43/1.8.1 and GD Lite (libpng). `=0`: the fake pair and a raw libSystem sigsetjmp, byte-identical dylib. Translation-time. Lane arm32-shim-gaps. Gate `arm32shimgaps`. | fix (docs/arm32-shim-gaps-sigjmp-memalign-libm_2026-09-28.md) |
| MACAPK_ARM32_ALIGNED_ALLOC | ON | `0` | ARM32 translator binds `posix_memalign` to `__macapk_shim_posix_memalign` and `memalign` to `__macapk_shim_memalign_heap`, both allocating from the ARM32 guest heap (over-allocate by align-8, give the lead and a splittable tail back, move the live record to the aligned start so free/realloc find it). posix_memalign follows the scudo contract Bionic returns unchanged: EINVAL for 0 / non-power-of-two / not a multiple of 4, ENOMEM on failure, errno untouched, memptr written only on success; memalign rounds the alignment up (0 -> 1). Before: libSystem posix_memalign wrote an 8-byte host pointer through the 32-bit guest memptr (OpenTTD v7a SEGV in `_posix_memalign`, fault 0x3e0fff58), and the old memalign shim took HOST memory and could only return 0. Importers: posix_memalign 32, memalign 11 v7a libs. `=0`: the old bindings, byte-identical dylib. Translation-time. Gate `arm32shimgaps`. | fix (docs/arm32-shim-gaps-sigjmp-memalign-libm_2026-09-28.md) |
| MACAPK_ARM32_SOFTFP_LIBM | ON | `0` | ARM32 translator binds 44 libm / fpclassify names that no softfp set covered to softfp `__macapk_shim_<name>` twins: asinh, expm1, log1p, logb, hypot, remainder, scalbn, nan, llrint, llround, llrintf, llroundf (64-bit results, split into r0:r1), lrint, lround, lrintf, modff, and all of bionic `fpclassify.cpp` (`__fpclassify{,d,f,l}`, `[__]is{inf,nan,finite,normal}{,f,l}`, returning BIONIC's FP_* values 0x01/0x02/0x04/0x08/0x10). Before they bound libSystem's hardfp versions (argument read from s0/d0, result returned in s0/d0: garbage both ways). Corpus importers: GD Lite 2.2.11 (`__isnanf`, `__fpclassifyd`, `asinh`), Telegram, Signal, Subway, Dumb Ways, Fossify Gallery, Wikipedia, Organic Maps, AnarchRE, Rocks'n'Diamonds. `=0`: the bare libSystem names, byte-identical dylib. Translation-time. Gate `arm32shimgaps`. | fix (docs/arm32-shim-gaps-sigjmp-memalign-libm_2026-09-28.md) |
| MACAPK_ARM32RC_POOL_RESYNC | ON | `0` | arm32rc: a Thumb intra-function branch target the linear capstone sweep swallowed (the covering instruction starts in a byte the runtime's literal-pool BYTE SCAN marks -- a blind heuristic, not a proof: it also marks the 2nd halfword of an ordinary 32-bit Thumb-2 insn) is re-decoded from the target and spliced over the misaligned run, so the branch gets a label instead of a panic stub. The branch SOURCE is gated too (skipped if it is itself inside a marked byte, or is lld's 0xd4d4 trap fill), added after two gdlite-v7a miscompiles (libfmod FIR loop, libcocos2dcpp jpeg_idct_12x12) traced to trusting an un-gated branch decoded from a false-positive pool byte. Victim: OpenTTD liblzma's LZMA decoder `beq.w 0x172e0` past pool word 0x172dc (LZMA_DATA_ERROR on input boundaries). `=0` byte-identical. | fix (docs/arm32-exidx-confirmed-seeds_2026-09-28.md) |
| MACAPK_ARM32RC_POOL_RESYNC_SRCFILTER | ON | `0` | arm32rc: drops JUST the POOL_RESYNC branch-source checks above (leaving POOL_RESYNC itself on), reproducing the narrower pre-fix filter that only checked the covering instruction at the TARGET and trusted any branch, however decoded, as the source. A regression-testing knob, not a supported deployment configuration -- it exists so `lzmadec_gate.sh`'s C4 can reproduce the two confirmed miscompiles (gdlite-v7a libfmod FIR loop 694->687 insns, libcocos2dcpp jpeg_idct_12x12 3596->3584 insns) from the CURRENT binary, without needing a second translator built from pre-fix source. | diagnostic (docs/arm32-exidx-confirmed-seeds_2026-09-28.md) |
| MACAPK_ARM32_CRIT_RELEASE_DEFER | ON | `0` | ARM32 JNI: a mode-0 `ReleasePrimitiveArrayCritical` inside an ARM32 native keeps the guest copy (plus a snapshot) until that native returns or the thread's next critical Get, then copies the bytes the guest wrote AFTER Release into the array and frees it -- ART's direct-pointer outcome (ART never copies a critical array). Needs `MACAPK_ARM32_HEAP_COPY_LIFETIME` on. Victim: OpenTTD `XZInputStream.nativeRead` stores offsets after Release (LZMA error 9). `=0` frees at Release as before. | fix (docs/arm32-exidx-confirmed-seeds_2026-09-28.md) |
| MACAPK_ARM32_CRIT_GLOBALREF | ON | `0` | ARM32 JNI: CRIT_RELEASE_DEFER's queued retirement takes a GLOBAL ref on the array at Release time, before the native can call DeleteLocalRef (or PopLocalFrame) on its own local ref -- a deleted local ref is invalid immediately (JNI spec), so retiring through it after the guest deletes it is a use-after-free of a JNI handle (measured: SIGABRT). `=0` defers with the raw local ref instead, reproducing that crash -- the first cut of CRIT_RELEASE_DEFER, kept only so `critdefer32_gate.sh`'s C2 can demonstrate the bug this fixes. | fix (docs/arm32-exidx-confirmed-seeds_2026-09-28.md) |
| MACAPK_ARM32_LLD_THUNKS | ON | `0` | ARM32 translator: lld's `ThumbV7PILongThunk` (`movw ip; movt ip; add ip,pc; bx ip`) and `ARMV7PILongThunk` become Veneer forwarders in their OWN mode (target decoded from movw/movt; a target that is this image's own local PLT stub is forwarded to the definition). Before, every Thumb thunk was translated as ARM (100% in the libs measured) and a tail call through it returned r0 unchanged (OpenTTD liblzma `lzma_alloc` returned its size). Corpus: 9,803 Thumb + 9,085 ARM thunks in 149 of 246 libs. `=0` byte-identical. | fix (docs/arm32-exidx-confirmed-seeds_2026-09-28.md) |
| MACAPK_ARM32_SEED_EXIDX_CONFIRM | ON | `0` | ARM32 discovery: a NON-authoritative heuristic function-pointer seed (Thumb-16 PIC scan, Thumb-2 PIC scan, ARM BL-argument idiom) that the terminator-precedence walk-back rejects is admitted when `.ARM.exidx` names its address as a function start (the confirmation exidx already gave authoritative seeds, v1.0.806). Nothing is seeded FROM exidx. Victim: OpenTTD v7a liblzma `stream_decode` 0x119b4 (behind a 9-word literal pool; the `blx lr` callback). Corpus: 11,965 of 15,421 rejected heuristic seeds in 246 ARM32 libs. `=0` byte-identical. | fix (docs/arm32-exidx-confirmed-seeds_2026-09-28.md) |
| MACAPK_ARM32_SHARED_TAIL_ENTRY | off (a shared-tail branch target interior to another function is not published as its own entry) | 0/1 | Publishes an interior branch target as an independent function entry when linker evidence (an ARM-mode `STT_FUNC` symbol) proves it is a real shared-tail/continuation, fixing e.g. `__mulsf3`'s tail so calls into it stop resolving to a return-0 stub. | experiment |
| MACAPK_ARM32_SIZELESS_EXPORTS | ON | `0` | ARM32 translator: a defined GLOBAL/WEAK `STT_FUNC` `.dynsym` export with `st_size == 0` (hand-written assembly with no `.size`: libc++abi's `__cxa_end_cleanup`, libgcc's `__gnu_Unwind_Save_VFP*` / `__aeabi_uidiv`, FMOD's `*_VFP` mixers) becomes a discovery seed of its own, in the instruction set its own `st_value` bit 0 declares (AAELF32 "Symbol Values"), sized by the next-known-start rule, exempt from the inside-a-known-body filter. Discovery only lifted SIZED symbols, so such a function was translated only if another seed reached it, then with a heuristic mode and an extent that ran on into the next helper; `MACAPK_ARM32_EXPORT_ALIASES` then exports its ELF name. Corpus: 485 in 77 of 246 libs (429 addresses not already a sized symbol's start). Measured on the sweep-row libs (default vs `=0`): no mode flips except GD Lite `libfmod.so`, whose three `FMOD_DSP_Connection_*_VFP` ARM mixers were decoded as THUMB (and spawned 63 phantom Thumb functions) at HEAD; 9-11 extents shortened per libgcc image (HCR v7a `__text` -3.5 KB, Flappy 23,056 -> 19,636 B); 1 function added in HCR armeabi, JJ and GD Lite. `=0`: the pre-change seed list exactly. Translation-time. 2026-09-27 (ALIAS lane). Gate `arm32alias` rows S1/C2/C3. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ARM32_STRW_TAIL_NOT_POP | ON | `0` | ARM32 discovery prologue-boundary scan: a Thumb16 `sub sp,#imm` / `push` (no lr) candidate whose two preceding halfwords are `str.w Rt,[sp,#-4]!` (f84d Rt:d04, clang's r11 save) is NOT an entry -- the store's second halfword reads as `pop {r2,pc}`, which the precedence check took for a return. Unmasked by `MACAPK_ARM32_POOL_SKIP_NV` (a false NV mark had hidden GD Lite libfmod 0x4d748, so the NV fix alone capped 0x4d740 at 8 bytes and lost 0x4d184), and a defect of its own: libfmod had 112 routines truncated to 8 bytes with no lifted tail (0 with the guard); Mindustry libarc 11. Other 8 sweep libs unchanged. `=0`: the old scan. Translation-time. v1.0.1655. Gate `poolnv` rows F1/C2/C3/F2 (C2/C3 also set `MACAPK_ARM32_THUMB_PARITY=0`, which covers the same tail on its own since 2026-09-28; the guard stays: a false Thumb pool mark on the f84d (GD Lite libfmod 0xd6740) and 2 organicmaps sites admitted via the pool-mark walk-back are only caught by it). | fix (docs/arm32-pool-nv-blxlr-setenv_2026-09-27.md) |
| MACAPK_ARM32_SYNTH_TRUNCATE | off (the corrected synth-name-length truncation check does not run; a 7-weeks-silently-disabled behaviour stays disabled) | 0/1 | Re-enables (for A/B against the protected sweep) a `__macapk_synth_` name-length comparison that has been dead since 2026-06-06 due to a length-mismatch bug, restoring the "corrected" behaviour that several later literal-pool carve-outs were calibrated without. | experiment |
| MACAPK_ARM32_THUMB_PARITY | ON (2026-09-28) | `0` | ARM32 discovery: a Thumb-16 terminator / NOP reading (pop {..,pc}, bx lr, b imm11, bx Rm, nop) of the halfword before a candidate, and a Thumb-2 terminator reading of the word before it, is refused when that halfword is the SECOND halfword of a 32-bit Thumb-2 instruction -- decided by the parity of the run of halfwords >= 0xE800 before it (ARM ARM A6.1; LLVM ARMDisassembler "2-byte iff < 0xE800"). Also in the throw-helper back-walk. Generalises the str.w-r11 idiom (`MACAPK_ARM32_STRW_TAIL_NOT_POP`, which stays: 2 corpus sites it catches come from the pool-mark walk-back, not a tail). A Thumb literal-pool mark ends the run (without that, 256 real entries admitted by coincidental readings inside pools were lost). Corpus 246 libs, rebased onto MACAPK_ARM32_SEED_EXIDX_CONFIRM/_LLD_THUNKS/ARM32RC_POOL_RESYNC (already in main): both gates fns -5,874, EXHIT +47, EXINT -3,058 (EXINT redefined 2026-09-28 to EXTAB-ref entries only, a lower bound -- lld's isDuplicateArmExidxSec also merges identical inline-unwind entries), ARMMIS -4,103; parity alone (TERM_THUMB_SEED=0) fns -6,103, EXHIT +45, EXINT -3,039, ARMMIS -4,076, no lib shows either indicator increasing. Prints `[translator] thumb-parity: refused N ...` (-v lists them). `=0`: byte-identical to the previous translator. Translation-time. Gate `thumbparity` (11 rows: P0/R1/R1c/R2/R2c/R3/S1/S1c/S2/S2c/D1/B1). | fix (docs/arm32-thumb-parity-and-thumb-seed_2026-09-28.md) |
| MACAPK_ARM32_TERM_THUMB_SEED | ON (2026-09-28; was an OFF diagnostic since v1.0.1053) | `0` | ARM32 discovery, data_fnptr seed site: a Thumb-TAGGED seed whose own bytes open with PUSH {..,lr} (ARM ARM A8.8.133 T1 0xb5xx / T2 e92d with LR / T3 f84d ed04) is exempt from the arm_prior veto (the 4-aligned word before it has an ARM AL/UNCOND top nibble, so its Thumb-16 `b` terminator was discarded as an ARM coincidence). The old =1 exempted EVERY Thumb-tagged seed and admitted 24 phantoms in GD Lite's ARM-mode range (+~1,000 cascade functions); that arm is gone. GD Lite v7a +102 Thumb starts (0x4cb2a4 / 0x4dff64 / 0x4f3924 ...), 0 in the ARM range, 0 exidx-confirmed among the 102. Corpus-wide (rebased onto MACAPK_ARM32_SEED_EXIDX_CONFIRM, already in main) this gate's OWN incremental contribution is 378 added starts / 246 libs, of which only 3 (0.8%) are independently .ARM.exidx-confirmed -- most of what the old measurement credited to this gate (e.g. "HCR 1.8.1 v7a +17, 15 exidx-confirmed") is a combined-with-THUMB_PARITY figure that does not decompose additively (parity alone adds 0 on that lib, seed alone adds 2; see docs/arm32-thumb-parity-and-thumb-seed_2026-09-28.md section 3/4 for the full attribution). Only 9 of 18 real Thumb entries GD Lite v7a was known to be missing are recovered by this narrowing (section 3 lists the other 9 and why: zero-padding step-back, long offset tables, and non-push-lr leaf entries the rule correctly has no evidence for). `=0`: the uniform veto byte for byte. Translation-time. Gate `thumbparity` S1/S1c/S2/S2c (S2c: positive control, --base OLD + this var forced =1 puts >0 starts in the ARM-mode range, proving S2 is not blind). | fix (docs/arm32-thumb-parity-and-thumb-seed_2026-09-28.md) |

| MACAPK_GUEST_CLOCK_NANOTIME | On (since 2026-09-28) -- ONE monotonic clock, as on Android: guest clock_gettime CLOCK_MONOTONIC / MONOTONIC_RAW / MONOTONIC_COARSE read CLOCK_UPTIME_RAW (mach_absolute_time == HotSpot's System.nanoTime(), ns resolution), CLOCK_BOOTTIME / BOOTTIME_ALARM read CLOCK_MONOTONIC_RAW (mach_continuous_time), REALTIME_ALARM reads CLOCK_REALTIME, both tiers (arm64 + ARM32); no libunity process rebase; FUTEX_WAIT_BITSET, clock_nanosleep(TIMER_ABSTIME) and the cond-deadline converter measure a guest MONOTONIC deadline on that clock; Java SystemClock.uptimeMillis/uptimeNanos == System.nanoTime(), elapsedRealtime(Nanos) == CLOCK_BOOTTIME (NativeLog.nativeBootTimeNanos); Process.getStartUptimeMillis/getStartElapsedRealtime now real (Boot.java calls Process.setStartTimes before Application construction); SensorEvent.timestamp uses elapsedRealtimeNanos(), matching AOSP. Fixes Crossy Time.unscaledTime ~ host uptime | `0` = the previous regime byte for byte: macOS CLOCK_MONOTONIC (gettimeofday - boottime, us resolution, 7 s off System.nanoTime on a never-slept host), the libunity rebase, REALTIME-measured clock_nanosleep / host-MONOTONIC futex deadlines, process-relative SystemClock, Process.getStart*=0, SensorEvent.timestamp=System.nanoTime() | Rollback / A/B for the clock unification. ⚠ `=0` ALONE IS NOT THE OLD REGIME if MACAPK_CHOREO_NANOTIME_BASE is left at its own default ON: the Choreographer frame clock stays in the System.nanoTime() base while this flag rebases the guest's native CLOCK_MONOTONIC process-relative, which is the same disagreement this lane fixed, with the opposite sign. The old regime needs BOTH flags at `0` together (see the MACAPK_CHOREO_NANOTIME_BASE row).
