IGNORED` line unless that gate is 0.** Overrides which guest function entry the completer-core delay trap installs at, for retargeting the MCPE 54%-stall race-confirmation probe to a different binary's equivalent site. | experiment |
| MACAPK_COMPLETER_DELAY_ARGS | off (no argument/field dump on a delay-trap hit) | 1 | **Inert by default (2026-09-27): a sub-knob consulted only after a member of the retired MCPE sign-in probe family armed, so it has no effect unless `MACAPK_MCPE_SIGNIN_PROBES_RETIRED=0`.** Dumps the trapped completer-core call's arguments and a handful of guarded pointer-chain dereferences (task selector/affinity fields) on each printed hit, instead of just a bare `HIT #N`. | diag |
| MACAPK_COMPLETER_DELAY_CHAIN | none (no extra chain walk) | `<hexbase>:<hexoff1>,<hexoff2>,...` | **Inert by default (2026-09-27): a sub-knob consulted only after a member of the retired MCPE sign-in probe family armed, so it has no effect unless `MACAPK_MCPE_SIGNIN_PROBES_RETIRED=0`.** Follows one pointer out of the completer-delay trap's first argument (`*(x0+baseoff)`) and prints fields of that object, reaching state one hop away from the trapped call's own arguments (e.g. a TaskGroup's WorkerPool). | diag |
| MACAPK_COMPLETER_DELAY_EVERY | 0 (only the first 64 hits print) | integer N | **Inert by default (2026-09-27): a sub-knob consulted only after a member of the retired MCPE sign-in probe family armed, so it has no effect unless `MACAPK_MCPE_SIGNIN_PROBES_RETIRED=0`.** Samples every Nth completer-delay-trap hit for printing instead of only the first 64, so a hot call site's behaviour LATER in a run (not just at arm time) can be observed. | diag |
| MACAPK_COMPLETER_DELAY_OBJSTR | none (the queued task's own name is not printed) | hex offset | **Inert by default (2026-09-27): a sub-knob consulted only after a member of the retired MCPE sign-in probe family armed, so it has no effect unless `MACAPK_MCPE_SIGNIN_PROBES_RETIRED=0`.** Decodes and prints a libc++ std::string living inside the completer-delay trap's queued object at the given offset -- the task's own NAME, as opposed to PATHSTR's worker name. | diag |
| MACAPK_COMPLETER_DELAY_OFFS | none (no extra field dump) | comma-separated hex offsets, up to 8 | **Inert by default (2026-09-27): a sub-knob consulted only after a member of the retired MCPE sign-in probe family armed, so it has no effect unless `MACAPK_MCPE_SIGNIN_PROBES_RETIRED=0`.** Prints extra byte/u32 fields read out of the completer-delay trap's target object at the given offsets, so the "interesting offset" can move without a rebuild. | diag |
| MACAPK_COMPLETER_DELAY_PATH | none (no pointer-path walk from x0) | `<h1,h2,...>:<f1,f2,...>` | **Inert by default (2026-09-27): a sub-knob consulted only after a member of the retired MCPE sign-in probe family armed, so it has no effect unless `MACAPK_MCPE_SIGNIN_PROBES_RETIRED=0`.** Walks a pointer path from the completer-delay trap's x0 argument (printing every intermediate hop) and then dumps fields at the given offsets off the final object, so a mis-specified hop is visible instead of reading as a confident wrong answer. | diag |
| MACAPK_COMPLETER_DELAY_PATHSTR | none (no worker-name string print) | hex offset | **Inert by default (2026-09-27): a sub-knob consulted only after a member of the retired MCPE sign-in probe family armed, so it has no effect unless `MACAPK_MCPE_SIGNIN_PROBES_RETIRED=0`.** Prints bytes at `path-end + offset` as ASCII, decoding the WORKER's inline name (e.g. "Streaming Pool", "REST Thread(0)") that a completer-delay-trap path landed on. | diag |
| MACAPK_COMPLETER_DELAY_THREAD | none (delay-trap hits are not filtered/printed by thread) | substring of a thread name | **Inert by default (2026-09-27): a sub-knob consulted only after a member of the retired MCPE sign-in probe family armed, so it has no effect unless `MACAPK_MCPE_SIGNIN_PROBES_RETIRED=0`.** Filters the completer-delay trap's printed hits to only those firing on a thread whose name contains the given substring, separating a hot generic call site's interesting caller from thousands of uninteresting ones. | diag |
| MACAPK_COMPLETER_DELAY_US | disarmed (no completer-core delay trap installed) | integer microseconds | **RETIRED by default (2026-09-27): read through `MACAPK_MCPE_SIGNIN_PROBES_RETIRED`, ignored with one `[mcpe-probes] ... IGNORED` line unless that gate is 0.** Installs a trap on MCPE's completion-resume prologue that sleeps for the given microseconds before continuing, testing whether delaying the completer core's drain lets a race-losing schedule-continuation register in time to avoid the 54% sign-in stall. | experiment |
| MACAPK_COMPLETION_PUMP | off (the guest's own per-frame Completion-port dispatch is unassisted) | any set value | **RETIRED by default (2026-09-27): read through `MACAPK_MCPE_SIGNIN_PROBES_RETIRED`, ignored with one `[mcpe-probes] ... IGNORED` line unless that gate is 0.** Captures MCPE's HTTP-completion XTaskQueue handle and XTaskQueueDispatch veneer from the guest's own per-frame pump, then runs a dedicated host thread that keeps dispatching that Completion port even while MINECRAFT MAIN is blocked in the sign-in wait, so late HTTP completions are not stranded. | experiment |
| MACAPK_COND_TIMEDWAIT_REPOLL_MS | off (only the 30-day "effectively infinite" clamp applies) | integer ms | Clamps ANY `cond_timedwait` deadline longer than the given cap and returns a spurious wake on that timeout, letting a lost/mis-timed `notify_one` fail safe; REFUTED for the MCPE stall by a drift-controlled A/B (0/10 vs 3/10 -- made it worse), kept only for other lock-free-queue apps. | experiment |
| MACAPK_COND_WAIT_CAP_MS | 250 ms | integer ms (0 disables the cap) | Caps how long an "effectively infinite" (>30-day) `cond_timedwait` deadline (e.g. webrtc's `rtc::Event::Wait(kForever)`) actually parks before returning a POSIX-legal spurious wake, so a lost/mis-routed notify cannot hang a worker forever. | bisect |
| MACAPK_COND_WAIT_INF_MS | off (a plain, non-timed `pthread_cond_wait` parks truly infinitely) | integer ms (0 = true-infinite) | Caps every infinite (non-timed) `pthread_cond_wait` at the given bound and returns a spurious wake on timeout, so a lock-free-queue worker with a lost wakeup re-polls its queue instead of sleeping forever; shipped default-off since it did not resolve the MCPE 54% stall in testing. | experiment |
| MACAPK_CORO_PUMP | RETIRED 2026-09-27: ignored by default (MACAPK_REGISTERNATIVES_VERBATIM); under =0, enabled (Unity's coroutine player-loop node is driven every frame) | 0, read only under MACAPK_REGISTERNATIVES_VERBATIM=0 | Under MACAPK_REGISTERNATIVES_VERBATIM=0 only: disables the structurally-discovered Unity player-loop coroutine-node pump. By default nothing calls it: the engine drives that node itself (v1.0.322 census), so the pump only ever ran it a second time. | bisect |
| MACAPK_DATA_SUBDIR | not propagated (the elfexec helper falls back to its own default data-subdir resolution) | path/subdir string | Propagates the app's data subdirectory to a forked APK-native-executable helper process (via inherited environment), so the helper resolves the same per-app data directory as the parent runtime. | dev |
| MACAPK_DC_NO_BACKPRESSURE | off (real AOSP possession-based backpressure: the guest producer waits for a free slot) | any set value | Restores the old "newest-wins" decouple-ring behaviour (producer never blocks, consumer drops stale frames) instead of the real BufferQueue-style backpressure that re-imposes the guest-swap-blocks-until-a-slot-frees contract. Since 2026-09-27 reachable only under MACAPK_VK_WINDOW_SURFACE=0 (the default guest surface is a real window surface, so the decouple ring never activates). | bisect |
| MACAPK_DEBUG_AUTOCLICK | off (no synthetic clicks) | `t[:x,y][;t2[:x2,y2]]...` | Schedules one or more synthetic mouse down/up sequences at given elapsed seconds (and optional view-point), for headless test-harness verification of touch dispatch. | diag |
| MACAPK_DEBUG_AUTOCLICK_DRAG_PX | 0 (a synthetic tap is perfectly stationary) | integer pixels, [0,100) | Adds a small move between a synthetic tap's down and up events, to defeat an engine's (e.g. cocos2d-x v2 ScrollView) dead-stationary-delta classification that would otherwise reject the synthetic tap as a scroll-cancel. | diag |
| MACAPK_DEBUG_AUTOKEY | off (no synthetic key events) | `<t>:<chars>` | Types each character in `<chars>` as a staggered synthetic key down+up sequence at elapsed time `<t>`, through the real keyDown: dispatch path, for headless keyboard-injection testing. | diag |
| MACAPK_DEBUG_AUTOROTATE | off (no synthetic rotation) | comma/space/semicolon-separated seconds | Fires `CocoaRotateRender()` once at each given elapsed time since first frame, letting Cmd+R-style rotation be verified headlessly on a non-frontmost custom-NSApplication window. | diag |
| MACAPK_DEBUG_TAP_HOLD | off (a synthetic click fires down+up in the same frame) | float seconds (non-numeric value = 0.12 default) | Splits a synthetic tap's down and up across separate frames (held for the given seconds) so a Unity uGUI button, which does not register a same-frame down+up as a click, actually sees a press/release. | diag |
| MACAPK_DECOUPLE_NO_THROTTLE | off (a bounded 2-PBO async readback throttles the pixel-readback ring under backlog) | any set value | Restores the old v612 unconditional every-swap 3-deep PBO ring, ignoring the throttle that presents a stale loading-screen frame under genuine GPU backlog; A/B lever. Since 2026-09-27 reachable only under MACAPK_VK_WINDOW_SURFACE=0 (the default guest surface is a real window surface, so the decouple ring never activates). | bisect |
| MACAPK_DECOUPLE_PUBRATE | off (no publish-rate logging) | any set value | Logs the guest's actual unique-frame publish rate through the decouple path once every 5 seconds -- the true signal for whether the v618 throttle is dropping guest frames (the host FPS counter cannot reveal that). Since 2026-09-27 reachable only under MACAPK_VK_WINDOW_SURFACE=0 (the default guest surface is a real window surface, so the decouple ring never activates). | diag |
| MACAPK_DECOUPLE_SYNC | off (glReadPixels into a PBO is async; a prior frame's fence is polled non-blocking) | any set value | Restores the old SYNCHRONOUS `glReadPixels` readback (blocking on `vkWaitForFences`) instead of the async PBO+fence pipeline that releases the guest GL-context mutex promptly; A/B lever for the v612 54%-wedge fix. Since 2026-09-27 reachable only under MACAPK_VK_WINDOW_SURFACE=0 (the default guest surface is a real window surface, so the decouple ring never activates). | bisect |
| MACAPK_DIAG_SKIP_BL | none (every real decoded direct-call target inside a lifted function's own extent is admitted) | comma-separated hex vaddrs | Excludes the named mid-function `bl` targets from being lifted (reverting them to a return-0 panic stub), to confirm whether one specific thunk's body is what a crash or bad behaviour depends on. | diag |
| MACAPK_DIFF_FIRST_ONLY | off (each distinct instrumented function captures its own first invocation) | any set value | When no MACAPK_DIFF_TARGET is set, restricts the differential-capture harness's single global trace sink to only the very first function entered process-wide, instead of the first invocation of each instrumented function. | diag |
| MACAPK_DIFF_MAX_INSNS | 500000 lines | integer instruction count | Caps how many `__macapk_trace_regs` lines the differential-capture harness writes per captured function before stopping, bounding the trace file size for a long-running boot-path function. | diag |
| MACAPK_DIFF_OUT | disabled (capture/trace shims are no-ops) | directory path | Names the output directory the ARM32 Unicorn-differential capture harness writes entry snapshots, resident-memory dumps and per-instruction register traces into; unset, the whole capture mechanism costs nothing. | diag |
| MACAPK_DIFF_TARGET | none (the harness captures the first invocation of every instrumented function) | hex vaddr | Pins the differential-capture harness's single global trace sink to exactly one named function even when many functions in the dylib are instrumented, so a batch sweep's `.init_array` constructor doesn't grab the sink first. | diag |
| MACAPK_DIFF_TARGET_END | MACAPK_DIFF_TARGET + 0x8000 | hex vaddr | Bounds the address range the differential-capture harness records per-instruction register traces for, so a nested instrumented callee's trace does not pollute the target function's trace. | diag |
| MACAPK_DIFF_VADDRS | none (no functions are instrumented for the differential harness) | comma-separated hex vaddrs | On arm32rc (the only live ARM32 backend) it compiles one `__macapk_capture_entry` call (r0-r3, sp, is_thumb) at the entry of each named ARM32 function. Records are written only when MACAPK_DIFF_OUT=<dir> is set at run time. Instrumented dylibs get their own `-diff` cache slot, which is itself existence-based, so clear that slot when you change the vaddr list. The per-instruction `__macapk_trace_regs` stream for Unicorn single-stepping exists only in the LLVM-IR text and has had no runtime effect since v1.0.802. Use this to trace an engine entry such as cocos2d-x `CCActionManager::addAction` (HCR 1.8.1 `0x12176c`); the calloc(52,1)-size tripwire it replaced was refuted and deleted 2026-09-27 (`docs/cocos-actionmanager-tripwire-retired_2026-09-27.md`). | diag |
| MACAPK_DISABLE_PRIM_DETECT | off (a JNI arg is classified primitive-vs-pointer by inspecting its upper 32 bits) | 1 | Falls back to the pre-2026-05 behaviour where every non-env JNI argument is unconditionally minted as a handle, for A/B-testing whether a game's lifted code happened to render more correctly under the old "broken" dimension flow. | bisect |
| MACAPK_DISABLE_TRANSLATOR | disabled (the APK's native library is translated and loaded normally) | 1 / true (case-insensitive) | Skips native-library ABI translation and loading entirely and forces the framework's empty GLSurfaceView-stub fallback, used when a translated dylib crashes the JVM during native init and you want to see the activity's Java-side UI render instead. | bisect |
| MACAPK_DISCOVERY_SIDECAR | none (no ARM32 discovery `.disc.tsv` sidecar is written) | a path, or the literal "1"/"auto" (both mean `<output-dylib>.disc.tsv`) | Names the file the ARM32 discovery sidecar is written to, for callers (like the runtime's own translator subprocess launch) that fork the translator and cannot pass the equivalent `--emit-discovery` CLI flag. | diag |
| MACAPK_DRAIN_BASEQ | off (only the driver's derived-composite Completion queues are pumped, never the base process queue) | 0/1 (any nonzero) | **Inert by default (2026-09-27): a sub-knob consulted only after a member of the retired MCPE sign-in probe family armed, so it has no effect unless `MACAPK_MCPE_SIGNIN_PROBES_RETIRED=0`.** Makes the MAIN-side completion pump also dispatch MCPE's BASE process XTaskQueue's distinct Completion(+Work) port -- the actual home of the GS sign-in completion that the driver's composite-queue pump can never reach. | experiment |
| MACAPK_DRAWABLE_TIMEOUT_MS | 33 ms (two vsync intervals) | integer ms, [1,1000] | Tunes the hard timeout the bounded-`nextDrawable` acquire (Vulkan-ANGLE path) waits before giving up and returning nil, part of the A/B kill/tune switch pair for the CAMetalLayer-drawable-starvation fix. ⚠ Since 2026-10-01 the GUEST's own acquire (its MoltenVK submit, off the main thread) no longer takes this bound -- it is a dequeue (MACAPK_GUEST_DEQUEUE_FAITHFUL) -- so this knob only acts under MACAPK_GUEST_DEQUEUE_FAITHFUL=0, where a small value (e.g. 1) is the fastest way to reproduce the dropped-drawable freeze. | bisect |
| MACAPK_GUEST_DEQUEUE_FAITHFUL | ON (2026-10-01): the guest's own `-[CAMetalLayer nextDrawable]` on its native-EGL Vulkan window layer (its MoltenVK submit, off the main thread) is a DEQUEUE: it re-asks CoreAnimation (each ask waits up to CA's own ~1 s timeout) until a drawable comes, giving up only when the app is quitting (abandon is quit-only today; the native-EGL layer is never replaced, and a quit in progress takes the old bounded acquire so teardown cannot block) -- AOSP's unbounded dequeueBuffer (BufferQueueProducer mDequeueTimeout -1; libvulkan NATIVE_WINDOW_SET_DEQUEUE_TIMEOUT -1) | `0` = the 33 ms bounded acquire (MACAPK_DRAWABLE_TIMEOUT_MS) for the guest too, byte for byte | Fixes Minecraft's "freeze after a few minutes": a nil from the bound makes MoltenVK present the swapchain image with NO drawable (addPresentedHandler(nil): beginPresentation without endPresentation), the image is never made available again and the guest stops presenting for good while its game thread runs on. Prints `[drawable] guest dequeue: faithful ...` once and `[drawable] guest dequeue: drawable after N CoreAnimation timeout(s) (stall #k)` per long wait. Gate: debug/guestdequeue_gate.sh (`guestdequeue`); detector: owner_issue_scan.py FREEZE-DROPPED-DRAWABLE. docs/mcpe-guest-drawable-drop-freeze_2026-10-01.md | behaviour |
| MACAPK_DTASK_EXEMPT | off (every contended mutex, including a DeferredTask's own, pays the MUTEX_FAIR handoff tax) | any set value | **RETIRED by default (2026-09-27): read through `MACAPK_MCPE_SIGNIN_PROBES_RETIRED`, ignored with one `[mcpe-probes] ... IGNORED` line unless that gate is 0.** Exempts a Bedrock `Core::DeferredTask`'s own per-instance mutex from the MUTEX_FAIR boot-window handoff sleep, restoring Android-speed completion for MAIN's per-frame wait / the sign-in step's shared CAS handshake while keeping the barging tax on every other mutex; part of the MCPE 54%-stall fix family. | experiment |
| MACAPK_DUMP_DATA_IMPORTS | unset | a file path | Translator diagnostic: writes the ARM32 image's DataImportSite table (`slot name addend r_type sym_type sym_bind`, hex, after a `# dynamic_vaddr .. sites ..` header). Changes no output byte. Read by `debug/dataimport_gate.sh`. | dev |
| MACAPK_DUMP_GUEST_SEGMENTS | unset | a file path | Translator diagnostic: write the relocated PT_LOAD bytes (what the backends embed) as [u32 vaddr][u32 size][bytes]. Read by debug/reladdend_gate.sh. | dev |
| MACAPK_ELFEXEC | enabled (an APK-shipped native ELF executable is made runnable via the elfexec helper) | 0/1 | Governs whether Mac.apk installs an `elfexec`-shim stand-in for an APK's own bundled native executable (letting `ProcessBuilder`/`Runtime.exec` launches of it actually run); "0" reverts any previously-installed stand-in back to the raw guest ELF. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ELFEXEC_LOG | `<sidecar_dir>/<default log file>` | file path | Redirects the elfexec helper's diverted-stderr diagnostic log to the given path instead of the default sidecar-directory log file. | diag |
| MACAPK_ELFEXEC_SHIM_DIR | falls back to a `.shimdir` sidecar file, then MACAPK_SHIM_DIR | directory path | Tells the elfexec helper process where to find `libmacapk-bionic-shim.dylib`; normally set by the runtime and inherited by the forked helper, but can be hand-set for a manually-run helper. | dev |
| MACAPK_ELFEXEC_VERBOSE | off (silent success path, since an app may treat non-empty stderr as an error) | 1 | Logs the elfexec helper's guest-entry parameters (entry point, stack pointer, argc/envc/auxv count) to stderr on entering the guest executable. | diag |
| MACAPK_EPOLL_WAIT_CAP_MS | 250 ms (default-on liveness net) | integer ms (0 = restore true-infinite epoll_wait) | Caps an infinite `epoll_wait` (used by webrtc's `rtc::Thread`, which MCPE's cohtml/Gameface run tasks on) at a liveness bound, so a lost/missing cross-thread WakeUp cannot hang an epoll-based message loop forever; on each capped return the loop just re-checks its queues. | experiment |
| MACAPK_EXC_NATIVE_STACK | off (no native frame-pointer backtrace on a managed exception) | any set value | Walks the live x29 frame-pointer chain to print a native backtrace (`libil2cpp+0xRVA` per frame) alongside the managed-exception logger's output, working even when il2cpp's own managed-stack API is unavailable (release-build stack tracking off). | diag |
| MACAPK_EXC_STACK | off (no live managed-frame stack walk on a service-level exception) | any set value | Walks the throwing thread's LIVE il2cpp frames (via `il2cpp_current_thread_get_frame_at`) for service-level exception types (Uri/Format/NullRef/Argument/Web/Http/Aggregate/Exception), naming the method that built the bad value; skipped for spammy Socket/FileNotFound exceptions. | diag |
| MACAPK_EXIDX_DISCOVERY | off (`.ARM.exidx`-based function-start seeding does not run) | any set value | Runs a broad-net discovery pass that seeds ARM32 function starts from `.ARM.exidx` unwind-table entries, gated off by default because it is an intentionally wide net whose false-positive rate needs the protected sweep before shipping default-on. | experiment |
| MACAPK_FAIR_BASE_US | 0 (steady-state v618 behaviour: inert under incidental contention) | integer microseconds | Sets the steady-state (post-boot-window) base MUTEX_FAIR handoff sleep charged on a sustained-starvation streak, before it grows 500us/step; 0 keeps ordinary incidental contention (streak 1-2) completely free. | dev |
| MACAPK_FAIR_BOOT_MS | 0 ms since 2026-09-29 (the boot window is RETIRED, see MACAPK_FAIR_BOOT_RETIRED); 90000 ms under MACAPK_FAIR_BOOT_RETIRED=0 | integer ms (an explicit value wins in either arm; 0 = no boot window, pure v618 steady-state behaviour) | Sets how long after the first contended unlock the MUTEX_FAIR "boot window" (an unconditional handoff sleep on every waiter-present unlock) stays active. | dev |
| MACAPK_FAIR_BOOT_RETIRED | On (2026-09-29) -- no MUTEX_FAIR boot window: a contended guest unlock is Bionic NormalMutexUnlock (exchange + one futex wake, no sleep) until the steady streak gate engages | `0` | Restores the old 90 s / 50 ms boot window byte-for-byte (and its Swappy/choreographer latch + `[fair-latch]` line). Retired because the window slept every non-Unity guest's game loop 50 ms per contended unlock for its first ~90 s: Smash Hit guest-swap deltas > 50 ms 0-1 ON vs 44-51 OFF per 90 s run (contention-dependent), Minecraft menu 13 ON vs 788-968 OFF. docs/mutex-fair-boot-window-retired_2026-09-29.md; gate debug/fairtax_gate.sh (`fairtax`). | dev |
| MACAPK_FAIR_BOOT_US | 50000 us (the proven v606 value) | integer microseconds | Sets the unconditional per-contended-unlock sleep MUTEX_FAIR charges during the boot window (see MACAPK_FAIR_BOOT_MS), so a boot-time producer thread reliably gets scheduled. 2026-09-29: inert under the retired default; applies only under `MACAPK_FAIR_BOOT_RETIRED=0` or an explicit `MACAPK_FAIR_BOOT_MS` (docs/mutex-fair-boot-window-retired_2026-09-29.md). | dev |
| MACAPK_FAIR_STREAK | 4 (minimum 1) | integer | Sets how many consecutive contended-unlocks-without-a-waiter-breaking-through count as "sustained starvation" before MUTEX_FAIR's steady-state handoff sleep engages, keeping ordinary incidental contention (e.g. FMOD mixer threads) from ever paying the tax. | dev |
| MACAPK_FILEBACKED_SEGMENTS | enabled (a Stage-6 guest .so's non-writable segments are `MAP_PRIVATE` file-backed, matching bionic's own linker) | 0/1 | Governs whether Stage-6 native-arm64 loading maps a guest .so's read-only PT_LOADs as clean, demand-paged file-backed memory (the Android-faithful, evictable-under-pressure behaviour) instead of memcpy'ing them into dirty anonymous RAM; "0" opts out. | bisect |
| MACAPK_FILEBACKED_VERIFY | sampled verification (ELF header + phdr table + ~4KB at 3 points per segment) | "full" (anything else keeps sampled verification) | Forces a chunked full memcmp of a candidate on-disk .so against the embedded blob before file-backing its segments, instead of the default cheap sample, for diagnosing a suspected content mismatch. | diag |
| MACAPK_FILE_HEAL | enabled (pre-existing poisoned file modes in the app's data dir are healed once at startup) | 0 (anything else keeps healing on) | Governs whether Mac.apk heals pre-existing poisoned file permission modes in the app's data directory once, before the guest opens anything. | bisect |
| MACAPK_FIND_CORO | RETIRED 2026-09-27: deleted with the Crossy 7.11.1 il2cpp diag kit; setting it prints one `[il2cpp-diag] ... RETIRED and IGNORED` line | any set value | Runs two one-shot diagnostic scans: naming the il2cpp class/method whose native pointer matches a specific coroutine method (to find who is supposed to start it), and probing a captured BootController's enabled/active managed state. | diag |
| MACAPK_FIRST_MOUSE | YES (the window accepts its very first click even when not key/main) | 0 | Restores the plain AppKit default of ignoring a window's first click while it is not the key/main window, for bisecting an input regression; there is normally no reason to want this. | bisect |
| MACAPK_FIX_MAINTHREAD | RETIRED 2026-09-27: deleted with the Crossy 7.11.1 il2cpp diag kit; setting it prints one `[il2cpp-diag] ... RETIRED and IGNORED` line | any set value | Writes the detected UnityMain (JVM) pthread back into libunity's own main-thread-id slot when it mismatches the engine-init-time value, fixing `Object.InstantiateAsync` integration/Awake/activation that Unity gates on `IsMainThread()`. | experiment |
| MACAPK_FNPTR_ARG_SEED | enabled (the BL-argument PIC address-computation discovery idiom is active) | 0 | Disables a discovery idiom that seeds a function pointer computed via PIC address math (r0-r3) immediately followed by a `bl`/`blx`, which recovers targets like `__emutls_init_once` reached only through `pthread_once`; was previously gated off only by an unrelated env var accident; since 2026-09-27 it is the ONLY switch (the retired MACAPK_PLT_REDIRECT / _FREETYPE knobs no longer force it on). | bisect |
| MACAPK_FORCE_ABI | none (the runtime's own ABI priority order picks the native-library ABI) | arm64-v8a / armeabi-v7a / armeabi / x86_64 / x86 | Pins native-library selection (and the translated-dylib cache filename suffix) to a single requested ABI subdirectory with no fallback, so the same APK can be A/B'd across the arm64 Stage-6-native oracle path and the ARM32-lifter path. | diag |
| MACAPK_FORCE_GS_HANDLER | off (MCPE's GatheringService access-handler is only invoked if the user-session's own terminal sign-in-state signal actually fires, which it never does on Mac.apk) | integer ms | **RETIRED by default (2026-09-27): read through `MACAPK_MCPE_SIGNIN_PROBES_RETIRED`, ignored with one `[mcpe-probes] ... IGNORED` line unless that gate is 0.** Captures the GatheringManager instance, forces its internal readiness gate to 1, and invokes the GS-access handler directly from a host thread -- reproducing the emulator's terminal-signed-out listener fire to CONFIRM that a never-invoked handler (not a build defect) is the 54%-stall root. | diag |
| MACAPK_FORCE_JOBSYNC | RETIRED 2026-09-27: deleted with the Crossy 7.11.1 il2cpp diag kit; setting it prints one `[il2cpp-diag] ... RETIRED and IGNORED` line | any set value | Replicates the JobQueue frame-sync's deferred-call-queue-drain tail once per frame on UnityMain (before the AsyncInstantiate pump integrates), testing whether driving that missing drain lets pending InstantiateAsync completions integrate. | experiment |
| MACAPK_FORCE_LIFT_VADDRS | none (only heuristically-discovered functions are lifted) | comma/space-separated hex vaddrs, optional `:thumb` suffix | Force-lifts specific guest addresses as functions, bypassing all discovery heuristics, to test "is X a real function?" (e.g. an ARM long-call veneer or a non-canonical-prologue Thumb function) without modifying and retranslating with a broadened heuristic. | diag |
| MACAPK_FORCE_RET_ADDR | off (no forced-return trap installed) | hex file offset | **RETIRED by default (2026-09-27): read through `MACAPK_MCPE_SIGNIN_PROBES_RETIRED`, ignored with one `[mcpe-probes] ... IGNORED` line unless that gate is 0.** Overwrites a guest function's entry so it immediately returns a fixed value without running its body, used to confirm that the MCPE 54% stall is the GS-issue readiness gate (forcing the user-session predicate to return 1 should make sign-in proceed). | diag |
| MACAPK_FORCE_RET_VAL | 1 | integer | **RETIRED by default (2026-09-27): read through `MACAPK_MCPE_SIGNIN_PROBES_RETIRED`, ignored with one `[mcpe-probes] ... IGNORED` line unless that gate is 0.** Sets the fixed `x0` return value the MACAPK_FORCE_RET_ADDR trap installs, when something other than a truthy "1" is needed. | diag |
| MACAPK_FOREIGN_WAKE_DEFER_BCAST | off (a host-originated `pthread_cond_broadcast` wakes all parked waiters immediately) | any set value (also needs MACAPK_FOREIGN_WAKE_DEFER_US > 0) | Extends the foreign-wake defer (see MACAPK_FOREIGN_WAKE_DEFER_US) to cover `cond_broadcast` as well as `cond_signal`; REFUTED by an 18-run interleaved A/B (18% vs 22% cross -- no effect), since the real stealer busy-spins rather than waiting on the condvar. | experiment |
| MACAPK_FOREIGN_WAKE_DEFER_US | 0 (off; a host-originated cond wake is delivered immediately) | integer microseconds | Delays a HOST-thread-originated (not guest-originated) `pthread_cond_signal`'s wake-delivery by the given microseconds, reproducing the Bionic/Linux CFS wakeup-granularity window a guest producer/consumer implicitly depends on, so a running "pump" wins the queue-pop race instead of a just-woken parked stealer. | experiment |
| MACAPK_FREE_QUARANTINE_MAX_CHUNK | 65536 bytes (64 KiB) | bytes | Sets the chunk-size threshold above which a freed allocation bypasses the UAF free-quarantine (and its memset) entirely and goes straight to `real_free`, since large chunks (texture/mesh buffers) never needed the FMOD-UAF-class zeroing+holding. | dev |
| MACAPK_FREE_QUARANTINE_MAX_MB | 32 MiB | megabytes | Hard-caps the total bytes the free-quarantine backlog may hold; when adding a chunk would exceed it, the oldest entries are evicted (freed for real) even if younger than the hold window, bounding worst-case retention under heavy allocation churn. | dev |
| MACAPK_FREE_QUARANTINE_SECS | 30 seconds | seconds (floating point) | Sets how long a freed allocation is held (zeroed, in a quarantine list) before it can be recycled, defending against a known FMOD/libfmod use-after-free class; the guarantee is a hard time window, not size- or count-based. | dev |
| MACAPK_FUTEX_WAIT_CAP_MS | off (0; an infinite raw FUTEX_WAIT parks truly forever) | integer ms | Caps an infinite raw `FUTEX_WAIT` at a liveness bound (returning a futex-legal spurious wake on timeout) so a lock-free work-queue worker with a lost wakeup re-polls; default off because capping every infinite futex wait is broad (Unity Baselib parks here heavily) and did not resolve the MCPE 54% stall in limited testing. | experiment |
| MACAPK_GC_APPEND_WATCH | RETIRED 2026-09-27: deleted with the Crossy 7.11.1 il2cpp diag kit; setting it prints one `[il2cpp-diag] ... RETIRED and IGNORED` line | any set value | Patches bdwgc's free-region/block-list-add entry to log every heap-region-add call's `(start, bytes)` and flag garbage (`0xffffffff` low32) or huge (>=1GiB) regions with a stack-scanned il2cpp caller chain, localizing the caller behind a "Deallocating excessively large block" abort. | diag |
| MACAPK_GMS_CHILD_FIRST | enabled (a GMS-bundling app's classloader resolves its own R8'd GMS classes before the framework's shadow) | 0 / false (disables; anything else, including unset, keeps it on) | Governs whether the app classloader looks up GMS/Firebase classes child-first (the app's own bundled client) instead of parent-first (Mac.apk's shadow implementation), so a bundling app computes its own SERVICE_MISSING verdict rather than depending on the shadow's answer. | bisect |
| MACAPK_PREVENT_DISPATCH_ATTACH | Enabled -- a child a ViewGroup adds while running its OWN attach dispatch (onAttachedToWindow + its OnAttachStateChangeListeners) is attached by the children loop that follows, i.e. after those listeners, as AOSP's FLAG_PREVENT_DISPATCH_ATTACHED_TO_WINDOW does (android14 ViewGroup.dispatchAttachedToWindow / addViewInner) | `0` = off (the old order: such a child attaches inside addView, before the group's listeners) | Conductor's ViewAttachHandler listens on the controller view's deepest first child from the controller view's attach listener; with the old order a ComposeView's AndroidComposeView was already attached, the listener never fired and the controller (and its screen) never became attached -- Reddit's welcome-sheet "Continue with Google" did nothing. | behaviour |
| MACAPK_RETIRE_ENGINE_SHADOWS | On (2026-09-27) -- AppClassLoader defines the app's OWN copy of any framework class marked `@Deprecated(since = AppClassLoader.RETIRED_SHADOW, forRemoval = true)` when the app bundles a class of that name (today: the AndEngine EngineLock shadow `org.andengine.b.c`, and FMOD's `org.fmod.AudioDevice`, the superseded init-arg clamp: GD Lite 2.2.11, GD Meltdown 2.2.147, MCPE bundle it; Unity's `org.fmod.FMODAudioDevice` etc. are not marked and untouched), logging `retired shadow -> app's own copy` once; an app that does not bundle it still gets the shadow | `0` | Restores the parent-first shadow exactly (`AndEngineLockShadow: loaded`; for FMOD the shadow's `AudioDevice::init(channels=..)` line and chCfg=12 return). Also OFF whenever MACAPK_GLSV_PAUSE=0: the retirement is only safe under the AOSP pause contract, so `MACAPK_GLSV_PAUSE=0` also restores the shadow. `MACAPK_RETIRE_ENGINE_SHADOWS=0` alone (pause contract kept) is safe: the shadow never blocks. docs/andengine-lock-shadow-retired_2026-09-27.md, docs/fmod-audiodevice-shadow-retired_2026-09-27.md; gates debug/geh_andengine-lock-shadow_gate.sh, debug/geh_fmod-audiodevice-shadow_gate.sh | bisect |
| MACAPK_GPAC | enabled since v1.0.801 (a `bl` to an address inside the same lifted ARM32 function is admitted as a real synth target) | 0 | Governs whether a direct-call target that is a real decoded `bl` destination inside the SAME lifted function's own body (e.g. reusing a division routine past its zero-check) is lifted as its own synth so the call resolves correctly, instead of falling to a return-0 panic stub; "0" reverts to the old panic-stub behaviour. | bisect |
| MACAPK_GUEST_EXIT | the new MacApkProcessExit logic runs (native _exit, plus AOSP-style ActivityManager "restart myself" handling) | legacy (anything else/unset keeps the new behaviour) | Restores the raw `Runtime.exit()` call for `System.exit`/`Runtime.exit`/`Runtime.halt`, for bisecting back to the deadlock that motivated MacApkProcessExit (a re-entrant JNI static destructor blocking forever in `JavaThread::block_if_vm_exited`). | bisect |
| MACAPK_GUEST_NULL_WGUARD | off (a write through a NULL guest object silently corrupts guest page 0) | 1 | Write-protects guest page 0 so a store through a NULL "this" faults AT ITS ORIGIN (naming the first bad store) instead of surfacing hundreds of stores later as a wild-read crash; cannot be default-on because some engines' `.init_array` legitimately writes into that region. | diag |
| MACAPK_GUEST_STACK_JVM_RESERVE | ON (131072 bytes on a 16 KiB-page host) | `0` | An arm64 guest thread created with an EXPLICIT stack size gets HotSpot's attach zones (red+yellow+reserved guard + shadow, the JDK formula over Stack{Red,Yellow,Reserved,Shadow}Pages 1/2/1/20, each overridable by a later -XX:Stack*Pages=N in JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS, MACAPK_JVM_OPTS or _JAVA_OPTIONS, the VM's own precedence) added BELOW its request, plus, for a request under 72 KiB, the page-rounded shortfall to HotSpot's 72 KiB usable-stack floor (an attached thread runs Java on that stack); so it keeps the whole request as usable stack the way it does on a device, and pthread_getattr_np(self) still reports the request. Without it a verbatim 48-112 KiB thread gets AttachCurrentThread = JNI_ERR and a 16/32 KiB one kills the process (SIGILL). Default-sized (1 MiB) threads untouched. `=0` restores the verbatim request and the pre-change pthread_create log line, and prints one warning line. ⚠ `=0` ALONE (with MACAPK_FMOD_GENERIC_ROUTING on, the default) makes every FMOD app's mixer/file thread fail AttachCurrentThread and hangs FMOD init (gate row L3); when bisecting, pair it with MACAPK_FMOD_GENERIC_ROUTING=0. v1.0.1654; gate `fmodtag`; docs/fmod-image-tag-retired_2026-09-27.md. | bisect |
| MACAPK_GUEST_TOTALRAM | 0 (uncapped; the guest `sysinfo()` reports the host's true physical RAM) | bytes | Caps the RAM value Mac.apk's `sysinfo()` shim reports to the guest; kept only for future per-device tuning after a v1.0.624 experiment that capped it at ~3 GiB was found to make MCPE's RenderDragon pick a low, black-rendering device-memory tier and was reverted. | dev |
| MACAPK_HIDDEN_DRIVE_HZ | 2.0 Hz | float Hz, [0.1, 30.0] | Sets the low-rate cadence at which every engine's frame-pacing pump (Swappy/Choreographer/present-bridge) is still ticked while the app's window is hidden/occluded, keeping pacing state alive without a catch-up burst on resume. | dev |
| MACAPK_HOSTWAKE_REARM | off (a host-JVM-originated `pthread_cond_signal`/`broadcast` wakes the guest only once) | any set value | Re-registers a host-thread-originated condvar wake and re-broadcasts the SAME cond a few times over a re-arm window, catching a guest worker that commits to parking in the tiny window right after the host bumped the cond counter but before it captured "seen" -- a ~40%-on-macOS-vs-100%-on-Linux host-to-guest wake-loss gap. | experiment |
| MACAPK_HOSTWAKE_REARM_MS | 25 ms | integer ms | Sets how long the HOSTWAKE_REARM re-broadcast window stays active for a given registered cond after the host's original wake. | experiment |
| MACAPK_HOST_WRITE_SKIP | off (a guest store into host executable code faults and is left to the normal SIGSEGV path, and the mprotect guard already refuses to make host pages writable) | any set value | Skips (no-ops, PC+=4) a guest store that lands on one of Mac.apk's own host executable pages, instead of letting it fault; kept only as a diagnostic escape hatch since it leaves an inline-hook installer's own trampoline bookkeeping half-written. | diag |
| MACAPK_HTTP_COMPLETION_DELAY_US | 0 (no delay at the HTTP-completion native entry) | integer microseconds | **RETIRED by default (2026-09-27): read through `MACAPK_MCPE_SIGNIN_PROBES_RETIRED`, ignored with one `[mcpe-probes] ... IGNORED` line unless that gate is 0.** Sleeps for the given microseconds at the entry of MCPE's `OnRequestCompleted` native hook, deferring the coroutine-readiness-set itself so the completion pump parks before readiness flips -- the confirmed fix direction for the 54%-stall self-orphaning race. | experiment |
| MACAPK_ICON_ROUND_N | 5.0 (superellipse exponent) | float, [2.0, 40.0] | Overrides the superellipse exponent used to round the app's own Dock icon to macOS's icon shape (2 = ellipse, high = near-square). | user |
| MACAPK_ICON_SCALE | ~0.8047 (824/1024, macOS's standard icon-body-to-canvas ratio) | float, [0.5, 1.0] | Overrides how much an Android app icon is inset before being placed on macOS's Dock icon grid (1.0 = full bleed, the pre-v1.0.674 look). | user |
| MACAPK_IL2CPP_EXC_LOG | off (no managed-exception logging) | any set value | Entry-hooks `vm::Exception::Raise` (resolved from libil2cpp's own exported symbols, zero hardcoded RVAs) and logs every managed exception's type, message, inner exception and (optionally) live/native stack, the primary visibility tool for a silently-stuck Unity app. Since 2026-09-27 a build without il2cpp_raise_exception, il2cpp_class_get_name or il2cpp_format_exception is not hooked (one "exports missing -- DISABLED" line) instead of falling back to Crossy 7.11.1 RVAs, and a PC-relative first instruction at Exception::Raise disables it too (docs/il2cpp-diag-kit-retired_2026-09-27.md). | diag |
| MACAPK_IL2CPP_TIME_PROBE | off (no hook, no output) | a positive integer N (a set non-number or 0 means 6000, the interval batch W measured with) | The instrument for the notated Crossy `Time.unscaledTime` regression (docs/unity-choreographer-vsync-nanotime-contract_2026-09-27.md 4a; falsifier `unscaledTime < 1e4`). Entry-hooks vm::Runtime::Invoke, found by following the EXPORTED `il2cpp_runtime_invoke` through its unconditional `b` (refused if the displaced first instruction is PC-relative), and every N calls prints `[time-probe] Time.<getter> = <value>` for UnityEngine.Time (deltaTime, unscaledDeltaTime, smoothDeltaTime, fixed/maximum/captureDeltaTime, timeScale, time, unscaledTime, realtimeSinceStartup, frameCount, unscaledTimeAsDouble, realtimeSinceStartupAsDouble) by asking il2cpp itself through six exports resolved by NAME (il2cpp_domain_get, il2cpp_domain_assembly_open("UnityEngine.CoreModule"), il2cpp_assembly_get_image, il2cpp_class_from_name, il2cpp_class_get_method_from_name, il2cpp_runtime_invoke). No hardcoded address: it arms on Crossy 7.11.1 (Unity 6000.3) and Subway 3.62.0 (2022.3) alike. Successor of the retired kit's `MACAPK_UHOOK=1 MACAPK_IL2CPP_TRACE=<N>` probe, which was byte-gated to Crossy. Digest a log with `debug/il2cpp_timeprobe.py`. Gate rows X2/B4/B5 of debug/geh_il2cpp-diag-hooks_gate.sh. | diag |
| MACAPK_INPUT_FAIR_EXEMPT | enabled (the thread synchronously delivering one touch/key event is exempt from the MUTEX_FAIR handoff sleep) | 0 | Governs whether the OS thread synchronously dispatching a single input event to the guest skips the MUTEX_FAIR tax on a contended guest mutex; "0" makes it pay the tax like every other thread, for A/B comparison. | bisect |
| MACAPK_INTEG_COUNT | RETIRED 2026-09-27: deleted with the Crossy 7.11.1 il2cpp diag kit; setting it prints one `[il2cpp-diag] ... RETIRED and IGNORED` line | any set value | Entry-patches a fixed list of libunity/PreloadManager async-scene-load addresses with pure counters, localizing whether Mac.apk's async scene-load completions ever integrate at all (all-zero) or stall after a specific stage. Its counters are printed only by MACAPK_RENDER_STATE, which since 2026-09-27 runs only under MACAPK_REGISTERNATIVES_VERBATIM=0; set under the default it arms them anyway and prints one "[integ] MACAPK_INTEG_COUNT: counters installed but never printed" line. | diag |
| MACAPK_JIT_RWX_TO_RW | enabled (an RWX `mprotect` that fails on Apple Silicon falls back to RW, dropping only PROT_EXEC) | 0 | Governs the W^X-compat fallback that lets Android JIT engines (e.g. cohtml's V8, used for MCPE's Ore-UI menu) unprotect a code page as RWX -- which macOS refuses -- by retrying as RW instead, so the write phase still succeeds; "0" disables the fallback. | bisect |
| MACAPK_JIT_SERIALIZE | enabled (concurrent JIT-code-page write windows on different threads are serialized under one lock) | 0 | Governs whether opening a JIT engine's RWX "unprotect for writing" window on one thread excludes another thread from doing the same to a different host page at the same time, preventing a race where two compiler threads corrupt each other's code-page protection transitions; "0" disables serialization. | bisect |
| MACAPK_JNI_ART_LIB_ORDER | ON (since 2026-10-01) | 0 disables (only exactly `0` turns it off) | Every `BaseDexClassLoader` (AppClassLoader, PathClassLoader, DexClassLoader, ...) searches its loaded JNI libraries in PATH-SORTED order (UTF-8 bytes, unsigned = ART's `std::less<std::string>` over `libraries_` in `java_vm_ext.cc` FindNativeMethodInternal) when binding an unregistered native method, instead of HotSpot's ConcurrentHashMap path-HASH order. Decides the winner when two libraries export the same `Java_*` symbol: Play-Minecraft's HttpClient.Android vs PlayFab `OnRequestCompleted` (PlayFab-first install roots SIGILLed at +0xdf544). `=0` = HotSpot hash order, byte-identical. Needs `--add-opens=java.base/jdk.internal.loader`; prints one `[jni-order]` line either way. Gate `jniorder`. | behaviour |
| MACAPK_JNI_BARE_BRIDGE | off (only the `...V`/`...A` ellipsis JNI vtable slots are bridged) | 1 | Installs the bare-ellipsis (true C `...`) counterparts of the 21 marshalled JNI vtable slots, for plain-C guests that call the non-`V`/`A` forms directly; a no-op that only logs when unset, since the blast radius is the vtable every Stage-6 guest shares. | experiment |
| MACAPK_JNI_FINDCLASS_NOINIT | ON (since v1.0.1609; extended 2026-09-30) | 0 disables (only a leading `0` turns it off) | A guest's JNI `FindClass` resolves WITHOUT running `<clinit>`, as ART's does, instead of HotSpot's initializing `jni_FindClass`: first through the app class loader, then (2026-09-30) through the JNI_OnLoad CONTEXT loader (`jdk.internal.loader.NativeLibraries.getFromClass()` -> that class's loader; ART's `GetClassLoader`), so a protected SDK that loads its payload through its own ClassLoader subclass (Efteling's Adyen 3DS2 `atd.e.ChallengeResultSS`) no longer has a payload class initialised inside JNI_OnLoad before its natives are registered. `=0` = HotSpot's initializing FindClass as the primary path. | behaviour |
| MACAPK_JNI_STRING_RECOVERY_OFF | off (a fault inside HotSpot's `GetString*` JNI entry, from a guest's own bad jstring handle, is recovered by returning 0) | any set value | Kill switch that disables the JNI-GetString-fault recovery, so a bad-jstring fault crashes instead of being caught and turned into a clean "fast-JNI probe failed, fall back to standard JNI" return. | bisect |
| MACAPK_JOB_CONSTRAINTS | off (a scheduled job runs regardless of requiresCharging/DeviceIdle/BatteryNotLow/StorageNotLow/network constraints) | 1 | Honours JobInfo's constraint flags by deferring a job (polling every 15s) until they are satisfied, with AOSP's override-deadline escape; shipped default-off because it can turn a job that currently always runs into one that stops running, a fleet-wide regression risk not yet cleared. | experiment |
| MACAPK_JOB_PERIODIC_WINDOW | off (a periodic job's first run fires promptly) | 1 | Places a periodic job's FIRST run inside AOSP's `[now + period - flex, now + period]` window instead of firing it immediately; same fleet-wide regression caution as MACAPK_JOB_CONSTRAINTS. | experiment |
| MACAPK_JSON_NOPARSE | off (JSONObject/JSONArray perform a real JSON parse) | 1 | Reverts `JSONObject`/`JSONArray`'s tokener constructors to the pre-v1.0.288 stub behaviour (an empty object/array, no parsing at all), an emergency rollback knob to confirm whether a regression comes from real JSON parsing without a rebuild. | bisect |
| MACAPK_JSON_MAP_WRAP | on (`new JSONObject(Map)` wraps every value with `JSONObject.wrap`, as AOSP libcore does: nested Map -> JSONObject, Collection/array -> JSONArray, null -> JSONObject.NULL; null key -> NPE "key == null") | 0 | Restores the pre-2026-10-01 raw copy (values stored unwrapped, null values dropped, keys via String.valueOf). The raw copy made `optJSONObject` answer null for a config built from nested maps: Efteling's AWSMobileClient threw "Neither Cognito Identity or Cognito UserPool was used" out of Application.onCreate, which skipped the rest of the app's init. Gate `icuintl` rows J1 J2. docs/android-icu-hermes-intl-surface_2026-10-01.md | behaviour |
| MACAPK_ICU_PLATFORM_TYPES | on (android.icu factories return ICU's concrete types: NumberFormat.get*Instance -> DecimalFormat, DateFormat.get*Instance -> SimpleDateFormat, Collator.getInstance -> RuleBasedCollator; formatToCharacterIterator carries android.icu NumberFormat.Field / DateFormat.Field; DateFormat.getInstanceForSkeleton / getPatternInstance format with the skeleton's best pattern) | 0 | Restores the pre-2026-10-01 opaque wrappers (JdkNumberFormat / JdkDateFormat / JdkCollator), attribute-less iterators and getInstanceForSkeleton == getDateInstance(). Hermes' Intl casts to those concrete types (a ClassCastException on Collator, a 24-hour cycle for every locale on DateFormat) and compares Field keys with ==. Gate `icuintl` (=0 fails exactly N1 N6 D1 D2 D3 C1). Not restored by =0 (corrections, not behaviour changes): ICU strength numbering in RuleBasedCollator.set/getStrength, NumberFormat.format dispatch, deep DecimalFormat/SimpleDateFormat.clone(). docs/android-icu-hermes-intl-surface_2026-10-01.md | behaviour |
| MACAPK_JT_TRACE | off (no jump-table detection logging) | any set value | Logs, for every `rd==15` ALU operation the arm32rc core dispatch reaches, the operand shape (register/immediate/shift) so an ARM inline-jump-table (`add pc,pc,rN,lsl#k`) construct can be traced during lowering. | diag |
| MACAPK_JVM_OPTS | none (no extra JVM options) | space-separated JVM command-line options | Passes arbitrary extra options straight to `JNI_CreateJavaVM` (e.g. `-Xint`, `-XX:TieredStopAtLevel=1`, `-agentlib:jdwp=...` for a JDWP debugger), a general-purpose escape hatch for diagnosing JIT/GC/heap issues without rebuilding. | dev |
## Native code & ABI (continued)

| Flag | Default (unset) | Accepted values | Effect | Audience |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| MACAPK_MIDR_EMULATE | ON (2026-09-27): a guest `mrs Xt, MIDR_EL1 / MPIDR_EL1 / REVIDR_EL1` is answered by the SIGILL emulator the way Linux emulate_id_reg answers it (MIDR = kMacapkArm64Midr 0x410fd0c0, the value /proc/cpuinfo prints; MPIDR = 1<<31; REVIDR = 0), and the guest instruction is not rewritten | "0" disables | Restores the c20c9434 load-time rewrite of every MIDR read to `mov Xt,#0` and removes the three registers from the emulator. Byte for byte the old path only together with MACAPK_SVC_BRK_EXEC_ONLY=0; with EXEC_ONLY at its default the restored rewrite spares a MIDR-shaped word positively inside a non-executable section. | bisect |
| MACAPK_MODIMM_CARRY_CONSERVATIVE | the lifter computes the exact ARMExpandImm_C carry-out for a rotated modified immediate (a translate-time constant, free to compute) | "1" | Restores the old, always-conservative "carry unchanged" behavior for a data-processing modified-immediate instruction's carry flag. | bisect |
| MACAPK_MPROTECT_HOST_GUARD | ON: a guest mprotect() that would strip exec permission from one of Mac.apk's own host-code segments is refused (reports success, changes nothing) | "0" disables | Turns off the guard that protects Mac.apk's own JVM/shim/ANGLE executable pages from being de-executed by a guest mprotect() call. | bisect |
| MACAPK_MUTEX_FAIR | adaptive default: for the first ~90s of runtime every contended unlock sleeps ~50ms to hand off; afterward only a sustained (>=4) contended-unlock streak gets a scaled sleep | "0" disables entirely; "1" always sched_yield(); an integer N>1 always usleep(N) us; unset = adaptive boot/steady behavior | Fixes a macOS-scheduler mutex-fairness bug (a tight-looping unlocker can starve a co-consumer, since macOS gives no wakeup preemption) by handing a starved waiter a real scheduling window on a contended unlock. | bisect |
| MACAPK_MUTEX_HANDOFF | OFF: contended-unlock fairness uses the v599-v619 usleep-based streak mechanism (MACAPK_MUTEX_FAIR) | any non-"0" value | Replaces the sleep-based heuristic with a fair, direct grant-word handoff (parking_lot/WebKit-style unlock_fair) so no thread can barge in ahead of the waiter that was granted the lock. | bisect |
| MACAPK_MUTEX_OWNER_AWARE | the guest mutex parks via os_sync_wait_on_address (UL_COMPARE_AND_WAIT), giving the kernel no priority-inheritance information | any non-"0" value, read once at process startup | Switches the mutex to park via the raw UL_UNFAIR_LOCK ulock protocol so the kernel can resolve the owner and apply priority inheritance, fixing priority-inversion starvation. | bisect |
| MACAPK_NO_BOUNDED_DRAWABLE | on the Vulkan/MoltenVK path, [CAMetalLayer nextDrawable] for the guest's own layer is bounded by a hard timeout to avoid drawable-pool exhaustion deadlock | any set value | Disables that bounded/timeout-guarded acquire, reverting to a plain passthrough call. | bisect |
| MACAPK_NO_DECOUPLE | since 2026-09-27 a NO-OP on the default path: the native-EGL Vulkan guest already gets its real window surface (MACAPK_VK_WINDOW_SURFACE, default ON). Under MACAPK_VK_WINDOW_SURFACE=0 (the v612 offscreen pbuffer + async PBO readback, the old MCPE 54% stall fix) it keeps its OLD meaning | any set value (even empty) | Under MACAPK_VK_WINDOW_SURFACE=0 only: opts out of the offscreen-decouple, giving the guest a real window surface again. Kept as the =0-arm alias so every pre-2026-09-27 A/B recipe still reproduces. | bisect |
| MACAPK_NO_DISPLAY_SYNC | the CAMetalLayer's displaySyncEnabled stays at its default (vsync-gated) on the ANGLE-Metal path | any set value | Sets displaySyncEnabled = NO, an opt-in escape hatch removing vsync-gating from the free-drawable wait inside eglSwapBuffers. | bisect |
| MACAPK_NO_EH_REGISTER | each loaded image's .eh_frame FDEs are registered with the host libunwind so guest C++ exceptions unwind across host frames | any set value | Disables that registration, so a guest __cxa_throw that would unwind into a host frame instead hits END_OF_STACK and aborts. | bisect |
| MACAPK_NO_GUEST_TEXT_PATCH | the stack-canary neutralization and MIDR_EL1-read .text patches are applied to the guest image | any set value | Skips those .text rewrites, leaving the guest's own code byte-unmodified, to test whether an integrity check that self-hashes its .text detects our patches as tampering. | diag |
| MACAPK_NO_LOCAL_JUMPSLOT | an intra-module JUMP_SLOT relocation naming a local symbol has its GOT slot bound to that local definition | any non-empty, non-"0" value | Kill switch that disables binding intra-module PLT/GOT slots to their local definitions, for bisecting a regression back to that pass. | bisect |
| MACAPK_NO_NMB | the JVMTI NativeMethodBind callback (feeds the ARM32 jlong/jdouble/jfloat re-marshal and Stage-6 argument-packing thunk) is installed | any set value | Skips installing that callback entirely, for A/B bisection of the re-marshal machinery. With it off, every float argument of a name-bound ARM32 native is lost (the generic JNI thunk needs the bind-time descriptor to place it; see MACAPK_ARM32_JNI_SIG_FP). | bisect |
| MACAPK_NO_NMB_THUNK | on Stage 6, a name-bound native with stack arguments gets an Apple-PCS<->AAPCS64 repacking thunk installed | any set value | Disables installing that thunk, for bisecting a regression (its absence reproduces a JVM-fatal SIGSEGV on multi-argument native methods). | bisect |
| MACAPK_NO_PRELOAD | AppClassLoader eagerly preloads every native library packaged in the APK before the app's own loadLibrary calls | any set value | Skips that eager preloading pass, to isolate whether a load-order/timing issue is caused by the preload itself. | diag |
| MACAPK_NO_REGCACHE | the ARM32 lifter's block-local SSA register cache is used | "1" | Bypasses the register cache, falling back to a direct load/store-per-access register-file model, isolating the cache when bisecting a codegen regression. | bisect |
| MACAPK_NO_WAKE_KICK | after a wake-from-sleep NSWorkspace notification, every live MTKView is forced to redraw immediately | any non-empty value other than "0" | Disables that forced redraw kick; there is normally no reason to set this. | bisect |
| MACAPK_NPROC_OVERRIDE | Bionic sysconf CPU-topology answers report the host's real online/configured CPU count | an integer CPU count | Overrides the reported CPU count so a guest that branches on perceived core count can be tested against a different topology. | diag |
| MACAPK_NULL_RECOVERY_NEARNULL | ON (unchanged behaviour): the arm64 null recovery zero-and-advances near-null loads/stores, recovers the x18 register-offset cascade and "returns 0" from a branch to NULL | "0" disables | Turns those three OFF while KEEPING the TPIDR-magic stack-canary backstop (a Bionic-ABI bridge), so an underlying null fault crashes where it happens; for root-causing Subway libunity+0xf9106c and MCPE's null callees. MACAPK_NULL_RECOVERY_OFF still disables everything. | bisect |
| MACAPK_NULL_RECOVERY_OFF | ON: an arm64-native NULL-deref zeroes the destination register and advances PC instead of crashing | any set value | Disables that default-on recovery so the first unexpected NULL deref crashes cleanly with a real .ips backtrace instead of being silently masked. | bisect |
| MACAPK_OSWAIT_CAP | the OSWAIT trace log caps at 5,000,000 printed park/wake events | an integer count | Overrides the print cap for the (separately gated) OSWAIT diagnostic trace of futex-backed park/wake events. | diag |
| MACAPK_OSWAIT_FLOOR | the OSWAIT trace only logs addresses at/above the libunity allocator-arena floor (0x300000000) | a hex address (0 traces everything) | Overrides that address floor, so engines whose sync words live outside the Unity arena can still be traced. | diag |
| MACAPK_OVERLAY_IMPLICIT_FADE | the overlay layer's Core Animation fade is explicit, avoiding a present-smear-during-motion artifact | exactly "1" | Restores the old implicit-fade behavior, for a one-run A/B against the explicit-fade fix. | bisect |
| MACAPK_PARKWAKE | OFF: no generic idle-worker-pool-cond detection/broadcasting runs | any set value | App-agnostic generalization of MACAPK_WAKE_BGPOOLS: detects a thread repeatedly re-parking on the same (cond,mutex) pair and periodically issues a spurious (POSIX-legal) broadcast to keep the box non-quiescent. | bisect |
| MACAPK_PARKWAKE_NOBCAST | the PARKWAKE broadcaster thread issues its periodic broadcasts | any set value | Isolation mode: the broadcaster starts but never broadcasts, leaving only the busy-spinner threads' CPU churn as PARKWAKE's live effect. | diag |
| MACAPK_PARKWAKE_SPIN | 0: no busy-spinner threads are spawned | an integer K | Spawns K host threads that hard-spin on a core while any idle-pool cond is fresh, reproducing WAKE_BGPOOLS's sustained-CPU-churn effect generically. | bisect |
| MACAPK_PARKWAKE_WINMS | 6000 ms re-entry window for the idle-pool-cond detector | an integer (milliseconds) | Overrides the time window within which two re-entries into the same wait are treated as the idle-pool-worker polling signature. | diag |
| MACAPK_PATH_REMAP_VADDRS | inactive, zero translation-time cost. RETIRED 2026-09-26: a set value is IGNORED (one warning) unless `MACAPK_COCOS_OWN_FULLPATH=0` or `MACAPK_COCOS_FORCE_FOPEN=1` | a comma/space-separated list of hex vaddrs | Per-APK diagnostic stub: replaces a synth at a listed vaddr with a tail-call to the cocos2d-x fullPathFromRelativePath shim. Has NO positive-identification gate: its one recorded vaddr, 0x222238, is the forwarder in HCR 1.43 armeabi-v7a but `beq` mid-function in the same APK's armeabi lib; and that target never runs ARM32 (ABI pruning picks arm64-v8a). | diag |
| MACAPK_PAUSE_WHEN_HIDDEN | ON: while the window is minimized/occluded, the MTKView present/drive tick throttles to a low rate (default 2 Hz) instead of free-running off-screen | "0" disables (state still tracked) | Mirrors real Android's SurfaceFlinger spending ~0 work on an invisible window, without ever fully stopping the guest's render/simulation thread. | bisect |
| MACAPK_PLOOP_CENSUS | RETIRED 2026-09-27: ignored by default (MACAPK_REGISTERNATIVES_VERBATIM); under =0, OFF, no instrumentation installed | any set value, read only under MACAPK_REGISTERNATIVES_VERBATIM=0 | Under MACAPK_REGISTERNATIVES_VERBATIM=0 only: discovers Unity's flattened player-loop function-pointer table and pointer-swaps every slot to a counting wrapper, logging which Update nodes the engine itself drives. Driven from the nativeRender wrap, so it needs =0; that is also how to re-run the v1.0.322 census. | diag |
| MACAPK_PLT_REDIRECT | RETIRED 2026-09-27: deleted from the translator, setting it does nothing (the default translation is byte-identical to the pre-retire tree; debug/geh_libgdx-freetype-plt-redirect_gate.sh A1) | (none) | Was: opt every library into a translate-time `bl <plt_stub>` -> local-body rewrite inside Arm32Lifter. On the pre-retire tree it only perturbed discovery (+13,812 panic-stubbed sites on OpenTTD libapplication). docs/plt-redirect-knobs-retired_2026-09-27.md | retired |
| MACAPK_PLT_REDIRECT_FREETYPE | RETIRED 2026-09-27: deleted from the translator, setting it does nothing (the default translation is byte-identical to the pre-retire tree; debug/geh_libgdx-freetype-plt-redirect_gate.sh A1) | (none) | Was: the same rewrite for a library defining FT_Init_FreeType, FT_/ft_ names only. Bionic has no per-library or per-name policy; FreeType's error 11 is fixed by the generic JUMP_SLOT binding. docs/plt-redirect-knobs-retired_2026-09-27.md | retired |
| MACAPK_PLT_REDIRECT_STRONG_ONLY | RETIRED 2026-09-27: deleted from the translator, setting it does nothing (the default translation is byte-identical to the pre-retire tree; debug/geh_libgdx-freetype-plt-redirect_gate.sh A1) | (none) | Was: a bisect sub-knob of the retired PLT redirect. docs/plt-redirect-knobs-retired_2026-09-27.md | retired |
| MACAPK_PLT_REDIRECT_VMAX | RETIRED 2026-09-27: deleted from the translator, setting it does nothing (the default translation is byte-identical to the pre-retire tree; debug/geh_libgdx-freetype-plt-redirect_gate.sh A1) | (none) | Was: a bisect sub-knob of the retired PLT redirect. docs/plt-redirect-knobs-retired_2026-09-27.md | retired |
| MACAPK_PLT_REDIRECT_VMIN | RETIRED 2026-09-27: deleted from the translator, setting it does nothing (the default translation is byte-identical to the pre-retire tree; debug/geh_libgdx-freetype-plt-redirect_gate.sh A1) | (none) | Was: a bisect sub-knob of the retired PLT redirect. docs/plt-redirect-knobs-retired_2026-09-27.md | retired |
| MACAPK_POLL_GUEST | no guest-memory polling thread starts | comma-separated hex guest addresses | Samples the given 32-bit guest words and prints every value change (plus the pointee's first word if it looks like a pointer), independent of both translator backends. | diag |
| MACAPK_POLL_MS | 100 ms sample interval for MACAPK_POLL_GUEST | an integer (milliseconds, 0 = spin) | Tightens the guest-memory poller's sample interval, to distinguish "the store never landed" from "it landed and was clobbered soon after". | diag |
| MACAPK_PRINT_DESTINATION | PrintManager uses the host's default CUPS destination logic | "<cups-queue-name>", "default", or "none" | Selects where a guest print job is spooled: a named CUPS queue, the host default, or "none" to fail the job. | user |
| MACAPK_PRIO_POOL_BOOST | off | any truthy integer | Lifts every setpriority-managed WorkerPool thread to at least QOS_CLASS_USER_INITIATED, compensating for macOS's strict-tier scheduler putting a nice-0 pool worker in the same bucket as a boot-time thread storm. | bisect |
| MACAPK_PROCESS_SELF_KILL | OFF: Process's self-kill idioms (ProcessPhoenix/ACRA/game-exit paths) are empty no-op bodies | "1" | Makes those self-kill paths actually send the kill signal (AOSP-faithful), pending a fleet A/B before it becomes unconditional. | bisect |
| MACAPK_PROCESS_VM_SELF | ON: process_vm_readv/process_vm_writev on the guest's OWN pid copy fault-free through mach_vm_read_overwrite with the kernel's answers (bytes copied, partial count at the first bad page, EFAULT, EINVAL for flags or >1024 iovecs); other pids keep ENOSYS | "0" | "0" restores the old ENOSYS stub for every pid, which sends libunity to its memcpy fallback (the Subway SIGSEGV in _platform_memmove, v1.0.1654 sweep). | bisect |
| MACAPK_PROCESS_VM_TRACE | Off | any non-"0" value | Logs `[procvm]` lines for process_vm_readv/writev: the first 16 calls, every EFAULT/partial (up to 64), and a running `total calls=N faults=M` every 4096 calls, with the caller lr. | MACAPK_PROCFS_STAT | OFF: a synthesized /proc file reports the real host temp-file's stat() values | any set value | Makes stat/lstat/fstat on synthesized procfs files and Android /data-dir paths report Linux-procfs-faithful values (size 0, proc dev 0x13, uid/gid 0, or the /data partition's measured identity) instead. | bisect |
| MACAPK_PROC_FULL_MAPS | ON: /proc/self/maps reports the full real region census, not just guest PT_LOADs | "0" restores the pre-v1.0.1000 guest-PT_LOAD-only output | Lets a regression in any maps consumer be bisected in one run against the old, narrower maps content. | bisect |
| MACAPK_PROC_SMAPS_TTL_MS | 100 ms | an integer (milliseconds) | Sets the cache lifetime of the synthesized /proc/self/smaps census, since a real device's memory layout can't change faster than this. | dev |

| MACAPK_PTRACE_EMU | OFF: a guest clone(flags=0) self-supervisor call returns -ENOSYS and the app tolerates the failure | any set, non-"0" value | Answers the guest's self-ptrace anti-debug scheme in-layer with a real fork()'d child servicing PEEK/POKE over a socketpair, with ATTACH honestly refused if a real debugger is already attached. | experiment |

IGNORED` line unless that gate is 0.** Log-only capture of the GS-coroutine WorkerPool pump's (pthread, TaskGroup) pair, so the pump thread can be identified before wiring the completion-pump redirect. | diag |
| MACAPK_PUMP_CAPTURE_ADDR | 0x13203b28 (the hardcoded TaskGroup::queue owner-branch offset) | a hex file-offset | **RETIRED by default (2026-09-27): read through `MACAPK_MCPE_SIGNIN_PROBES_RETIRED`, ignored with one `[mcpe-probes] ... | MACAPK_PUMP_DRAIN_HOST | the host completion-pump does not additionally drain background WorkerPools off-MAIN | any set value | **RETIRED by default (2026-09-27): read through `MACAPK_MCPE_SIGNIN_PROBES_RETIRED`, ignored with one `[mcpe-probes] ... | MACAPK_REAL_LOOPER | ON (default since v1.0.391): the main thread runs a real, blocking Looper.loop()/MessageQueue.next() model, and prepare()'d threads get a genuine per-thread queue | "0" reverts to the legacy per-frame Handler.drainGlobal model | Gates the whole AOSP-faithfulness campaign (real ViewRootImpl, Choreographer, Compose, HandlerThread, Unity's render-thread Looper) on a real Looper model instead of a per-frame drain stand-in. | bisect |
| MACAPK_MAIN_LOOP_REENTRY | ON (2026-10-01): a re-entrant `Looper.loop()` on the thread already running the real main loop returns to the outer loop | "0" restores the old fall-through into `NativeLooper.runMainPoll` | ActivityHost.start/startAll call `Looper.loop()` after posting LAUNCH_ACTIVITY, re-entrantly on every in-app launch from main-thread code. The old fall-through ran the shim's native-only owner poll, which never returns once the app has an ALooper fd on the main thread (Mapbox's RunLoop): the Java main queue then never drained again (Efteling stuck on its splash, the next activity never created, Fabric never mounted). Gate `loopreentry`; detector FREEZE-NESTED-LOOP. docs/main-loop-reentry-native-poll-starvation_2026-10-01.md | bisect |
| MACAPK_TEXTVIEW_SPAN_LINES | ON (2026-10-01): a wrapped TextView whose text carries a MetricAffectingSpan or LineHeightSpan takes each line's top/baseline (draw) and the block height (onMeasure's wrapped branch) from a StaticLayout over the same text, paint and width (only when that Layout breaks the lines identically) | "0" = the span-blind line positions (base-paint FontMetrics), byte-identical | AOSP's TextView.onDraw draws mLayout, whose line metrics are the union over the line's span runs and then every LineHeightSpan.chooseHeight. React Native styles all text with spans on a default-size TextView, so every line of a paragraph was painted over the first. Unspanned / colour-only text is untouched (gate `spanlines` S4/S5 hash-identical on both arms). | bisect |
| MACAPK_RECOVER_GUEST_NULLCALL | RETIRED 2026-09-27 (deleted; the default branch-to-NULL shape in the null recovery covers pc=0) | any set value | Prints one "RETIRED and IGNORED" line at handler install; changes nothing. | retired |

| MACAPK_REGISTERNATIVES_VERBATIM | ON (every native is registered as the app gave it, as ART does: no by-NAME nativeRender swap, so no Unity frame-boundary wrap, no Director / coroutine / census / scene-integrate / render-state driver, and no Director-cluster scan at libunity load; knobs read only by that chain are ignored with one RETIRED line) | `0` | `=0` restores the v1.0.233..2026-09-26 path exactly: a native named nativeRender is swapped for the wrap that runs DirectorManager::Update phases 0/1/2 and the coroutine node after every real call, and the sub-knobs (MACAPK_DIRECTOR_OFF, MACAPK_CORO_PUMP, MACAPK_PLOOP_CENSUS, MACAPK_PLOOP_DUMP, MACAPK_DIRECTOR_DUMP, MACAPK_SCENE_INTEGRATE, MACAPK_RENDER_STATE) are live again. (MACAPK_UNITY_PRESENT's post-render swap is not: it was deleted outright on 2026-09-27, see that row.) Kept for one clean fleet cycle, then the wrap, the drivers and this gate are deleted. Gate debug/geh_unity-nativerender-wrap-directorpump_gate.sh; docs/unity-nativerender-registered-verbatim_2026-09-27.md. | bisect |

IGNORED` line unless that gate is 0.** Caps that one infinite cond_wait (by return address) so a timeout returns nonzero, making ProcessMessages re-drain instead of parking forever. | bisect |
| MACAPK_RWLOCK_SPIN | 0, off: a contended rwlock immediately parks | an integer iteration count (bare "1" = 20000) | Diagnostic instrument: spins on try-lock with sched_yield() before parking, to test whether Bionic-faithful parking starved the fairness mechanism the MCPE sign-in stall depends on. | diag |

| MACAPK_SCENE_INTEGRATE | RETIRED 2026-09-27: ignored by default (MACAPK_REGISTERNATIVES_VERBATIM); under =0, OFF | any set value, libunity-only, byte-checked, read only under MACAPK_REGISTERNATIVES_VERBATIM=0 | Under MACAPK_REGISTERNATIVES_VERBATIM=0 only: calls the engine's own PreloadManager step each frame (skipping while a load is in flight) so any completed scene gets integrated in-context. | MACAPK_SELF_SAMPLE | OFF (one getenv at load, no thread) | a trigger-file path | Arms an IN-PROCESS all-thread stack sampler: a watcher polls the file every 250 ms; writing `"<rounds> <interval_ms> [<min_cpu_ms>]"` (default `3 300 0`) into it prints every thread's name, run state, CPU and frame-record stack (`[self-sample] ...`, guest frames as `guest <lib>+<off>`), then deletes the file. For when `sample`/`lldb -p` cannot attach (a pending Developer Tools authorization blocks task_for_pid; measured 2026-10-01 on a frozen Minecraft). Async-safe: one thread suspended at a time, nothing allocates while it is. Read it with `debug/selfsample.py`. | diag |

| MACAPK_SIGCHAIN | ON (2026-09-27): libsigchain semantics for arm64 guests. sigaction/signal for Linux SEGV/BUS/ILL/TRAP/ABRT/FPE is RECORDED (not installed), returned as oldact, and run with a Linux siginfo_t + Bionic ucontext_t only for a fault the runtime's recoveries and HotSpot (JVM_handle_bsd_signal(...,0)) both decline; sigprocmask cannot block the claimed set nor bits 9/11 (host SIGBUS/SIGSYS); SIGABRT/SIGFPE get front handlers; a Linux 12 (SIGUSR2) install is refused (its raw slot is Darwin SIGSYS, the inline-syscall forwarder); a handler that leaves through the shim siglongjmp/longjmp/_longjmp ends its delivery; x18 preservation is set before a guest handler runs | "0" disables | Restores the v1.0.348 refusal (zeroed oldact, never run), raw queries, no mask filter, the plain SIGABRT handler, and raw Linux 12 installs onto SIGSYS. | MACAPK_SIGNALING_REDRIVE_MS | off (0/unset), libminecraftpe-only | an integer (milliseconds) | **RETIRED by default (2026-09-27): read through `MACAPK_MCPE_SIGNIN_PROBES_RETIRED`, ignored with one `[mcpe-probes] ... IGNORED` line unless that gate is 0.** Candidate fix for a token-async state stuck by a lost scheduling race: captures each stuck state via a NotifyB trap, then a watchdog replays its transition plus the executor submit after the delay. | bisect |
| MACAPK_SIGNIN_DRAIN | OFF: only MAIN (via the other MAIN_DRAIN mechanisms) ever drains the sign-in WorkerPool P | any set value | **Inert by default (2026-09-27): a sub-knob consulted only after a member of the retired MCPE sign-in probe family armed, so it has no effect unless `MACAPK_MCPE_SIGNIN_PROBES_RETIRED=0`.** The "emulator-grounded hop-B": a dedicated host thread reproduces the emulator's concurrent multi-worker drain of P, only while MAIN is actively parked in the sign-in wait. | bisect |
| MACAPK_SKIP_LIBS | every native library the guest loads is loaded normally | a comma-separated list of library names (e.g. "fmod") | Forces the listed libraries to use the empty stub fallback instead of being loaded/translated. | dev |
| MACAPK_INLINE_SIGSYSCALLS_REAL | "virt" (2026-09-27): on the svc->BRK path, inline tkill/tgkill (130/131), rt_sigsuspend (133), rt_sigaction (134, into the MACAPK_SIGCHAIN registry) and rt_sigprocmask (135, edits the interrupted context's uc_sigmask) are serviced by the libc shims in the kernel convention; sigaltstack (132) is recorded per thread and reported back but never installed on the host | "0" = old fake 0; "1" = real, sigaltstack installed too; unset / "virt" / "2" = the default | "0" restores the old fake-0 neutralize (copied from the SIGSYS path, where x0 really is lost). "1" is NOT the default because routing sigaltstack to the host crashed HotSpot C2 (Arena::grow) on IG 5/5 on a different route; it becomes the default only after IG main feed 5/5 and MCPE sign-in with it. | bisect |
| MACAPK_EVENTFD_COUNTER | ON (2026-09-28): eventfd(2) has the kernel's counter semantics -- one u64 per eventfd kept in the shim; write() adds (EAGAIN only if the sum would pass 0xff..fe under O_NONBLOCK, EINVAL for ULLONG_MAX or a non-8-byte write), read() returns the sum (1 under EFD_SEMAPHORE) and subtracts it (EAGAIN only when 0 under O_NONBLOCK); the fd is a self-connected AF_UNIX datagram carrier holding at most ONE token while count>0, so poll/epoll readiness is exact and every write re-arms an EPOLLET edge; unknown flags -> EINVAL; dup/dup2/dup3/F_DUPFD aliases share the counter (review pass, same day: dup/dup2 had no shim at all before, so an alias made that way was unregistered); a non-owner process (a forked child, whose C++ state is COW-copied and diverges from the true owner's) has its write reconciled via a carrier drain on the owner's next read/write, rather than left stuck | "0" disables | Restores the loopback-UDP datagram model byte for byte (unsummed reads, EFD_SEMAPHORE value ignored, un-read writes queue until macOS silently drops them past ~4k, a write->read on one thread can miss). timerfd keeps the UDP socket either way. Gate: `debug/gates.sh eventfd`. docs/eventfd-counter-and-rw-errno_2026-09-28.md | bisect |
| MACAPK_RW_ERRNO_LINUX | ON (2026-09-28): a failing guest read()/write()/pread()/pwrite()/pread64()/pwrite64()/__read_chk/__write_chk/__pread_chk/__pwrite_chk/__pread64_chk/eventfd()/eventfd_read()/eventfd_write() (arm64 faces, and ARM32 read/write) leaves errno in LINUX numbering (errno_mac2lin: EAGAIN 11, not Darwin 35) | "0" disables | Restores the raw Darwin errno (the pre-fix value; Instagram's folly EventBaseAtomicNotificationQueue drainFd CHECK read `errno = 35`). The raw syscall router keeps calling the host-errno cores either way. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ROUTER_WRITE | ON (2026-09-28): the raw syscall router (svc->BRK inline path and the redirected syscall() wrappers) services Linux nr 64 write through the same core as libc write() (an eventfd write adds to its counter) | "0" disables | Restores `-ENOSYS` for a raw write. Instagram's libappstatelogger2 writes its termination status with a raw write from its SIGTERM handler and logged `Error writing to app state log file` on every SIGTERM. Gate: `debug/gates.sh eventfd` (raw rows). docs/eventfd-counter-and-rw-errno_2026-09-28.md | bisect |
| MACAPK_EVENTFD_TRACE | off | any value but "0" | Diagnostic: logs `[eventfd] read fd=N on EMPTY counter -> EAGAIN (#k, thread ...)` for a non-blocking eventfd read that found the counter at 0 (first 16, then each power of two) -- the event folly's drainFd CHECKs for `errno == EAGAIN`. Counter model only. | diag |
| MACAPK_KEEP_APP_CRASHHANDLER | Off: the guest's actions for SIGSEGV/SIGBUS/SIGABRT/SIGFPE/SIGILL/SIGTRAP are recorded by MACAPK_SIGCHAIN (or refused under =0) and the runtime's front handlers stay installed | any set value | Restores the guest app's own signal handler for those signals instead of leaving them owned by Mac.apk's crash-recovery net/embedded JVM; may break the runtime's own recovery net. New, generalized name for the legacy MACAPK_UNITY_KEEP_CRASHHANDLER (both OR'd together at the same check). Takes precedence over MACAPK_SIGCHAIN: the raw install is the Candy Crush order the gate's J2 row uses as its control. | diag |
| MACAPK_LAZY_DEX | Off: classes.jar is eagerly converted at install | any set value | Skips the eager DEX-to-classes.jar conversion at install, deferring to per-class lazy conversion instead. | experiment |
| MACAPK_LIFTED_SEGV_PC_GATE | Some default gating behavior for a lifted function's SIGSEGV PC handling | 0 | Kill switch: `=0` restores the old unconditional behavior this gate replaced; effect not further documented at the site beyond that. | bisect |

IGNORED` line unless that gate is 0.** Installs a cooperative-drain trap on MAIN that helps deliver the GS sign-in completion (a WorkerPool P task); part of the v650 MCPE 54%-sign-in-stall fix family. Implied by `MACAPK_MAIN_DRAIN_PUMP`. | experiment |

A dep from a SoLoader UNPACKING root (lib-compressed / lib-main) keeps `System.load` at premap: on a device SoLoader itself loads those (RESOLVE_DEPENDENCIES), registering them and running JNI_OnLoad | `0` | `=0` restores the old premap, `System.load` via the app loader for EVERY dep, byte-identical (it ran every dependency's JNI_OnLoad before any app code: the Efteling fbjni ThreadScopeSupport "NativeLoader has not been initialized" SIGABRT). Gate `debug/soloader_premap_gate.sh`; docs/soloader-premap-jni-onload-timing_2026-09-29.md | bisect |

Superseded by MACAPK_SV_GUEST_BELOW (below + hole, default ON). Setting it now does nothing. | retired |
| MACAPK_SVC_BRK | ON (v1.0.1622): the Stage-6 loader rewrites guest inline `svc #0` to `BRK #0x4D41` and the SIGTRAP emulator services it with x8/x0..x5 intact | "0" disables | Leaves `svc #0` for XNU, which reads the number from x16 (residual junk) and can silently run a live Darwin syscall. | bisect |

The slots follow the LIVE canvas overlay when it shares the layer's superlayer. Note: docs/surfaceview-native-window-hole_2026-09-30.md; gate `svguestz`. | bisect |
| MACAPK_SV_GL_LAYER_RECT | ON (2026-10-01): a GLSurfaceView / reflected renderer whose view the window box cannot hold -- LARGER than its window (a crop/fill resolution policy: Flappy Bird's AndEngine RenderSurfaceView, 1346x2392 centred at y=-298 in a 1346x1796 window) or off the window box's GL bottom-left corner (a GL view above a banner) -- is told its own laid-out size (AOSP SurfaceView.updateSurface :985-988) and the framework ANGLE layer is moved onto the view's rect every frame (SurfaceView.macapkGlLayerRectPacked -> cocoa_bridge MacapkApplyGlLayerRect), the layer cropped to the window box by a layer mask (and the host view's bounds). Logs "[I] Mac.apk.SurfaceView: GL layer follows <view>" and "[I] cocoa_v2: ANGLE layer follows the GL view: x,y wxh px in the WxH window box". The anchored case (bouncy) and window-filling views are untouched; not while a native-EGL engine owns rendering. | "0" | Restores the window box for such a renderer (the scene drawn squashed into the window while its MotionEvents stay view-local, so touches land at the wrong point -- the owner's "Flappy Bird does not respond to touch") and never publishes a rect; Java answers byte-identical to the pre-change file (gate `svgeometry`, CTL-OFF-ALL / CTL-GLLAYER).
