MACAPK_SV_LAYER_AT_CREATE fallback" once and runs the pre-change body | "0" (any value starting with 0) | =0 restores byte for byte the three pre-change paths: the v1.0.162 first-frame pre-create keyed on the libunity soname (g_unity_app; framebufferOnly=YES, angleLayer hidden at once), the on-main on-demand creation (v1.0.572) and the 2 s off-main hand-off (MACAPK_NO_OFFMAIN_EGL_LAYER). | MACAPK_V8_FLAGS | "--jitless" (avoids a W^X-toggle/I-cache codegen corruption that SIGSEGVs MCPE's cohtml/V8 in TurboFan) | any V8 flag string; "" disables the override entirely | Overrides the flag string passed to a V8-embedding guest's own SetFlagsFromString before V8::Initialize freezes them; a no-op for non-V8 guests. | bisect |
| MACAPK_VERIFY_SYMBOL_HASH | off: the hash-walk dynsym lookup() is not cross-checked | "1" | TEMPORARY verification mode rebuilding the old eager symbol map in a scope-local copy and asserting the hash-walk lookup returns identical results, including identical NOT-FOUND for known-absent names. | diag |
| MACAPK_VK_WINDOW_SURFACE | ON (since 2026-09-27): a native guest's eglCreateWindowSurface on the ANGLE Vulkan path gets the WINDOW surface it asked for (ANGLE WindowSurfaceVkMac -> MoltenVK VkSwapchain -> its CAMetalLayer), as on Android (eglCreateWindowSurfaceTmpl; BufferQueue backpressure). The decouple readback ring, readback throttle and PresentPacer stay inert. Logs "[arm64-shim] eglCreateWindowSurface: VULKAN window surface (MACAPK_VK_WINDOW_SURFACE default ...)" once where the old rule would have substituted | "0" (any value starting with 0) | =0 restores the v612 offscreen-decouple byte for byte: the guest gets an OFFSCREEN pbuffer, eglSwapBuffers does the async PBO readback and the runtime presents the guest layer from drawInMTKView (MACAPK_NO_DECOUPLE still opts out under =0). A/B lever for the retirement; gate `vkwinsurf`. Ref docs/offscreen-decouple-present-retired_2026-09-27.md. | bisect |
| MACAPK_VSYNC_THREAD | ON (since 2026-09-28): the display vsync tick (NDK AChoreographer fire, per-thread Java Choreographer vsync, FramePacer grid, the Swappy bridge when installed) runs on a dedicated `macapk-vsync` thread driven by `-[NSWindow displayLinkWithTarget:selector:]` (CADisplayLink on that thread's run loop, pinned to the drive rate via `preferredFrameRateRange`), so an AppKit main-thread stall no longer withholds vsync from the guest (AOSP: SurfaceFlinger delivers vsync to each Looper independently). Vulkan present credit is the ONE exception (review issue 2): while `CocoaDecoupleIsActive()` (the offscreen-decouple pbuffer, `MACAPK_VK_WINDOW_SURFACE=0`), the credit still comes from drawInMTKView on the main thread right after it composites, because that composite -- not a vsync -- is the credit's real AOSP-BufferQueue consumer; off the decouple path it comes from the vsync thread same as everything else. drawInMTKView drains only its own looper's AChoreographer callbacks, and ticks itself while the vsync thread has not beaten for 3 periods. Logs "[vsync] display vsync now ticks on its own thread" once | `0` | Every tick from drawInMTKView on the main thread, the pre-2026-09-28 path (MacapkDriveTickAt is the same body either way). Also the old path when the shim lacks `__macapk_arm64_choreographer_run_ready` or the window has no display link | behaviour |
| MACAPK_MAIN_STALL_MS | off (no watchdog thread) | a number 5-10000 (ms) | Diagnostic: a watchdog samples WHERE the AppKit main thread is once it goes that long without entering drawInMTKView (thread_suspend + PAC-stripped frame-pointer walk + dladdr after resume): `[main-stall] +<ms> #k: sym+off <- ...`, 6 samples 8 ms apart then one a second (up to 20), and `[main-stall] ended after <ms>`. Use it instead of `sample`, which freezes the runtime for the whole sample | diag |
| MACAPK_VMSPACE_PROBE | off | "1" | Diagnostic-only report of the process's address-space layout via mach_vm_region_recurse, checking the sub-16 GiB region census relevant to guest-pointer/host-mapping placement. | diag |
| MACAPK_WAITSITE_LOG | RETIRED 2026-09-27: deleted with the Crossy 7.11.1 il2cpp diag kit; setting it prints one `[il2cpp-diag] ... RETIRED and IGNORED` line | any set value | Logs the deduplicated caller return-address of every distinct wait-primitive call once armed, so a deadlocking libunity wait-wrapper can be identified by subtracting the module's load base. | diag |
| MACAPK_WAKE_BGPOOLS | off | any set value | **RETIRED by default (2026-09-27): read through `MACAPK_MCPE_SIGNIN_PROBES_RETIRED`, ignored with one `[mcpe-probes] ... IGNORED` line unless that gate is 0.** REFUTED as a fix (mechanism description kept accurate): level-triggers every background WorkerPool captured by MACAPK_PUMP_DRAIN_HOST's park-site hook while MAIN is in the sign-in wait; a controlled A/B showed no reliable benefit. | diag |
| MACAPK_WAKE_MODE | "full" (set the work flag AND broadcast the cond on every captured pool) | "full", "bcast", "flag", or "nudge" | **Inert by default (2026-09-27): a sub-knob consulted only after a member of the retired MCPE sign-in probe family armed, so it has no effect unless `MACAPK_MCPE_SIGNIN_PROBES_RETIRED=0`.** Ablation switch for MACAPK_WAKE_BGPOOLS distinguishing work-stealing (targeted drain) from mere scheduler perturbation. | diag |
| MACAPK_WAKE_SIGNIN_MS | off (0/unset) | an integer (milliseconds) | **RETIRED by default (2026-09-27): read through `MACAPK_MCPE_SIGNIN_PROBES_RETIRED`, ignored with one `[mcpe-probes] ... IGNORED` line unless that gate is 0.** The Fable-confirmed 54% sign-in fix: every N ms, level-triggers the sign-in pool's own worker cond so a parked worker drains the stranded `deliver` task off-MAIN (running it on MAIN would deadlock on the completion mutex). | bisect |
| MACAPK_WAKE_SUBMIT | off | any set value | **Inert by default (2026-09-27): a sub-knob consulted only after a member of the retired MCPE sign-in probe family armed, so it has no effect unless `MACAPK_MCPE_SIGNIN_PROBES_RETIRED=0`.** v672 targeted nudge: also wakes the ACTUAL submit-target WorkerPools (captured via MACAPK_SUBMIT_POOL_DRAIN) during the sign-in wait, rather than only the bgpark-captured siblings an overlap experiment showed are different pools. | bisect |
| MACAPK_WATCH_ADDR | the lifter's per-store trace call does nothing | a hex guest address | Logs every traced 32-bit guest store whose target lies within +/- MACAPK_WATCH_ADDR_MASK bytes of this address, to pinpoint which lifted instruction writes a specific location. | diag |
| MACAPK_WATCH_ADDR_MASK | 7 (an 8-byte slop window) | a hex byte count | Sets the +/- window around MACAPK_WATCH_ADDR (or MACAPK_WATCH_INDIRECT's resolved address) within which a traced store is logged. | diag |
| MACAPK_WATCH_GUEST_ADDRS | the guest-global watcher is not installed | comma-separated hex guest vaddrs | Samples the given static guest globals every MACAPK_WATCH_GUEST_MS and logs each change, for a Stage-6 guest, complementing the ARM32-only store-trace mechanism. | diag |
| MACAPK_WATCH_GUEST_LIB | the guest-global watcher does not arm for any library | a substring of the target library's debug name | Restricts MACAPK_WATCH_GUEST_ADDRS's sampling to only the named guest library's load. | diag |
| MACAPK_WATCH_GUEST_MS | 1000 ms sample period | an integer (milliseconds) | Sets the sample period for MACAPK_WATCH_GUEST_ADDRS. | diag |
| MACAPK_WATCH_GUEST_N | 400 max samples | an integer sample-count cap | Bounds the total samples MACAPK_WATCH_GUEST_ADDRS takes so a long run cannot flood the log (it only ever reads, so this cannot corrupt anything). | diag |
| MACAPK_WATCH_INDIRECT | MACAPK_WATCH_ADDR (if any) is a fixed address | a hex address of a stable guest global that holds the address to watch | Watches whatever object the given global currently points at, rather than a fixed address, so a heap victim whose address changes run to run can still be pinned. | diag |
| MACAPK_WATCH_VALUE | logs every traced store within the watch window regardless of value | an integer | Filters MACAPK_WATCH_ADDR's logged stores to only those writing this specific value. | diag |
| MACAPK_WATCH_WRITE_VADDR | no watchpoint page is armed | a hex guest vaddr | mprotects the 16 KiB host page containing that vaddr to PROT_READ, so the first (or, re-armed, every) lifted store to it SIGSEGVs into a handler that logs the writer (symbol + backtrace + decoded value), then re-opens the page and lets the store retry. | diag |
| MACAPK_WEBVIEW_PROBE_FILE | the WebView page-state trace timer never evaluates an ad hoc question | a filesystem path | Whenever that file's mtime changes, its contents are evaluated as JavaScript in the live WebView page and the result printed as a PROBE log line. | diag |
| MACAPK_WEBVIEW_SAMEDOC | ON: a same-document (fragment-only) navigation delivers doUpdateVisitedHistory + onPageFinished, matching AOSP/Chromium | "0" disables | A/B lever for that navigation-delivery fix (Aurora Store's Google sign-in depends on it and would otherwise hang on the last hop). | bisect |
| MACAPK_WEBVIEW_TRACE | off: no WebView navigation/page-content logging | any set, non-"0" value | Logs every finished page's title/URL/user-agent/visible text plus ongoing in-page script activity between navigations, the only way to inspect WKWebView content since it's a native NSView sibling the Canvas frame-dump tooling can't see. | diag |
| MACAPK_WP_CAP_END_MS | 130000 ms (the cap window self-lifts at 130s) | an integer (milliseconds) | End of the time window during which MACAPK_WP_WORKER_CAP's worker cap is active. | diag |
| MACAPK_WP_CAP_START_MS | 6000 ms (the cap engages 6s in, past early boot) | an integer (milliseconds) | Start of the time window during which MACAPK_WP_WORKER_CAP's worker cap becomes active. | diag |
| MACAPK_WP_DRAIN_MS | off (0/unset); libminecraftpe-gated | an integer (milliseconds) | **RETIRED by default (2026-09-27): read through `MACAPK_MCPE_SIGNIN_PROBES_RETIRED`, ignored with one `[mcpe-probes] ... IGNORED` line unless that gate is 0.** The 54%-signaling-stall fix at the WorkerPool level-trigger layer: captures each pool at its worker-park site, then a watchdog sets its work flag and broadcasts its cond every N ms so a parked worker re-runs dispatch and drains a stranded cross-thread post. | bisect |
| MACAPK_WP_DRAIN_SITE | 0x1325cfe4 (the current build's worker-park file offset) | a hex file-offset | **RETIRED by default (2026-09-27): read through `MACAPK_MCPE_SIGNIN_PROBES_RETIRED`, ignored with one `[mcpe-probes] ... IGNORED` line unless that gate is 0.** Overrides the untimed worker-park address MACAPK_WP_DRAIN_MS captures its target pool from, for a build where that offset has moved. | diag |
| MACAPK_WP_QUEUE_PROBE | off | any set value | **RETIRED by default (2026-09-27): read through `MACAPK_MCPE_SIGNIN_PROBES_RETIRED`, ignored with one `[mcpe-probes] ... IGNORED` line unless that gate is 0.** Pure observer (never wakes anything): logs each MACAPK_PUMP_DRAIN_HOST-captured background pool's queue-emptiness/flag/state during the sign-in wait. | diag |
| MACAPK_WP_WORKER_CAP | off: every Bedrock WorkerPool worker runs concurrently as normal | an integer N (workers allowed per start-routine) | Non-viable as a shipped fix (wedges boot; the racing pool is boot-critical) but kept as a gated diagnostic: parks the (N+1)th+ instance of a thread-pool's workers at their idle wait, only during a time-gated window. | diag |
| MACAPK_X18_EXEC_ONLY | ON (2026-09-29): the Stage-6 x18 platform-register rewrite stops each .eh_frame_hdr function window at the first byte positively inside a NON-executable section of the r-x PT_LOAD (and skips a window that starts in one), using the section table read_exec_sections already reads for MACAPK_SVC_BRK_EXEC_ONLY | "0" restores the whole-window scan (the last window runs to the end of the PF_X segment, capped 1 MiB) byte for byte, and x18 plan version 2 on every library (the default arm uses version 3 only on a library where it cuts or skips a window) | In BFD/gold-linked libraries .rodata/.eh_frame follow .text in the r-x segment, so the old last window renamed x18 in DATA: Jetpack 2 "JniIdTokenListener" became "&niIdTokenListener" and the game sat at 59% loading. 32 of 179 installed arm64 libraries were exposed (Firebase C++ SDK, libSDL2, libconscrypt_jni, Hill Climb, GD Meltdown ...). No usable section headers -> the old window, logged. Plan version is per library (3 only where exec-only changes the pass, else 2), so unaffected libraries keep their plan. Gate: `debug/x18execonly_gate.sh` (controls C1, C2). docs/x18-rewrite-data-window_2026-09-29.md | bisect |
| MACAPK_X18_CACHE | ON: the x18-rewrite scan's clean/dirty decision is cached per-.so (keyed on a content hash), skipping a ~40s re-scan on a cache hit | "0" disables the cache | Forces a full x18-rewrite scan on every launch, for bisecting the cache's correctness or measuring true scan cost. | bisect |
| MACAPK_X18_DEBUG | off | any set value | Verbose per-instruction logging of the x18 platform-register rewrite pass's decisions plus a post-pass scan reporting every x18 instruction that still survives unrewritten. | diag |
| MACAPK_X18_DUMPWOFF | off | a hex byte-offset within the function window being analyzed | Dumps the computed use/def/livein/liveout register masks and successor edges for every instruction within +/-0x40 of the given offset, for hand-debugging the x18-rewrite liveness analysis. | diag |
| MACAPK_X18_FN | 0 (no function traced) | a hex file-offset | Traces the x18-rewrite pass's per-window decision (status/instructions-rewritten/thunked) for whichever function window contains this offset. | diag |
| MACAPK_X18_LIMIT | -1 (no limit: every eligible window is rewritten) | an integer N | Bisect knob (never for production): commits x18 rewrites only for the first N committing function windows, for narrowing which window's rewrite causes a regression. | diag |
| MACAPK_X18_LIMIT_INSN | -1 (no per-window instruction limit) | an integer M | On the boundary window selected by MACAPK_X18_LIMIT, commits only its first M instructions (by address) instead of the whole window. | diag |
| MACAPK_X18_PLAN_VERIFY | off: a cached dirty x18 plan is replayed (only recorded write-something windows re-analyzed) | "1" (or any value != "0") | Loads the cached plan but also runs the full scan, then asserts the two effect-window sets agree -- the in-process half of the proof that a replay is byte-identical to a full re-scan. | diag |
| MACAPK_X18_POOL_BAREHINT | the x18 detour-thunk pool is placed via a reachability-checked closest-first search within +/-64 MB of the executable segment | "1" | Reproduces the pre-v1.0.762 bare-mmap-hint placement with no reachability check, which the kernel can silently ignore under ASLR, reproducing the historical "Subway residual x18 ~1-in-6" nondeterminism -- kept only to A/B that claim. | bisect |
| MACAPK_X18_REWRITE_GENERIC | ON: the generic x18 platform-register rewrite pass runs on every loaded image's executable segment | "0" opts out | The app-agnostic fix for XNU zeroing the reserved x18 register on EL0 return; the last line of defense on any host below the macOS 26.4 x18-entitlement floor, since the kernel-level preserve_x18 gate this pass overlapped with was deleted at v1.0.1018. | bisect |
| MACAPK_X18_THUNK | ON: an x18 web with no free GPR to rename into gets lowered to a SIMD-register detour thunk | "0" disables thunk lowering (also drops the thunk pool's mmap reservation) | Kill switch for detour-thunk lowering; without it those nofree webs simply stay residual x18, covered only by the older prefault/recovery mitigations. | bisect |
| MACAPK_XAC_CAPTURE | OFF; HttpClient-lib-gated | any set value | **RETIRED by default (2026-09-27): read through `MACAPK_MCPE_SIGNIN_PROBES_RETIRED`, ignored with one `[mcpe-probes] ... IGNORED` line unless that gate is 0.** Traps XAsyncComplete (the one place every HTTP completion is submitted) to capture the GS completion's ACTUAL queue with no offset guessing, feeding it into both the completion-pump dispatch set and the dedicated MAIN_DRAIN_XACONLY set. | bisect |
| MACAPK_XSUBMIT_PUMP | OFF; libminecraftpe-gated | any set value | **RETIRED by default (2026-09-27): read through `MACAPK_MCPE_SIGNIN_PROBES_RETIRED`, ignored with one `[mcpe-probes] ... IGNORED` line unless that gate is 0.** The v655 fix: captures each auth XTaskQueue and starts a dedicated host thread dispatching both ports of every captured queue, so sign-in continuations run off-MAIN while MAIN sits in its wait. | bisect |
| MACAPK_XTQ_SERIALIZE | not installed: MCPE's (Manual,Manual) XTaskQueues are pumped only by the guest's own per-frame driver, unreliable once MAIN blocks | an integer mode 0-3 (Manual=0, ThreadPool=1, SerializedThreadPool=2, Immediate=3; an out-of-range value clamps to 2); HttpClient-lib-gated | **RETIRED by default (2026-09-27): read through `MACAPK_MCPE_SIGNIN_PROBES_RETIRED`, ignored with one `[mcpe-probes] ... IGNORED` line unless that gate is 0.** Overrides libHttpClient's XTaskQueueCreate to force the completion port (and, with MACAPK_XTQ_SERIALIZE_PORTS=both, the work port) into SerializedThreadPool mode, matching Android's effectively single-threaded work port. | bisect |
| MACAPK_XTQ_SERIALIZE_PORTS | "comp" (only the completion port is serialized) | "comp" or "both" | **RETIRED by default (2026-09-27): read through `MACAPK_MCPE_SIGNIN_PROBES_RETIRED`, ignored with one `[mcpe-probes] ... IGNORED` line unless that gate is 0.** With MACAPK_XTQ_SERIALIZE set, also serializes the work port when set to "both". | bisect |
## Networking / GMS / FCM

| Flag | Default (unset) | Accepted values | Effect | Audience |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| MACAPK_ADDRCONFIG | Enabled -- DNS lookups are filtered to match AOSP libcore's AI_ADDRCONFIG behavior | 0/false disables; anything else/unset = enabled | Default ON, AOSP-faithful filtering of DNS results by which address families the host actually has routes for. MACAPK_ADDRCONFIG=0 disables the filtering so every lookup passes through unfiltered, for A/B without a rebuild. | bisect |
| MACAPK_ALPN | Disabled -- platform SSLSockets get no ALPN negotiation | any non-null value enables (checked via `!= null`) | When set, serves ALPN for sockets produced by the platform's own (boot/platform-classloader) TLS stack, mirroring what gates OkHttp's Android10SocketAdapter on a device. Default off because it changes OkHttp's wire protocol, multiplexing and delivery threading fleet-wide and is not yet measured. | experiment |
| MACAPK_CRASHLYTICS_TRACE | Off -- Crashlytics calls are silently no-op'd | any non-null value enables | Prints every Crashlytics call (custom keys/logs, including the boot-stage markers some apps funnel through it) to stderr as "[crashlytics-trace]" lines, showing how far boot progressed. | diag |
| MACAPK_GMS_AUTH_APP | "com.android.vending" | a package name string | Overrides the "app"/"callerPkg" identity field sent on every Google /auth token request, so the owner can run that identity experiment deliberately, without a rebuild. | experiment |
| MACAPK_GMS_AUTH_SIG | Our own platform certificate's SHA-1 | a signature SHA-1 hash string | Overrides the "client_sig"/"callerSig" identity field sent on every Google /auth token request, paired with MACAPK_GMS_AUTH_APP for the same deliberate, explicit identity experiment. | experiment |
| MACAPK_GMS_DIR | `~/Library/Application Support/Mac.apk/gms` | a directory path | Overrides where the GMS state file (signed-in account, checkin android_id/security_token, cached tokens) is stored on disk. | dev |
| MACAPK_GMS_FCM | Enabled (default ON since v1.0.1179) -- the in-process FCM/push backend is live | 0/false disables, restoring the pre-v1.0.1179 contract | Gates whether com.google.android.gms answers as present with the c2dm SEND permission and a resolvable register service, i.e. whether Firebase getToken() can actually register through the bound PushRegisterService seam. =0 restores the old contract where that path is unreached. | bisect |
| MACAPK_GMS_FINSKY_UA | Mac.apk's own honest User-Agent is sent to Google's licensing endpoint | 1/true switches to the Finsky (Play Store client) User-Agent string | Swaps in the real Play Store client's "Android-Finsky/..." User-Agent so any difference in Google's response between the two identities can be measured; a deliberate act by whoever sets it, never the default. | experiment |
| MACAPK_GMS_HONEST_IDENTITY | Off -- for the licensing token scope only, the Play Store's own client_sig/app identity is substituted (owner-authorized, scope-gated concession) | 1/true restores Mac.apk's own certificate for that request | Restores our own honest identity on the licensing token request instead of the Play Store's, which is how Google's refusal (UNREGISTERED_ON_API_CONSOLE) is reproduced and the honest posture demonstrated. | bisect |
| MACAPK_GMS_HONEST_MISSING | The "tuned" contract: availability() answers SUCCESS (provider present, each service fails honestly at its own call site) and gmsPackageInstalled() answers false | 1/true forces the fully-absent contract (SERVICE_MISSING); 0/false restores the legacy full spoof (gms package reported installed and permission-holding) | Three-way A/B switch over whether com.google.android.gms is present. =1 is a diagnosis-only fully-absent posture; =0 is the legacy pre-v1.0.574 spoof, kept only so that old behavior stays reachable for comparison. | bisect |
| MACAPK_GMS_KEYCHAIN | Off -- GMS secrets live only in the 0600 properties file | 1/true enables | Opt-in switch to also read/write GMS secrets via the macOS Keychain; kept opt-in because a Keychain item's ACL binds to the code signature, and the build re-signs the runtime every time, so a rebuilt binary reading an older item triggers an interactive access prompt that can hang a headless or Dock-launched run. | dev |
| MACAPK_GMS_PRESENT | Enabled (default ON since v1.0.1175) -- Mac.apk's own in-process GMS layer (real signed-in Google account, checkin identity, com.android.vending package + licensing) is live | 0/false disables, restoring the GMS-absent contract | Master gate for Mac.apk's own GMS layer; it does not change com.google.android.gms's own absent/package status, only what com.android.vending and AccountManager expose. =0 restores the pre-flip absent contract for A/B. | bisect |
| MACAPK_GMS_REMOTE_GRANT | Enabled -- a GetGoogleIdOption / One Tap request with filterByAuthorizedAccounts=true asks GOOGLE whether each account already authorized the calling app (/auth for the requested ID token, as the app's own package + its own signing-cert SHA-1, WITHOUT has_permission: Google issues the token only when the grant exists, else issueAdvice=consent; microG checkAccountAuthStatus). The answer is cached per account+package+client (signin.grant.<pkg> | <clientId>: yes 24 h, no 1 h); a network/refusal failure is not stored, the account is not offered (fail closed) and the same account/app/client is not re-asked for 60 s (in memory); the question has a 5 s connect + 10 s read budget and is asked off the caller's thread on BOTH entry points (Credential Manager answers through the app's executor; Identity beginSignIn replies later through its callback). The local record signin.authorized.<pkg> stays a positive hint with no network, and is still required for One Tap auto-select (a grant from another device is offered through the chooser) | 0 restores the local-record-only answer byte-for-byte (an account is "authorized" only after a sign-in on this Mac) | runtime/java/com/macapk/gms/GoogleSignInBroker.java (remoteGrantEnabled, probeGrants, probeGrantsAsync) | bisect |
| MACAPK_GMS_SIGNIN | Enabled -- com.google.android.gms answers Google sign-in: GOOGLE_SIGN_IN resolves to the in-process account chooser (com.macapk.gms.GoogleSignInActivity) the Identity API (IdentitySignInService: One Tap beginSignIn / getSignInIntent / signOut) and the framework CredentialManager answer GetGoogleIdOption / GetSignInWithGoogleOption, from the central Google account, with an ID token minted for the calling app's OWN package + signing cert after the user picks the account | 0 disables, restoring the pre-provider behaviour byte-for-byte (GOOGLE_SIGN_IN unresolved -> SignInHubActivity status 17; every CredentialManager get -> TYPE_NO_CREDENTIAL; the Identity START bind fails -> ApiException 17; clearCredentialState keeps doing nothing) | A/B lever for the Google sign-in provider (Reddit Continue with Google, Duolingo's Google button). Also inert while availability() is not SUCCESS (MACAPK_GMS_HONEST_MISSING=1). Gate: debug/gsignin_gate.sh (=0 must fail exactly R1 R3 C1 C2 C7 P1 C8 I1 I12). | bisect |
| MACAPK_ICON_ROUND | Enabled -- square, icon-sized decoded bitmaps with no explicit transform get the Android adaptive-icon rounded-square treatment, and the app's own Dock icon is masked to macOS's continuous "squircle" shape | 0 disables both (a sibling var, MACAPK_ICON_ROUND_N, tunes the squircle exponent) | Makes icons read as native by default: GlideRescue rounds square icon-sized images lacking their own transform, and cocoa_bridge masks the app's own Dock icon to macOS's superellipse corner. MACAPK_ICON_ROUND=0 disables both. | dev |
| MACAPK_NET_HOST_TRUTH | Enabled -- ConnectivityManager answers from the real host (default route, live transport, scutil) | 0 restores the old constant answer ("always WIFI, always connected") | Labelled bisect gate for the host-truth networking change, present so a future session can A/B it in one run instead of re-deriving the boundary. | bisect |
| MACAPK_OFFLINE | Off -- network state and DNS follow the host truthfully | 1/true forces an airplane-mode posture | Forces the framework to report a fully offline/de-Googled device (no default network) regardless of the host's real connectivity, and redirects JVM DNS lookups to a nonexistent hosts file; the reliable fix for apps that hang in a racy online-boot network handshake, and restores the old isolated-network behavior for users who explicitly want it. | user |
| MACAPK_PACKAGE | Unset -- if android.app.Application's singleton can't supply a package name, "the running package" resolves to null | a package name string | Fallback source for "the running package" that LicensingService's caller-attribution check (enforceCaller) needs, used when there is no live Application singleton to ask. | dev |
| MACAPK_TASK_DIAG | Off -- no census is kept | any non-null value enables | Records each TaskCompletionSource's creation stack and logs any still incomplete after a grace period, turning a silent async-gate stall into a named component; pure diagnostic, no behavior change. | diag |
| MACAPK_VERSION | `"1.0"` | a version string | Overrides the version component of the honest `"Mac.apk/<version> (Linux; U; Android ...)"` User-Agent string sent on every Google HTTP request. | dev |
## Logging & tracing

*(Trace/diag flags whose primary reader lives in a more specific subsystem file are kept with that subsystem's table instead -- e.g. `MACAPK_TRACE_SQL` is in Install & storage, `MACAPK_TRACE_TOUCH` is in Launch & window. This table holds the flags whose reader is a cross-cutting/hub file with no more specific subsystem home, plus the two master switches.)*

| Flag | Default (unset) | Accepted values | Effect | Audience |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| MACAPK_ARM32_JNI_TRACE_ARGS | tracing off | "1" (any value but empty or "0") | One `[jni-args] sig=... r0=.. r3=.. sp+0=.. sp+12=..` line per JVM-to-ARM32 native call through the shared marshaller (name-bound and RegisterNatives): the descriptor the walk used ("(none)" = the sig-miss heuristic) and the AAPCS32 soft-float image it built, so a jfloat 240.0f reads 0x43700000. The only outside view of a marshalling defect: a guest cannot tell "never got the float" from "got 0.0". | diag |
| MACAPK_ARM32_JNI_TRACE_RET | tracing off | 0/1 (any set value enables; getenv()!=null) | Prints the raw ARM32-to-JNI return value (hex, low byte, jboolean interpretation) for every name-bound (dlsym-resolved) native call return, to tell a guest-true/low-byte-zero value apart from HotSpot's narrow-type read. | diag |
| MACAPK_ARM64_INIT_VERBOSE | tracing off | 0/1 (any set value enables; getenv()!=null) | Logs each Stage-6 arm64 image's DT_INIT call and every .init_array[] entry invocation with its target address and file offset. | diag |

| MACAPK_DEV_TRACE | tracing off | 0/1 (any value except "0"/empty enables) | Master diagnostic switch (the Swift launcher's "Verbose Trace" toggle / --dev-trace CLI flag): a logical OR that force-enables MACAPK_TRACE_BL (mode 1) and MACAPK_TRACE_JNI_HANDLES without setting them individually, at roughly 1 fps cost for HCR-class games; despite the comment's broader claim ("every trace function in this file"), only those two call sites actually check it in code. This is the "one toggle enables a family" master switch, distinct from MACAPK_LOG_LEVEL (a log-severity threshold, see that row). | diag |
| MACAPK_DIRECTOR_DUMP | RETIRED 2026-09-27: ignored by default (MACAPK_REGISTERNATIVES_VERBATIM); under =0, tracing off | any set value (getenv()!=null), read only under MACAPK_REGISTERNATIVES_VERBATIM=0 | Under MACAPK_REGISTERNATIVES_VERBATIM=0 only: periodically (frames 1-3, then every 600) dumps the Unity DirectorManager's registered-graph bucket heads and pending-change-list pointers, to show whether any PlayableGraph is registered. | diag |

| MACAPK_DUMP_DIRECTOR | RETIRED 2026-09-27: read by nothing; setting it does nothing | (none) | Was: with MACAPK_DIRECTOR_VADDR set, a read-only dump of the first 256 bytes at that guest vaddr from the render callback (a cocos2d-x CCDirector, for the v1.0.59 HCR 1.43 LLVM-lifter bug fixed at v1.0.63-65). The code and its `macapk_get_guest_base_diag()` helper were DELETED, not gated: it was default-OFF, so there was no default path to restore. Use the generic `MACAPK_POLL_GUEST=<vaddr>,<vaddr>,...` word watcher instead. Gate: debug/geh_cocos-ccdirector-memdump-diag_gate.sh; docs/cocos-ccdirector-memdump-retired_2026-09-27.md. | diag |

IGNORED` line unless that gate is 0.** Installs only the 6 key MCPE chain sites, for lower census latency than the full trace. | diag |

IGNORED` line unless that gate is 0.** Runs the NotifyB state probe, libminecraftpe-gated. | diag |
| MACAPK_TRACE_REMAP | tracing off | 0/1 (any set value enables; getenv()!=null) | Logs any .apk-path string, and separately every path, passed through the guest path-remap/relabel logic. | diag |
| MACAPK_TRACE_SURFACE_SIZE | tracing off | 0/1 (any set value enables; getenv()!=null) | Traces the window/surface size measured at each boot, used to derive the AOSP-faithful surface-size formula. | diag |
| MACAPK_TRACE_TOKPOST | tracing off | 0/1 (any set value enables; getenv()!=null) | **RETIRED by default (2026-09-27): read through `MACAPK_MCPE_SIGNIN_PROBES_RETIRED`, ignored with one `[mcpe-probes] ... IGNORED` line unless that gate is 0.** Logs when a submitted WorkerPool task's RUN function matches the Token's terminal-transition RUN (the Token-RUN post detector), and records submit call-site return addresses in a ring buffer, libminecraftpe-gated. | diag |
| MACAPK_TRACE_VIEWPORT | tracing off | 0/1 (any value except "0"/empty enables) | Logs glViewport/glScissor calls (and their bound FBO) only on change, for a native-EGL (Stage-6) guest whose calls bypass the ARM32 shim's own viewport trace. | diag |
| MACAPK_X18_DIAG | tracing off | 0/1 (any set value enables; getenv()!=null) | On a fault whose address has the x18==0 small-offset signature, logs the FFT buffer base register, its arena/reserve membership, and page residency before recovery clobbers registers. | diag |
| MACAPK_X18_PLAN_DUMP | disabled | Value: output directory path | Writes base-independent evidence (the exact guest code and state) for an x18-handling differential comparison. | diag |
| MACAPK_XAC_TRACE | tracing off | 0/1 (any value except "0"/empty enables) | **Inert by default (2026-09-27): a sub-knob consulted only after a member of the retired MCPE sign-in probe family armed, so it has no effect unless `MACAPK_MCPE_SIGNIN_PROBES_RETIRED=0`.** Logs each XAsyncComplete-captured GS completion queue, its impl, and thread id. | diag |
| MACAPK_XSUBMIT_TRACE | tracing off | 0/1 (any set value enables; getenv()!=null) | **RETIRED by default (2026-09-27): read through `MACAPK_MCPE_SIGNIN_PROBES_RETIRED`, ignored with one `[mcpe-probes] ... IGNORED` line unless that gate is 0.** Installs the XAL submit-choke tracepoint in log-only mode (as opposed to MACAPK_XSUBMIT_PUMP's active-fix mode), libminecraftpe-gated. | diag |
## lanes19 java-ui-flows (2026-09-29)

| Flag | What it does | Off / other value |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `MACAPK_BARE_SURFACE_ROOT_ATTACH` | Default ON (2026-09-29). Window-attach a content view that is itself a SurfaceView/GLSurfaceView (no ViewCanvasDriver owns it) before first focus, as ViewRootImpl's first traversal does; a bare root installed AFTER first focus is attached at install, and a replaced bare root is detached (AOSP removeAllViews -> dispatchDetachedFromWindow). Also reaches libGDX's bare GLSurfaceView (mindustry, unciv). | =0: such a root is never attached or detached, its per-surface layer stays hidden (Frozen Bubble black). docs/java-ui-flows_2026-09-29.md |
| `MACAPK_RL_NORULE_TOPLEFT` | Default ON. RelativeLayout lays a rule-less, non-filling child out at padding+margin at its measured size (AOSP positionChild*). | =0: the equal-vertical-stack fallback (Simple Solitaire cards as strips). |
| `MACAPK_IMAGEVIEW_ANIM_HOOKS` | Default ON. ImageView.onAnimationStart/End delegate to View (set/clear mAnimationStarted), as if absent like AOSP. | =0: the old no-op overrides; the app's onAnimationEnd never runs. |
| `MACAPK_OVERLAY_NOTFOCUSABLE_NONMODAL` | Default ON. A FLAG_NOT_FOCUSABLE overlay is not touch-modal (LayoutParams.isModal); ACTION_OUTSIDE only with FLAG_WATCH_OUTSIDE_TOUCH, on the DOWN, for every non-modal window (NOT_TOUCH_MODAL too); the framework Spinner's dropdown popup is setModal(true) like AOSP DropdownPopup. AutoCompleteTextView's popup stays non-modal. By itself this had no owner-visible fix (Wikipedia's stuck state is MACAPK_OVERLAY_GESTURE_PIN); it is AOSP-correct. | =0: every overlay without FLAG_NOT_TOUCH_MODAL swallows outside touches; the Spinner popup is not focusable again. |
| `MACAPK_OVERLAY_GESTURE_PIN` | Default ON. A touch gesture belongs to the window that took its DOWN (WindowOverlayStack.deliverTouch / TouchDispatcher): later MOVE/UP go to it even outside its bounds, a window removed mid-gesture swallows the rest instead of handing the UP to the layer or Activity behind it, and an overlay added mid-gesture does not steal the Activity's UP. AOSP InputDispatcher TouchState. | =0: every event is re-hit-tested against the current layers (a tap on Wikipedia's tooltip balloon also clicked the article image behind it). |
| `MACAPK_OVERLAY_RAW_SCREEN` | Default ON. A floating window's (Dialog / PopupWindow) MotionEvent carries SCREEN coordinates as its raw values and minus the window frame as its offset (WindowOverlayStack.dispatchTo), as AOSP InputDispatcher delivers it: getX() stays window-local, getRawX()/getRawY() now agree with getLocationOnScreen(). Fixes Wikipedia's tooltip "Got it" (skydoves Balloon's touch-margin interceptor swallowed every UP). docs/overlay-motionevent-raw-coords_2026-09-30.md. | =0: raw == window-local (the pre-2026-09-30 event), exact old path. getX/getY are identical at the window root under both arms; a child's getX can differ by at most 1 ulp for points outside the window. |
| `MACAPK_SKELETON_HOST` | Default ON. getBestPattern / DateFormat.getBestDateTimePattern resolve skeletons through the host CLDR (java.time getLocalizedDateTimePattern) with ICU width adjustment (hour / minute / second keep the pattern's own width, as ICU under MATCH_NO_OPTIONS: en_US "hhmm" -> "h:mm a"); en / en-US patterns carry an ASCII space, never U+202F. | =0: the hand table / the skeleton itself ("hma" printed "126PM"). |
| `MACAPK_LIST_ITEMCLICK_AOSP` | Default ON. AbsListView turns taps into performItemClick from its own onTouchEvent (CheckForTap / CheckForLongPress / PerformClick) and never touches row listeners; setOnItemLongClickListener makes the list long-clickable. | =0: per-row click/long-click listeners that overwrote the adapter's own (deskclock could not add a city). |
| `MACAPK_LIBLOG_PRIVATE_FD` | Default ON. The first guest dup2/dup3 onto fd 2 saves the real stderr; liblog shims write there with one write(2). | =0: liblog writes to fd 2, i.e. into a guest's stdio-forwarding pipe (VLC froze on Next). |
| `MACAPK_LOG_CAP_MB` | Default 64 (2026-10-01). Per-run log size cap in MiB on a run log that is a REGULAR file on fd 1/2 (the app stub's `app_<id>.log`, a harness's `>log`): the shim's `macapk-logcap` thread polls it every 500 ms (50 ms while it grows fast) and at the cap ROTATES it: fclonefileat snapshot to `<stem>.prev.log` (one older segment kept), catch-up of what was appended meanwhile, ftruncate to 0, one `[macapk] logcap:` marker line; no descriptor is touched (O_APPEND is set on the description at start), so every writer, dup and child stays covered; a run is ~2x the cap on disk. Each tick re-checks that its descriptors still name the recorded inode (a reused descriptor number is dropped, never truncated). Byte-offset readers of a run log treat a log shorter than their offset as rotated. The launcher's Run-path mirror (`macapk_launcher.log`, fed by a pipe) applies the same value itself. Gate: `debug/gates.sh logcap`. | =0: unlimited, the old path exactly (no file tracked; the run log grows until the disk is full -- Plague Inc spun at 22.9 MB/s). A value that does not start with a digit (`-1`, ` 8`, `+8`) or is not a plain number keeps 64, the same as the launcher's parse. |
| `MACAPK_LOG_COLLAPSE` | Default ON (2026-10-01). liblog "chatty" collapse, modelled on the format of AOSP logd of Android <= 11 (android12-release the source defaults `logd.buffer_type` to "serialized", which never squashes, and main keeps only serialized/simple, so this is a disk/CPU safety net and NOT current-AOSP parity): consecutive identical lines from one thread (same tag + message, within a minute) print once, then one `[chatty] <thread> identical N lines (tag T)` line when the message changes, at most once a second while the run lasts, and before an assert aborts. A line that never repeats is byte-identical. Gate: `debug/gates.sh logcap`. | =0: every repeat is written (Plague Inc: ~595k identical lines/s, 45-54% of its spinning thread). |
| `MACAPK_TRACE_LEGACY_ANIM` | Default OFF diagnostic. =1 logs each legacy tween animation's start and finish (tag Mac.apk.LegacyAnim). | Unset: silent. |
| `MACAPK_PM_PLATFORM_PACKAGE` | Default ON. PackageManager answers getPackageInfo/getApplicationInfo("android") as framework-res (uid 1000, one platform certificate = Mac.apk's own CN=Mac.apk Platform). Gate `platformpkg`. | =0: NameNotFoundException("android") as before (NewPipe's PlayerService died on it). |
| `MACAPK_XMLPULL_KXML_EVENTS` | Default ON. Parsers from XmlPullParserFactory / Xml.newPullParser follow KXmlParser's event contract (first next() = first START_TAG; next() skips comments; whitespace is TEXT). Resource XmlResourceParsers keep XmlBlock's. Gate `xmlpull`. | =0: factory parsers report START_DOCUMENT from the first next(), as before (ExoPlayer DASH manifests failed). |
| `MACAPK_PROGRESS_STYLED_DRAWABLES` | Default ON. ProgressBar/SeekBar/RatingBar constructors read progressDrawable / indeterminateDrawable / thumb / thumbOffset from the style (default progressBarStyle / seekBarStyle / ratingBarStyle), and progress levels follow AOSP doRefreshProgress/setVisualProgress (one id per call: setProgress/setMax/setMin refresh the progress layer, setSecondaryProgress the secondary one, setProgressDrawable both; also for an APP-installed progress drawable). A style-supplied indeterminate drawable is returned but the synthesized arc still draws. Gate `progressdrawable`. | =0: no styled read and NO level is ever set (an app-installed drawable included); the getters answer null as before. |
| `MACAPK_LAYER_ITEM_IDS` | Default ON. Every layer-list `<item android:id>` is recorded (AOSP updateLayerFromTypedArray), so findDrawableByLayerId works. Gate `progressdrawable` D4. | =0: only the ripple mask id is recorded, as before. |
| `MACAPK_SV_UI_THREAD_CALLBACKS` | Default ON. A SurfaceHolder.Callback dispatch that starts off the UI thread (the Cocoa render thread) while the real main loop runs is POSTED to the UI thread, as AOSP delivers it from performTraversals (Organic Maps' drape engine CHECKed the thread); a native arrival that lands while the posted surfaceCreated is in flight is recorded and delivered as its own surfaceChanged right after it (created -> changed -> changed, as inline). | =0: every callback inline on the calling thread, as before. |
| `MACAPK_CODEC_PROFILE_LEVELS` | Default ON. Video decoders carry a real MediaCodecInfo profile/level table: AVC/HEVC measured on the host by an encode-then-decode round trip per profile (runtime/src/video_caps_probe.mm; ~0.7 s once per process at the first MediaCodecList build), libvpx VP9 Profile0/Level5 + VP8 Main per AOSP C2SoftVpxDec / Codec2InfoBuilder at media_codecs_google_c2_video.xml's limits (size 2..2048, <= 16384 blocks); VT size ranges from the largest decoded frame, area bounded in macroblocks. Gate `vtcaps`. | =0: every video decoder with an EMPTY table and the declared 2..4096 range, as before (libvpx 2..4096, unbounded; ExoPlayer: every AVC/HEVC/VP9 track NoSupport, NewPipe live at 144p). |
| `MACAPK_VTCAPS_TRACE` | Off. Diagnostic: prints every encode/decode trial of the video profile/level probe (`[vtcaps] avc High L5.1 4096x2304 enc=0 sps_idc=100 ... -> DECODED`). Also honoured by the standalone `debug/vtcaps/vtcaps_main.mm`. | unset: one summary line per mime (`[video-toolbox] capabilities video/avc: ...`). |
| `MACAPK_HTTP_STREAMED_REDIRECTS` | Default ON. http/https URLs resolve through com.macapk.net.AndroidHttpHandlerProvider (a java.net.spi.URLStreamHandlerProvider wrapping the JDK's own connection): a 300-303 answer to a request whose body was STREAMED (fixed-length/chunked) is followed as a GET, per Android's OkHttp HttpURLConnectionImpl; every other request is the JDK connection untouched. Needs the --add-exports in jvm_loader.cpp. Gate `hucredirect`. | =0: the provider returns null, stock JDK handlers: a streamed POST that is redirected reports a bare 302 (NewPipe: every YouTube VOD failed). |
